### 1.5.8 (release)
- 1.5.71-1.5.77 released as 1.5.8, plus (log f632af6e): used-up torches removed (UpdateTorch 1 s scan: Torch, m_destroyBroken, durability <= 0; DurabilityPatches removes m_destroyBroken items at 0) - Humanoid.BlockAttack drains the blocker's durability by the blocked damage for everyone and never removes it; no blocking with only a torch (TorchIsBlocker = left ?? right like GetCurrentBlocker -> TryEvade(always) or no block); no torch while a usable weapon waits to go on (right hand empty); MHR_Follow.Tick indoors, in a dungeon only (Character.InInterior - at home it keeps waiting, user): out of sight (Los) 2 s -> "player out of sight" stand-up (the 10 m FollowSitStayRange was straight-line through walls). README highlights reworded by the user. Then (log b4e6012b): DoorHandler.DirectUseDoor(door, close) - toggles (IsOpen/IsUsableDoor), nearest companion only, "It's locked." for a key/ward door; CompanionInteract.HandleGamepadCombo/Hold return unless Player.TakeInput (reflection; our panels are in it via TakeInput_BlockForCompanionUI). Default LogLevel stays Normal (user considered Off, not worth it).

### 1.5.77
- LogLevel moved to Advanced (user: no own section; MigrateAndClean drops the old Logging key). F8 had no row for string settings with an AcceptableValueList (LogLevel, PortalItems, RespawnStance never showed): `BuildChoiceRow` (button = value, click/A = next, left/right via AdjustSelectedValue, Default). The F1 window's dropdown position is that tool's own.
- Log guards pass 2: instance log helpers (controller Log/LogDebug, combat TLog/TargetLog/UnsafeLog) and partial-class siblings (CookFuel, UpkeepHoney/Piles) now guarded at the call too. [Radial]/[Return]/[UiLayers]/[GridFix]/[ContainerFix] -> Detailed.
- Food "already active" log: a HashSet per food, cleared when a food is eaten or expires (it reset whenever another food passed the check: 113 lines each with honey + yellow mushroom, log b15f00be). Repair scan: per-item list only when the name/percent signature changes, the "none below" line once a minute otherwise.
- Log b15f00be also showed the crosshair hold working ("Gunter stays put - your crosshair is on it", then Use opened its inventory).

### 1.5.76
- Logging level: `[Logging] Level` (1.5.77: `[Advanced] LogLevel`) Off/Normal/Detailed -> `CompanionsPlugin.LogNormal` / `LogDetailed` (bools, refreshed on SettingChanged; Normal before the config is read). Every LogInfo/LogDebug call and every per-file log helper call is prefixed `if (CompanionsPlugin.LogNormal|LogDetailed)` (scripted; `if (CompanionsPlugin.Log != null)` guards became the level guard - the flags include Log != null), so a level that's off doesn't build the string. Debug -> Detailed; Info -> Normal except the Diagnostics files, RiseWatch, [CompanionPrefabs]/[Setup]/[Interact]/[UI]/[EQUIPDIAG]/[AIGate]/[BowDiag] lines and WeaponLogic.NonCompanionLog/ContainerFix.Log -> Detailed. RiseWatch.Tick, AIGateDiag.Report, MHR_Diag.Tick (the stuck-follow [Diag] warning), EquipDiag hooks and the bow diag return at once unless Detailed; PlayerCombatLog.On needs Detailed. Warnings/errors always logged. New log lines: guard them the same way. Reason: a player asked to turn the debug logging off (stutter with big load orders). Eaf2 log: 2187 debug vs 230 info lines from the mod.

### 1.5.75
- Follow hold: `MHR_Follow.YouAimAt` - camera forward within atan(0.6/d)+3 deg of the companion's centre, player within 10 m, linecast clear (LosMask). Extends the 1.5 s set-off hold to 3.5 s while aimed, only while `!s.Moving` (never stops it once it walks). User picked the crosshair over "camera facing it"; the game's hover only works in interact range.

### 1.5.74
- Lava: `MHR_Mover.Lava(p)` = `WorldGenerator.IsAshlands` + `ZoneSystem.IsLava`, safe when a ray down hits a non-Heightmap collider (the game's own rule in `Character.UpdateLava`). `AvoidLava` after `AvoidFire` on every planned route: `LavaRing` (Dijkstra over 16x3 ring spots, legs checked by `LegClearOfLava`), else a job gives up (`GaveUp`) and Follow keeps going step by step. Per-step: lava 0.9/1.6 m ahead -> turn by `LavaExit` or halt. `EscapeLava` (walk out, jump after 1 s) runs in `Go` and early in `CompanionAI.UpdateAI` (after WaterSafety, gate "Lava"). Lava rejected in `Clear`, landings, `SafeSpot`, water Hop, the CompanionAI placement spots, combat reach (`it's standing in lava`) and `MHR_Loot`. Untested in game (user can't get to the Ashlands yet).
- F8: `NoUnitySelection()` every controller update sets every Selectable in the panel to `Navigation.Mode.None` and clears the EventSystem selection inside it - the second gold highlight was Unity's selection. Pad hint text black.

> **2026-10-04: the project moved from IL to C#.** The source is now `src/Companions/*.cs`, built with
> `dotnet build` (see the repo README). It was decompiled from the shipped 1.4.74 `Companions.dll`, and the
> four `MHR_*.cs` helpers are the original hand-written sources. The rebuilt DLL was checked against 1.4.74
> member by member: same signatures, calls, field accesses, strings, constants and embedded textures. The
> only differences are dead code the IL edits left behind (unreachable, so it never ran) and one fix:
> `CombatPatches.EquipBestWeapon_PreferHighestDamage` had its `[HarmonyPrefix]` on the class and
> `[HarmonyPostfix]` on the `Prefix` method (an ilasm `.custom` placement slip), so Harmony used `Prefix` as
> the postfix and never ran `Postfix`. The attributes are now on the right methods.
>
> Everything below that talks about `Companions_source.il`, ilasm/monodis, `.maxstack`, branch checks,
> the BepInPlugin byte blob or "transplanting" helpers describes the old IL workflow and no longer applies.
> The version now lives in one place in code: `CompanionsPlugin.PluginVersion` (plus `package/CHANGELOG.md`).

# Offline Companions (ProfMags) — Valheim 1.0 compatibility patch — progress notes

## IMPORTANT: verification pipeline now includes check_maxstack.py
1.4.44 shipped two new helpers with `.maxstack` too small (MHR_RadialDiag 6>5, MHR_SetBedCollisionIgnored 4>3). Unity's Mono throws `InvalidProgramException` at JIT; neither ilasm NOR the verify_all.exe JIT-prep sweep (desktop Mono 6.8) catches it - the sweep's "maxstack coverage" assumption from 1.4.31 is WRONG. New `check_maxstack.py` simulates stack depth per method (call/newobj signatures parsed, branch/try/catch aware) and flags peak > declared. First run also found two LATENT bugs from 1.4.29: `UpdateCrafting` (Cook/Homestead) and FarmController `UpdateTakingSeeds` call the 8-arg `Inventory.AddItem` -> peak 9 vs .maxstack 8 -> would crash the first time a companion crafts or takes seeds. **Always run: ilasm -> check_branches.py -> check_maxstack.py -> verify_all.exe -> BepInPlugin version check.** When writing new methods, count the stack for string.Format/object[] logging blocks - that's where these came from.

Current version: **1.4.52** — build tag `v70-despawn-popup`. Verified: branches 0/1648, maxstack 0/1648, refs 0, JIT 1595/1595, BepInPlugin 1.4.52. Untested in-game.
- User chose a Yes/No popup for despawn. `DoDespawn` now stores the target in new field `_mhrDespawnTarget`, hides the wheel, and pushes the game's `YesNoPopup(header, text, yes, no, localizeText:false, coverBackground:false)` via `UnifiedPopup.Push` (falls back to despawning directly if `UnifiedPopup.IsAvailable()` is false). Yes -> `MHR_DespawnConfirmed()` (Pop, then the old removal body: PrepareForDespawn, MHR_ReleaseBedForDespawn, DespawnWithTombstone, clear m_onDeath, ZNetView.Destroy). No -> `MHR_DespawnCancelled()` (Pop, clear target). Popups don't close themselves - both callbacks must call `UnifiedPopup.Pop()`. Localization: removed "hc_radial_despawn_confirm" default, added "hc_despawn_popup_title" ("Despawn Companion") and "hc_despawn_popup_text" ("Permanently remove {0}? ..."). `_despawnConfirmPending`/`UpdateDespawnLabel` are now dead code (harmless).

Previous version: **1.4.51** — build tag `v69-despawn-and-equip-flag-fix`. Verified: branches 0/1646, maxstack 0/1646, **refs 0 unresolved (new check_refs.py)**, JIT 1593/1593, BepInPlugin 1.4.51. Untested in-game.
- User log 77fee611 (1.4.50): Despawn threw `MissingMethodException CompanionSetup.CreateCompanionTombstone(...18 args)` - `DespawnWithTombstone` was never updated when 1.4.32 added hasHomeBed/homeBedPos (20 args). **ilasm happily emits a MemberRef to a method that doesn't exist**, and none of our checks caught it (JIT sweep only fails when the call is actually JIT-resolved... it wasn't). New `check_refs.py` compares every `call/callvirt/newobj/ldftn class Companions.X::M(...)` against defined methods (name + arg count, generic defs handled). **Add it to the pipeline: ilasm -> check_branches -> check_maxstack -> check_refs -> verify_all (run from inside verify_run) -> BepInPlugin check.** Fix: pass `false, Vector3.zero`; new `CompanionSetup._mhrDespawning` makes MHR_GetDeathItemRule return 3 (drop all) so nothing is "kept" for a companion that will never respawn; DoDespawn calls new `CompanionRest.MHR_ReleaseBedForDespawn()` (ReleaseClaimedBed -> clears bed ZDO label, HasHomeBedHash=false).
- Despawn confirmation (existing): first press sets `_despawnConfirmPending` + 3s timer and relabels the wedge "Confirm?" (hc_radial_despawn_confirm); a second press within 3s despawns.
- Equipped flag: dragging a companion's equipped item to the player goes through InventoryGui -> `Inventory.MoveItemToThis(from, item, amount, x, y)`, which clones ItemData with m_equipped=true (the shift-click path in CompanionInteractPanel.OnCompanionSelected already unequipped first). New `MHR_EquipMove.UnequipIfFromCompanion` + Harmony prefixes `MHR_MoveItemToThis5_Patch` / `MHR_MoveItemToThis2_Patch` (TargetMethod via AccessTools.Method with explicit arg types; try/catch wrapped): if the source inventory is a companion's (and target != source), `Humanoid.UnequipItem(item,false)` then `m_equipped=false` before the clone. check_maxstack.py only recognizes `catch` on its own line after `}`.

## NEXT BUILD
### Standing rules (user, 2026-10-04)
- (user, 2026-10-13) When the user suggests a fix, check it against the game's actual code and mechanics (decompiled source) before building something else - and don't build around a game mechanic without reading how the game itself does it for the player. Lesson: the ladder bump - the user suggested "just set Gunter's walking direction" early on; I built my own bump instead, which the game's walking code capped and which blocked the real one. The user's suggestion was the fix.
- (user, 2026-10-13) Check the WHOLE LogOutput.log every time, not just the movement lines - the user runs few mods, so it's short. Include what the player is doing (positions as follow goals, commands, menus, Follow toggles) for ideas on how the NPC should behave.
- (user, 2026-10-13) Stamina: running out is fine, but the NPC tracks it like a player everywhere and tries not to - no sprinting or hopping where a player would walk (ladders), rest on a landing rather than run dry.
- **Only this mod in the repo** (user, 2026-10-08): notes, logs, changelog, commit messages and PRs cover Offline Companions Reborn and its companions only. Anything else the user brings up (other mods, other tools, their setup) is a tangent: help in the chat, never write it to the repo.
- **Version numbers (user, 2026-10-13):** published builds take the full numbers (1.5.4, 1.5.5...); test builds in between are 1.5.41, 1.5.42... (after 1.5.4 is published, the speech work starts as 1.5.41).
- **The user builds the DLLs** (`dotnet build -c Release` on their PC). Don't build and hand over a DLL unless they ask. Compiling in a session just to check for errors is fine; say so, and don't send the output.
- Releases will go up as GitHub Releases once the repo is public ("very soon"). Until then the README's Source section says the code will be on GitHub soon.

### 1.5.73 - torch fixes, doors in a fight, F8 with a controller (user, log 702b4b9c (1.5.72) + "Yes this sounds good thanks. Lets build and push 1.5.73.")
- Game: Humanoid.EquipItem(torch) with a one-handed weapon in the right hand and the left empty puts the torch in the LEFT hand. UpdateTorch looked at the right hand only -> "lit a torch" every 3 s (EquipItem false: already equipped), never put away, AutoEquipBest pushed it out for the shield. Now either hand; checked every 1 s; AutoEquipBest leaves a wanted left-hand torch (torchLeft).
- In a fight: want stays true for a torch in the left hand (one-hander, no shield) - user: "If they have no shield and its night why are they putting away the torch during a fight?"; a right-hand torch goes for the weapon; no lighting during a fight (a shield/bow/2H the combat code equips pushes the left-hand torch out itself).
- AutoEquipBest: Torch no longer a weapon candidate (user: fists rather than a torch; TorchOpener still uses it) - "Companion" with only a torch looped "put the torch away" in the day.
- Burial chamber: [Torch] "in Crypt, light 0.31" -> put away at once (not the enemies). [Torch] DarkThreshold 0.15 -> DarkLevel 0.4 (new key so old files get 0.4; the old key is dropped as an orphan).
- Doors: the leaf-in-the-way close (1.4.136) also ran in a fight, and a fight won't open doors (1.5.65) -> Gunter shut the chamber door and stayed out. No leaf close under CombatTag; OpenDoor under CombatTag allowed when you're on the far side within 25 m (YouWentThrough: door forward-axis sides).
- MHR_RiseWatch: ignore rises over 50 m (dungeon entry teleport, 5001 m).
- ConfigPanel: ZInput.GetJoyLeftStickY is down-positive - rows (LY) and scroll (RY) inverted; selected row brighter + gold Outline; bottom hint line with glyphs while a controller is in use (content area 24 px shorter).

### 1.5.72 - controller binds (user, 2026-10-28: "These need to be able to be unmapped if a player wants ... I also dont think the settings show the controller binds ... we need to be able use modifiers for the controller" / "I dont want a player to be in combat hit block as a modifier and it not work. Or hit block decide they need to jump and instead issue a command")
- Game (ZInput, assembly_utils via ilspycmd): three controller layouts. Default: Use A, Jump B, Sit X, Inventory Y, Run LB, Crouch L3, Hide R3, Block LT, Attack RT, Secondary RB, AltKeys = LT. Alternative 1: Jump A, Use X, Block LB, Hide LT, AltKeys = LB. Alternative 2: Jump A, Use X, Hide LB, Block LT, AltKeys = LT. AltKeys (the block button) is the game's own modifier: Alt+B dodge, Alt+D-pad camera/minimap zoom, Alt+L3 autopickup, Alt+R3 alt place, Alt+Start HUD, Alt+Select chat; hotbar off while held. Free: Alt+X, Alt+Y. Block + jump = dodge (Player.SetControls). Stick clicks toggle crouch/run on press - no good as a held modifier.
- Core/MHR_Pad.cs: binds (None, Use, Alt, A..Start) -> ZInput names; Down/Held read raw (s_own); a no-modifier bind doesn't fire while another combo on the same button has its modifier held. HidePatch (prefix on ZInput.TryGetButtonState): per frame, the action paths of combo buttons whose modifier is held; any game query on a name with that path returns false. The modifier is never hidden.
- ModConfig [Controller]: Command Y + Alt, Wheel X + Alt, Settings/FarmZone/Passive/Balanced/Ranged/Melee None (+ Modifier each). CompanionInteract: command from the bind (hover exclusion only on plain Use, the old way); HandleGamepadCombo - wheel bind opens the wheel, Use on a companion opens its bag at once (old hold-Use path kept when Wheel = plain Use). Stance keys, F8 panel, farm zone (open and close) take their binds.
- ConfigPanel: Controller rows (PadBindListener - A or click, waits for release, next button; block on a modifier row -> "Alt"; Esc/6 s cancels), Clear button on controller and keyboard rows (X on a controller), Y = default as before. Hover hint: "[Use] Inventory / [Alt + X] Commands" with the game's glyphs (GetBoundKeyString + Localize); home zone and farm zone hints with glyphs (farm zone: LT/RT resize, D-pad up/down rotate, Start exit - the old text said bumpers / D-pad).

### 1.5.71 - torches (user, 2026-10-28: "lets go over how the npc uses a torch at night. We also need to add a check so the NPC will not try to repair the torch. Can you also check if a torch on a player disappears when it hits 0% durability?" / "Not all dungeons are dark ... Crypts and troll caves tend to be dark, while sunken crypts and the mistland dungeons are not as dark.")
- Game (Humanoid.UpdateEquipment): held items drain only for players (returns for non-players); DrainEquipedItemDurability at 0: "$msg_broke", unequip, RemoveItem if m_destroyBroken (default true). So a player's torch vanishes at 0; a companion's never burned down (only our per-swing drain).
- Old UpdateTorch: every 3 s, night and not fighting -> first torch in the bag; remembered in _equippedTorch, so a job's tool swap left it torchless for the rest of the night; no durability check; time of day only.
- New: drain the held torch each frame like the player's ([Torch] BurnsDown), burn out -> unequip + remove, say "My last torch burned out" when none is left; every 3 s WantsTorch = not fighting && dark (outdoors EnvMan.IsNight; Character.InInterior -> RenderSettings.ambientLight.grayscale < [Torch] DarkThreshold 0.15 while you're in it within 100 m, else dark; logged per environment/state) && !LitBaseNear (PlayerBase area + burning Fireplace within 8 m) && !YouHoldTorchNear (5 m); not while a job holds the hands (SuppressAutoEquip), TorchOpener, attacking or swimming; best torch by durability. AutoEquipBest leaves a wanted torch in hand. Repair skips torches (worn list + CanRepairAt).
- To tune: DarkThreshold from the [Torch] "in <env>, light X" lines in a crypt, troll cave, sunken crypt and Mistlands dungeon.

### 1.5.7 - release (user, log 7b050fc3: "As long as there are no issues in the log I think we should build release 1.5.7"): README Recent Highlights v1.5.7 (summon window, Stone/Ore and buildings, Wood, encumbrance, portals, safer round the house) and a Thanks section (Necrustic), wording approved by the user; version 1.5.7.

### 1.5.70 - repair at a base, repair wait by place, rise diagnostic (user, log 31cb468a: "Can you check if the NPC should have fixed up their gear when we were home?" / companion on the roof: "There is no way onto the roof"; log 188ac60a: "Lets add 1. We might as well build the set aside distance thing and the repair when near a workbech.")
- RepairThreshold (MHR_ToolKeeper): no home + inside EffectArea PlayerBase -> HomeThreshold (80%). Gunter has no home set, so 50% applied at the forge.
- RepairController: _noStationAt per item; the NoStationWait (10 min) ends once the companion is ScanRadius (20 m) from where it was set. The 47% axe was set aside in the woods and still skipped at home.
- AI/MHR_RiseWatch.cs: samples every 0.25 s (CompanionSetup.Update); one sample to the next > 1 m up and steeper than 1.5:1, no jump (Character.m_jumpTimer < 1.2 s) or ladder -> [Diag] warning with both spots, status before/after, solid pieces within 1.2 m (triggers ignored). Not while attached. First version (rise > 2 m in 2 s) flagged the outside stairs 3 times and listed the base-zone triggers of beds/fires/portals (log c6fee971).
- MHR_Chests.Open/Close: not before Player.m_localPlayer exists or with an invalid ZNetView - Container.SetInUse looks the local player up (NRE sorting a chest a second after loading, log c6fee971).
- Log bced720b (1.5.70 first build): sorting resin across the hearth in a tight room - FireBypass found no clear two-corner way, it fell back to step-by-step and ended on the stone (2 fire hits). User: "I thought there was a check to do the path step by step? ... This was working great before." Review of the step code: nothing was removed - the per-step check (FireArea 0.8 m ahead at 0.3/0.9/1.5 m, 1.5.46/47) still runs. Holes found: (1) the step-hop after Steer (StepAhead, 1.4.86) had no fire check - the hearth stone is a step it hops up onto; same for the unstuck Hop; (2) FireExit's way round only checked its end point; (3) all checks used the Burning EffectArea, not the fire's damage Aoe (game: Aoe m_useTriggers/m_useCollider/m_radius - separate). Sorting trips between chests either side of the hearth are new since the hearth tests, which is why it shows now.
- Fixes: FireZoneAt (lit Fireplaces' damaging Aoe zones, cached 2 s) in FireArea and InFire; no step-hop or unstuck hop toward a fire; FireExit (per-step use) checks its whole leg; FireRing (12 spots x 2 rings round FireReach, Dijkstra over clear in-sight legs) when FireBypass fails; still none -> a job's route gives up (GaveUp - Upkeep skips that chest 300 s), Follow keeps steering step by step.
- Player report: a companion used a hut's world-generated chest to drop its wood. MHR_Chests.Usable (every job: drop-off, restock, upkeep, smelt, cook, farm, ToolKeeper, portal leave-behind) and MHR_GatherRules.DropOff now need Piece.IsPlacedByPlayer() (creator id set; world chests have none). Command-wheel chest orders are untouched.
- Log 188ac60a: Gunter stood on the hearth stone in its flames 7 times (Upkeep sorting between chests either side of the hearth). FireBypass checked only its two corner points; now r = the fire piece's colliders + 0.8 m, pushed out 0.5 m at a time up to +3.3 m until from->a->b->next corner are all clear (LegClearOfFire, 0.4 m steps); AvoidFire skips while standing in a fire, and after EscapeFire the route is planned again (S.FireReplan). MHR_Mover - every movement tag.

### 1.5.69 - water banks, logs in trees, catch-up loop (user, log fc93f903 (1.5.68): "Gunter got stuck in the water. Character.Jump (game): a swimmer may jump when InLiquidSwimDepth and m_hitWorldTime < 0.25 - how a player gets out onto a bank.

- "You moved on" loop (57x): own pieces (1.5.67) bypass InWorkArea, so the rescan took the same log back; each cycle swapped axe/sword. Own pieces only while not TooFarFromYou(); _scanTimer = 3 s after "You moved on".
- Not ours: dungeon generation twice (41 and 26 rooms) - a location zone coming into range; both companions killed by drakes (Companion with no armour, 4 deaths).

### 1.5.68 - rolling and hung-up logs (user, log 9aef82b6 (1.5.67): "went chasing a rolling log that was not settling. Other than that one off situation it looks much much better.")
- Log 9aef82b6: own pieces finished first, hits from one spot, settle waits fine. A piece hung 13 m up in the branches was taken (own piece), swung at from 10.6 m below (the attack range check was horizontal for trees/logs), knocked loose and chased while it slid; also one rolling downhill (1627-1673). Food "no food found" 816 lines.
- LogSettling: wait up to 8 s (was 3, then chase), then SetAside (blacklist entry dated so it expires after 15 s, ResetToIdle). Wait line once per target.
- LogOutOfReach: LogReachPoint sets _logPointFound; no usable ground spot -> SetAside 15 s. Called with LogSettling at the top of UpdateMoving/UpdateAttacking.
- UpdateAttacking: TreeLog also needs 3D distance <= reach + 0.5 (else reapproach).
- CompanionFood: no-food line once per slot until the item count changes or it eats.

### 1.5.67 - hit register for gathering (user, log 9e9a7e57 (1.5.66): "Lost track of the other log that was created. Had a hard time finding where to stand for a stump. Same with a log. He would get a few hits then move if a tree had not fully fallen. Still kind of squirrely with figuring out where to stand and hit the log. Maybe we do need a hit register on the logs.")
- Patches/HarvestHitRegister.cs: postfixes on TreeLog/TreeBase/Destructible/MineRock/MineRock5.Damage(HitData); hit.GetAttacker() a companion -> HarvestController.OnHitRegistered(go) (matches the target, its parent or child).
- SwingsNotLanding now: at each swing, did a hit register since the last one? 2 misses in a row -> another side (logs) / blacklist. Replaces the 3-swing health check (TargetHealth kept, unused).
- _goodSpot: where it stood when a hit landed; HoldingGoodSpot (within 0.8 m, no miss since) skips the every-2-swings reapproach and the LateUpdate low-target walk-in.
- LogSettling: TreeLog Rigidbody velocity/angular > 0.3 -> stop, no spot, wait up to 3 s (called at the top of UpdateMoving and UpdateAttacking).
- Pieces by identity: NoteLogsAround at Target set records TreeLog ids within 15 m (minus known pieces); after a TreeLog/TreeBase breaks, a new TreeLog within 30 m of the break for 60 s is an own piece (_ownPieces, kept across a chain of breaks), preferred and exempt from InWorkArea.

### 1.5.66 - one spot per log, no step-aside for logs (user, log 2285db5e (1.5.65): "Keeps moving when hitting a log. Moves to an end then side or somewhere else along the log. Runs off a few steps after chopping a log then goes back to get the dropped items" - "Moved around the rock this time")
- Log 2285db5e: split halves finished first, swings land (3-7 hits per piece), round the rock. LogReachPoint refreshed every 2 s as "nearest to the companion" - it moved as he did; MHR_GatherRules.OnTargetDestroyed stepped 4 m uphill for 2.5 s on TreeLog too.
- LogReachPoint kept per target while the log stays within 0.5 m of where it was (a failed spot still forces a re-pick, _logPointAt = -10). OnTargetDestroyed: TreeBase only.

### 1.5.65 - finish the split log, clear line to the log, swings that don't land (user, log e5d77d02 (1.5.64): "the NPC swapped to a new log after chopping the one in half. This log is partially on a rock so swings are hitting the rock not the log. The NPC should finish chopping the log it was working on ... Also needs a hit / rock check")

- HarvestController: _splitPos/_splitAt on a TreeLog/TreeBase destroy (its last position); ScanForTarget (Wood) prefers TreeLog candidates within 6 m of it for 30 s ("Finishing the log it just broke"). LogReachPoint: the ground spot needs LineToLog (raycast chest -> log point on Default/static_solid/Default_small/piece, ignoring the log and characters) and isn't within 1.5 m of a point that failed. SwingsNotLanding: TargetHealth (ZDO s_health of TreeLog/TreeBase/Destructible; MineRock/MineRock5 not read) at each swing - 3 swings without a drop -> a log: that point is marked bad, another side ("trying another side"); otherwise/again: blacklisted ("leaving it for now").

### 1.5.64 - logs chopped where they can be reached (user, log 862662de (1.5.63): "Gunter got stuck on a log for a moment. Swinging at a log he cannot reach. Need to check for a part touching the ground and swing near there"; likely also the player report "skipping wood logs most of the time")
- Log 862662de: FirTree_log at (-283.5, 36.2, -382.3), companion at y 32.6: "stuck (standing still 3s)", route=partial, STUCK timeout -> blacklisted 60 s, back to it later, stuck again. GetEffectiveTargetPosition had no TreeLog case - the log's transform (its middle). Otherwise fine: honey order, logs -> stumps ("No logs found - taking the nearest stump"), Stone skipped walls, bow "Something is in the way of a shot - moving closer".
- HarvestController.LogReachPoint: ground spots on a 1 m grid over the log's bounds + 1.5 m (raycast down on terrain/Default/static_solid/piece, not the log itself); for each, Collider.ClosestPoint of every (primitive/convex) log collider from 0.8 m above the ground - usable if 1.2 m above the ground at most (0.5 m below at least) and 1 m sideways; nearest to the companion. Cached per target for 2 s; falls back to the transform.

### 1.5.63 - honey order pick, orders before bedtime, obstacle in the swing (user, log 4122afdf (1.5.62): "Gunter was having issues with attacking a greydwarf ... Kept swinging with a tree in the way then stood there and just blocked for a while" / honey: one resting, the other didn't go)

(now 0.9 m away, 40 hp)" - a tree in the line; then "Keeping just out of reach (its reach 2.7 m)" + "no safe way to back off" -> blocked for a while (greydwarf reach: backlog).

### 1.5.62 - encumbrance, portal item rules (user, 2026-10-27: "The NPC cannot become encumbered currently" / "add the check for items that cannot go through the portals ... match whatever the game is set at as a default and be adjustable in the settings" / "have the companion place items they cannot portal in a nearby chest if there is one, then portal to the player" - option 1: "The Player wont see it so in this case its ok to cheat a bit")
- Encumbrance: Character.IsEncumbered is false for non-players (Player overrides it with weight > GetMaxCarryWeight), so the Encumbered icon we added did nothing. Patches/CompanionEncumbrance.cs: Character.IsEncumbered postfix for companions (pack > CarryLimit) - the game then applies crouch speed (Character.UpdateWalking), no run (CheckRun), no jump, no block, the "encumbered" animator bool; Character.CanMove postfix: false when encumbered with 0 CompanionStamina (Player.CanMove). CompanionStamina: 10/s while moving encumbered (Player.m_encumberedStaminaDrain). CompanionTierData.CarryLimit(c) = MaxCarryWeight, SEMan.ModifyMaxCarryWeight (belt), x Game.m_carryWeightRate; CarryBonus raises Gathering OverweightThreshold (Harvest, Farm). All MaxCarryWeight call sites (auto pickup, loot, fish/hunt bag full, upkeep, piles, smelt, weight bar, inventory panel) use CarryLimit. Status Doing "carrying too much - walking slowly", one spoken line and a [Carry] log line on change.
- Portals: game rules checked in code - TeleportWorld.Teleport refuses unless Player.IsTeleportable(m_allowAllItems) (Inventory.IsTeleportable: tool tier >= 1000 never; else allowAll or GlobalKeys.TeleportAll (world Portals: Casual), else every item m_teleportable); NoPortals / NoBossPortals (Very hard / Hard) stop the player, so they never reach our patch. PortalPatches: TeleportWorld.Teleport prefix records m_allowAllItems; TeleportTo postfix within 1 s = via a portal; MayGoThrough per loaded following companion with [General] PortalItems = Game: IsTeleportable -> go; else LeaveInChest: usable chests (MHR_Chests.Usable) within 15 m of it, plan every stuck item first (the chest with the most of it, else a free slot), then move (claim the chest, unequip, AddItem/RemoveItem) at once - its area unloads seconds later; boss items stay with it. Not all fit -> it stays (not queued) and says why. Unloaded companions aren't checked (no inventory to read).

### 1.5.61 - summon window clicks, Stone/Ore skip buildings, Wood order (player reports via the user, 2026-10-27)
- Summon window "not clickable" (player screenshot: "Missing requirement" tooltip over the hair style arrow; "if you load the game near it, it works, if you go far away it won't"): CompanionPanel.CreateButton / CreateCategoryArrowButton copy InventoryGui.m_craftButton, which the game sets interactable = false (and its UITooltip to $msg_missingrequirement) whenever the last selected recipe can't be made (InventoryGui.UpdateRecipe). The copies kept that. MakeUsable: every Selectable interactable, UITooltip removed. The user couldn't reproduce with cheats (Player.NoCostCheat keeps the craft button interactable). Same family as the 1.5.90 grey stamina bar (a game element copied in a changed state).
- Stone/Ore: ScanForTarget skips candidates that are part of a building (Piece or WearNTear in the parents, or "wall" in the name) - the Stone filter took any Destructible a pickaxe can hurt and an axe can't.
- Wood: strict order logs -> stumps -> standing trees (stumps were mixed with logs, nearest first).
- Flying companions (another player, maybe a mod that changes player height): waiting for a log and mod list.

### 1.5.6 release (user, 2026-10-26)
- Hexium rejected the zip: "CHANGELOG.md is too long, max: 100000" (it was 104,980 characters). Split (user: "The change log mostly wont be read by anyone so the entries can be fairly brief for the zip builds"): `package/CHANGELOG.md` keeps 1.5.0 on with brief lines (1.5.6 cut to 12), plus a link to `CHANGELOG-full.md` (repo root, the full history with every detail). From now on: brief lines in the package changelog, detail in CHANGELOG-full.md.
- README highlights for 1.5.6 approved; Future Plans rewritten (next: second combat review with per-enemy strategies, combat on ships, late-game features and weapons, animal husbandry; Haldor recruiting dropped).
- Combat LeashFromYou default 25 -> 35 m (user: "35m is fine"): bow draws start inside 30 m, so 25 m cut off shots at the edge of bow range on your side; 40 m would overlap the fixed 40 m CombatLeash (enemy from the companion itself) and the 50 m follow catch-up. Existing config files keep their saved value.

### 1.5.93 - boarding: jump aboard first (user, log c22fc35d (1.5.92): "kept jumping into the water to get to the ladder when they could jump directly onto the boat deck")
- Log c22fc35d: three boardings, dive -> wade/tread -> ladder each time, never "jumping aboard". The near-ladder branch (ladder within 8 m or at the launch spot: dive / walk straight) returned before the jump-aboard check, and the launch spot is the dry ground nearest the ladder, not a spot in jump reach of the deck.
- MHR_Board: JumpCheck(c, ship, from, cols, ...) (DeckSpot on our side, 0.3-4 m across, <= 1.6 m up / 3 m down, MHR_Jump.Reach + 0.3, clear line); TryJumpAboard runs before the takeoff/launch/dive steps (every 1.5 s, on the ground, not swimming), and logs one near miss per boarding ("can't jump aboard from ...: gap, up, reach, clear").
- Takeoff (once per boarding, on dry ground / shallows): your own steps onto the ship (MHR_Follow.StepsOnto: the last step off the ship before the first on it) if that spot is dry, within 30 m and passes JumpCheck ("going to jump aboard where you did"); else JumpSpot: 1 m grid within 15 m, within 3.5 m of a hull collider (convex/primitive colliders only), dry ground (not the ship), within 4 m height, passing JumpCheck, nearest. Walk there (mover; gave up -> ladder), stand facing the ship for the jump; 3 jumps not landing aboard or 6 s without progress -> the ladder (launch/dive/wade as before).

### 1.5.92 - boarding: launch spot once, wade to the ladder (user, log 571a5159 (1.5.90): "the NPC is stuck in a loop trying to get on the boat. He did finally make it after running around the dock twice. The water did get shallow" / "Sometimes you will not need to be swimming to use the ladder or jump on")
- Log 571a5159 16:43:26: launch spot 7 m from the ladder, dive (8.1 m) -> landed standing in the shallows at (-319.4, 28.1, -354.1), not swimming, > 1.2 m from the launch -> toLaunch: the mover walked back to the launch spot the long way ("26m way round ... deep water", 15-16 corners), dived again (8.0 m), same; third dive (7.9 m) landed within 5.7 m, swimming -> tread, ladder, seat.
- MHR_Board: HasLaunch cleared on the dive. New wade step after the jump-aboard check: ladder within 15 m, not swimming, DepthAt(me) > 0.3 -> walk straight at the ladder ("wading to the ladder"); the ladder check (top of Update) fires from the shallows within use range (standing counts); deeper -> swimming/tread as before; no 0.3 m headway in 4 s -> WadeGaveUp ("can't wade any closer - going round") and the mover route. Affordability check: in the water it measures straight to ladder reach, not RouteSwim (the walking route round the dock).
- Jump aboard from the water: the existing check (gap <= 4 m, deck <= 1.6 m up, jump reach, clear) runs before the wade step every 1.5 s; from the shallows a longship's deck is usually higher than that, so the ladder.

It came ashore and swung at you (blocked, then a hit on you) while the companion stood by; engaged only when it hit the companion (dist 1.0). ~20 "[Combat] Engage" lines while swimming ashore.

### 1.5.90 - beehive command, grey stamina bar, food log spam (user, log 32bba563: "Tried to use command on a beehive and the NPC just moved to the area" / "the stamina bar above the head ... shows up as just a grey bar ... It may have broken when I came back through the portal" - "honey should stay in the inventory")
- Log 32bba563: ships fine (side walks 0.9 / 2.1 m, one jump each, no stuck). Beehive: no Beehive case in the command raycast -> "[Direct] move to". Portal: "[HUD] Removing stale companion bars" then "Created" - the next frame.
- New AI/MHR_Hive.cs (state per companion, driven from CompanionAI.UpdateAI before boarding, counted in the "busy" flag): walk to within 2.5 m (eye to hive), face it, "interact" trigger + RPC_Extract (as UpkeepHoney), pick up the new honey drops within 3 m of m_spawnPoint (DropsNear before/after) into its pack; ends with "picked up N honey - keeping it" (counts auto-pickup too) and RestoreFollowOrPatrol. 30 s to reach, 20 s to collect. CompanionInteract.DirectHive: first companion in scope; empty hive -> hc_cmd_hive_empty; ward checked (flash). CancelHiveOrder in the cancel paths and the portal warp.

CreateBar sets health_fast active; ShowHud drops a stale entry (both bars destroyed) and rebuilds at once.

### 1.5.89 - getting off a ship: nearest side facing you, run and jump, no mid-air follow route (user, log 81234e48: "The NPC struggled a bit with getting off the boat. They would eventually jump off")
- Log 81234e48 (food ticks ~1/s): boarding fine 4 times (launch spot, dive, tread, ladder, seat). Leaving from the bow seat (sit_box (2), where you'd pointed it): ~10 s from "getting off" to the first "jumping off" (deck walk to SpotNear(you) near the stern, likely the 5 s stuck limit); the first leap came down on the deck ("getting off" again ~1 s later), the second cleared. In the air ShipUnder is null, so MHR_Follow ran ("taking the 30m way round").
- MHR_Deck.SideSpot(ship, me, you): edge cells (a missing 4-neighbour whose direction . to-you > 0.5), nearest to the companion; picked once per leave (fallback SpotNear(you)). Walk(..., stuckAfter) - 3 s for leaving; Walk logs the start ([Deck] walking X m over N cells, debug) and the first stuck ([Deck] stuck at ..., info).
- Leave: MoveTowards(dir to you, run) every frame, Leap when on the ground (retry 0.5 s); no deck walk for 1.5 s after a leap. FollowHook: off the deck but in the air within 3 s of the leap -> keep MoveTowards(leap dir) instead of the follow route.

### 1.5.88 - swim stamina like the player, swim to ladder reach, ordered aboard gets off (user, log 5930316c: "waiting in the water for their stamina to recharge. They seemed to wait till it got to 90% rather than 60% or enough to get to the boat" / "stuck about getting on the boat. I commanded them and they came aboard. Then did not leave when I did")
- Log 5930316c (no food, 50 max stamina): ran dry swimming 11.9 m out, WaterSafety to the bank, launch spot 3 m from the ladder, "resting before the swim to the ladder (0/47)" - 6.6 m at 10/s x 1.25 + 5. Second boarding: "the swim to the ladder (9 m) needs about 61 stamina, it has 50 at most" (RouteSwim of the walking route) -> GiveUp/hold; ordered: dived from 4.4 m and made it. Ordered boarding never set SatOn, so it stayed seated when you left (15:59:09).
- Player.OnSwimming: Lerp(m_swimStaminaDrainMinSkill 5, m_swimStaminaDrainMaxSkill 2, Swim skill) while target speed > 0.1. Companion: [Stamina] SwimDrainAtSkill0 (new key, default 5; the old SwimDrainRate 10 is orphaned so existing configs move to the player's rate) x MHR_Water.SwimDrainSkill (Lerp(1, 0.4, Swim)) in CompanionStamina and MHR_Water.SwimStamina.
- MHR_Board.LadderReach 1.7 m taken off the ladder distance in KeepSwimming, the dive's stamina need and the affordability check; the affordability check runs after the launch spot is picked and uses launch -> ladder when there is one (else RouteSwim).
- SatOn set for ordered boarding too; ShouldLeaveSeat only while CompanionSetup.GetFollow().

### 1.5.87 - original portal-jump warp removed (user: "What else does the 'portal jump' touch. I am not sure we need this with all the movement changes we have made")
- The rule (follow target moved > 15 m since the last look -> TeleportToFollowTarget) and its _lastFollowPos / _lastFollowPosAt fields and the unused PortalJumpThreshold const are gone; nothing else read them. Every game teleport (TeleportWorld portals, Teleport dungeon doors, Minimap/console/chat) goes through Player.TeleportTo, which PortalPatches handles (queues following companions, warps them on arrival, loaded or not). Still there: Follow TeleportDistance catch-up (50 m out of sight and not progressing / 75 m), the 1.5.69 dungeon-door follow (other side, > 30 m), hazard recovery (water), StuckTeleport (off by default), ReturnLogic recall.

### 1.5.86 - stale portal-jump warp, deck pace, ladder mid-air (user, log 79fb1bde: "When I first jumped off the NPC was moving very slow" / "When I jumped off the boat they teleported to me" / "they jump to the ladder and teleported onto the deck" / "Is there leftover old code from the original mod causing a conflict?")
- Warp: the original mod's follow rule "the follow target moved > 15 m since the last look -> TeleportToFollowTarget" (meant for portals). The OnShip branch returns before it, so _lastFollowPos was your spot when it sat down; you walked ~16 m ashore and it warped (no "[Board] getting off the ship" line, next move from (-309, -343) where you were). Now the last look must be < 0.5 s old (_lastFollowPosAt), and AttachToShip / DetachFromShip clear it. Covers every early-return gate (sleep, rest, UI).
- Slow at load: "Player onboard" in the game log but ShipUnder(p) (ground ship / 1.2 m ray) missed you -> Leave walked it at walking pace (MHR_Deck.Walk walked the last 3 m). Aboard(p, ship) = ShipUnder or Ship.IsPlayerInBoat (FollowHook, ShouldLeaveSeat). Walk(..., walkLast): seats 1 m, leaving/following on deck 0 (jog); Leave releases the walker before the jump so walk pace can't stick.
- Ladder: Ladder.Interact (game) puts you at m_targetPos at once - no climb, for the player too. It fired in mid-dive (in use distance in the air); now only while swimming or on the ground.

### 1.5.85 - deck route planner, walking to seats and off ships, launch spot (user, log ce2af5bd: "It looked like they still took the long way around one of the times. They also seem to be teleporting a bit. Especially when commanding them to take a specific seat. Rather than getting up and walking to the seat they just apear there" / "A small route planning should only run while on a boat" / "a grid for each ship type")
- Log ce2af5bd: boarding worked, proper seat (sit_box). Long way once: ladder 9.7 m off (past the 8 m dive range), the mover took a 26 m route. Teleports: ladder top -> seat (AttachToShip), seat -> seat on the seat command (AttachToShip), seat -> ladder on going ashore (DetachFromShip). WaterSafety "stamina low while swimming" right after sitting (its state outlived the climb).
- New AI/MHR_Deck.cs: one grid per ship type (Utils.GetPrefabName), built the first time a companion uses it, in the ship's own frame: 0.5 m cells, a ray along -ship.up hits that ship's flat deck (normal . up >= 0.7), head room (capsule 0.6-1.6 m clear of vehicle/Default/static_solid/piece), and the deck carries on a cell each way (off the rail). A* (8 directions, no corner cutting), path followed in the ship's frame, replanned every 2 s, "stuck" after 5 s without 0.2 m progress; walks (SetWalk) in the last 3 m. API: For, SpotNear(ship, world, out local), Walk(ai, ship, local, dist, out stuck), Release. Only runs for a companion on a deck.
- MHR_Board aboard branch: walks to the deck cell nearest the seat (0.6 m), then AttachToShip; stuck 5 s or 20 s in all -> sits anyway. Free(chair, self) ignores the companion itself.
- Seat command while seated: DetachFromShip (stands up) then SetPendingShipBoard with the pointed seat.
- DetachFromShip: stands up in place like Player.AttachStop (seat attach point + m_detachOffset), or the deck cell next to the seat (within 2 m), carrying the ship's point velocity - no teleport to the ladder or next to you. CompanionAI keeps _attachedChair.
- FollowHook: on a ship you're not on (and you're on your feet, not swimming) -> Leave: deck walk to the cell nearest you, then a leap over the side towards you (or walk); after 15 s the normal follow and its catch-up take over. On the same deck as you (no seat) -> deck walk to within 1.5 m of the cell nearest you.
- Launch spot: ladder 2-15 m flat, on dry ground -> LaunchSpot: rings of 24 round the ladder (1-15 m), first ring with dry ground (not the ship, not 4 m above the ladder, within 40 m of us), nearest to us. Mover goes there; at it (1.2 m) the dive branch runs even past 8 m, and wades in when the water ahead isn't deep enough to dive. Mover gave up -> dropped.
- WaterSafety: Clear(Character); skipped and cleared while IsOnShip or standing on a ship (AttachToShip clears it too).

### 1.5.84 - boarding: dive in, tread water, real seats, seat command (user, log ca66e4e5: "Its faster to jump off the dock to the boat and wait till you pop up from the water then use the ladder ... rather than just treading water for a moment to wait for the ladder to be in range" / "sitting in the middle of the boat rather than one of the seats. I tried to use command on the seats and was ignored. There are 2 seats near the front and 2 near the rear")
- Log ca66e4e5: no stuck; "aboard - taking a seat: 'interactive/mast' at (0.70, 0.64, -0.24)" - the mast's Chair (nearest to the ladder); seat commands "[Direct] 0 companion(s) -> board ship" (DirectBoard skipped IsOnShip and ignored the pointed chair); swim: took the 24 m way round, swam to 5.2 m from the ladder, WaterSafety "stamina low while swimming - heading for the nearest shore", rested, dived in, used the ladder.
- CompanionStamina drains only while swimming and moving (> 0.25 m/s) - treading water is free.
- MHR_Board: ladder within 8 m flat - swimming within 3.5 m: tread (move to within 0.8 m, then StopMoving) until Ladder.InUseDistance, 20 s then GiveUp ("the ladder never came within reach"); on dry ground with no wall: walk straight at it, and at the edge (water deeper than 0.8 m 1.2 m ahead) dive in (MHR_Jump.Leap) if stamina >= SwimStamina x 1.25 + 5, else rest on the dock. WaterSafety.Tick skips shore-seeking while MHR_Board.KeepSwimming (ladder within 8 m, and within 3.5 m or enough stamina for the rest).
- Seats: MHR_Board.IsMast (name contains "mast") excluded in Free and in DirectBoard's list. CompanionInteract: a Chair under the pointed collider (m_inShip, not the mast) goes to DirectBoard - the first companion takes it: AttachToShip if aboard ("moving to the seat you pointed at"), else boards with it as its seat.
- The 1.5.84 code went out in commit 75b02f0 still labelled 1.5.83 (the doc script failed on a quote); the version and docs followed in the next commit.

### 1.5.83 - boarding: resting isn't stuck, wait on dry land, seat log (user, log 6b069f3f: "Seemed like the NPC got stuck the 2nd time and waited for their stamina to get full then just stood there in the water" / "The NPC is also sitting on a stool that does not look to be there" - choice: log the seat first)
- Log 6b069f3f: boardings 1 and 3 worked (rested, swam to the ladder, "used the ship's ladder", "aboard - taking a seat"); "you've gone ashore, getting off the ship" worked. Boarding 2: 16 m from the ship, ladder 14 m over water, stamina ~43/75 (one honey); "too tired to swim across - waiting on the bank" at (-315.2, 29.1, -352.6) - shallow water - and MHR_Board's 16 s no-progress rule gave up while it rested; the hold just stopped it there.
- MHR_Mover.RestingForSwim (WaitStamina, recent Go): MHR_Board refreshes ProgressAt while it's true ("resting before the swim (h/m stamina)").
- Unaffordable swim: the mover caps its wait at 90% of max stamina, so it never notices; MHR_Board checks RouteSwim -> SwimStamina x 1.25 + 5 against MaxStamina and gives up at once ("the swim to the ladder (N m) needs about X stamina, it has Y at most").
- GiveUp: DryShore - nearest point with water depth < 0.1 within 15 m (rings 2-15 m, 12 directions, a little towards the ship), walked to while holding (MHR_Mover, "Board"), then stand facing the ship.
- Seat log: "aboard - taking a seat: '<path under the ship>' at <local position> on the ship (world ...)" - to find the seat that looks empty.

### 1.5.82 - boarding ships like a player (user, log e26ca237: "The NPC does not seem able to use the ladder to get onto the ship or jump to it" / "The NPC should be able to get on a ship if I can get on a ship. It might be worth having the follow in my footsteps fall back as well"; choices: board on its own while following, wait on shore when it can't, stand on deck with no free seat)
- Log e26ca237: following you onto the docked longship it picked the ship's ladder ("2.1m below where it needs to be - going up the ladder at (-322.4, 33.1, -351.3)") but never got within its 2 m use distance (Ladder.InUseDistance - it hangs on the hull over the water); the ship isn't on the route map ("taking the 22m way round ... something in the way", "no closer for 4s"); jumps only land on ground/pieces. Then it dived in and swam about until out of stamina. The board order (DirectBoard -> SetPendingShipBoard) walked at the ship's middle with BaseAI.MoveTo, attached within 6 m of a ladder / 10 m of a ladderless ship, and after 30 s regardless; no free seat put it on the ship's middle.
- New AI/MHR_Board.cs, per frame: aboard (ShipUnder - GetStandingOnShip, else a 1.2 m ray down onto the ship's vehicle colliders) -> a free seat (none within 0.5 m of a character) -> AttachToShip, else stand on deck; within a ship ladder's use distance -> Ladder.Interact (to m_targetPos); on the ground with DeckSpot (the ship's closest collider point, 1 m inward, ray down onto the deck) at gap 0.3-4 m, dy -3..+1.6, MHR_Jump.Reach >= gap + 0.3 and no wall between -> MHR_Jump.Leap; else MHR_Mover.Go ("Board" tag - swims with the usual stamina rules) to the nearest ladder (or the deck spot). No progress (closer to the ship) for 8 s -> MHR_Follow.StepsOnto: the player's last step before their recorded steps on the deck and the first on it - walk there, then jump if it's up, or the ladder catches it. 16 s without progress, or 60 s in all -> "can't get aboard - waiting on the shore", held 20 s while you're on that ship (then it tries again).
- Auto: MHR_Board.FollowHook before MHR_Follow.Update - you aboard a ship within [Follow] BoardRange (20 m) that's moving < 3 m/s, not in combat, [Follow] AutoBoard on. A seat taken that way is left (DetachFromShip) when you've been ashore 2 s within 40 m (ShouldLeaveSeat).
- CompanionAI: _pendingShip* fields and the 30 s attach removed; SetPendingShipBoard -> MHR_Board.Start; IsBoardingShip / CancelPendingShipBoard -> MHR_Board. DirectBoard with no free seat boards the same way (was a teleport to the ship's middle).
- Standing on deck: the game carries any character on a ship's deck (Character.ApplyGroundForce takes the ground body's point velocity) - to be confirmed in a test.

### 1.5.81 - shrine button labels, no dark layers (user, log bccad051: "The text on the buttons in odin's shrine are a bit small" / "I think its the dark background that for each of the sections that makes it look odd" / "Lets remove it from the extras too" / bigger popup buttons: "We can revisit it after the next test")
- Log bccad051: no errors; "[UI] Game look: canvas 'UnifiedPopup' ConstantPixelSize, reference (800, 600), scale factor 1.33", panel 'woodpanel_crafting_240' border scale 1.00. (The new Gunter emptied the old one's unsummon tombstone - the user sent it with the command action.)
- Shrine labels: hc_shrine_btn_summon "Summon", hc_shrine_btn_recover_list "Recover" - the popup buttons are sized for Yes/No and auto-size long labels down.
- CompanionPanel: s_noDark (= StarterMode, set at the start of Build) makes the three sections' ColBg and the BtnTint layers (action button, toggles, slider tracks, category arrows) clear; the selected toggle still shows gold (SetToggleActive). Trader tab unchanged.
- Bigger popup buttons: possible (resize UnifiedPopup's button rects while ours is up, restore on close - the popup is shared with the game's own); after the next test.

### 1.5.80 - shrine as the game popup, unsummoned recovery, customise window scale (user, log 8ee0e331: "The font size is two different sizes. It should be something close to how the 'ok' button looks" / "when there is no companions the back button on the recover screen is at the top and covering the message" / "The summon companion page looks odd. The buttons and background look like they are poorly scaled ... Same with the Odin's Shrine pop-up ... The unsummon option does not have this issue. Same with the quit game menu" / accidental unsummon: recovery "without gear", "only be until the player logs in again ... a toggle in the settings" / Yes right, the game's standard popup order)
- Log 8ee0e331: "[UI] Game look: title text 'Valheim-Norsebold' 32, body text 'Valheim-AveriaSansLibre' 17 (auto), Craft button 334 x 60, label 'Valheim-AveriaSerifLibre' 28 (auto), parts: Text, gamepad_hint" - three different sources; our canvases scaled 1920x1080 while the copied sprites belong to the game's GUI canvas, and the panel copy lacked the image's pixelsPerUnitMultiplier. Summon with the price at 1: 147 -> 146 coins. (I first misread the log: the "Gunter" after the unsummon was the new companion being renamed - Guntere, then Gunter; the user caught it. No ghost.)
- ShrinePanel is now a controller over UnifiedPopup: YesNoPopup / WarningPopup pushed, then the button texts replaced through buttonRightText / buttonLeftText / buttonCenterText (UnifiedPopup.ShowYesNo: Yes = right, No = left). Menu: right "Summon companion", left "Recover companions"; text flavour + "Summoning a new companion asks an offering of N coins.". Recover list: statuses in the text, right "Recover" (then one Yes/No per candidate: Lost, Unsummoned, Unknown), left "Back"; nothing recoverable -> WarningPopup with "Back". Each step pops ours before pushing the next. Closing as before (Use/Escape/4 m), JustClosed guard. The custom panel, ModUiLayers.ShrinePanel and GameLook.ApplyTitle/ApplyBody/CopyButton are gone.
- Game order check: UnifiedPopup YesNo is Yes right / No left (server warning, save management, build categories, PlayFab, restore backup); Menu's quit/logout dialogs are prefab layouts (not visible in code, and nothing reorders them by language - no RTL handling). Kept the standard popup order.
- Unsummon: MHR_Roster.Forget with [General] RecoverUnsummoned (default on) keeps the entry with Unsummoned = true, Inventory cleared, Gen+1, and its id in s_sessionUnsummoned; Status.Unsummoned; RecoverAt accepts it (no gear) and clears the flag. EnsureLoaded drops Unsummoned entries not in the session set (the set is cleared when ZNet.instance changes - the world loaded again). Saved as a trailing bool (ReadOptionalBool; older rosters load). Despawn confirm adds hc_despawn_popup_recover when on.
- Unsummon tombstone: RespawnOwner is "" - MHR_RespawnTracker now logs "Tombstone with an unsummoned companion's gear - nothing to respawn" instead of "another player's game was running it".
- Customise window: GameLook.MatchGameScale copies the game GUI root canvas's CanvasScaler (found from UnifiedPopup's canvas; logged once); ApplyPanel copies pixelsPerUnitMultiplier; CompanionPanel.CreateButton labels take the popup OK button's font/size (auto-size, max = its size).

### 1.5.79 - shrine menu look and closing (user: "Does the font use the same in game font and size? It just looks like something is off for the buttons. Lets remove the X ... Pressing 'e' or use again should close the menu just like a chest or walking away from the shrine" / flavour text in the user's words / log 7dfa35b7: "[UI] Game look: panel background 'woodpanel_crafting_240' from the inventory window (Crafting/Bkg)", coin popup worked 4x, no errors / summon window's X: backlog)
- Text: ShrinePanel titles copy InventoryGui.m_containerName (font, material, size - fontSizeMax if auto-sized -, colour, style), all other text InventoryGui.m_recipeDecription (GameLook.ApplyTitle / ApplyBody); 1.5.78 had the first non-Liberation font with my own sizes 20/15. Logged once ("[UI] Game look: title text ... body text ...").
- Buttons: GameLook.CopyButton - the Craft button with its own children (1.5.78 used CompanionPanel.CreateButton, which strips every child but the label) less UIGamePad hints and m_craftProgressPanel, at the template's own height; logged once with size, label font and child names. Recover rows are 48 high to fit.
- Closing: no X; Update closes on Escape / JoyButtonB / Use or JoyUse (0.3 s after opening) / player > 4 m from the shrine or dead ("[Shrine] Menu closed - walked away"); ShrinePanel.JustClosed (0.3 s) stops the closing press reopening it through CompanionShrine.Interact.
- Flavour: hc_shrine_flavor under the title on the main menu.

### 1.5.78 - summoning, coin check first, game look (user, log b2e01999: "Can we make the menu use the ingame default look? I want to move away from using custom textures as much as possible ... consoles connecting to a dedicated server ... it fits in the game better" / "the coin check ... should pop-up a notification when you click on the button to bring up the menu ... If you dont have enough coins ... the custom menu should not open" / "'Summon Companion'" / the user's texts for both descriptions / "The text in the shrine should also be updated to Summon a companion")
- Log b2e01999: shrine menu and recover list fine; with 0 coins Purchase was pressed 14 times with nothing visible - MessageHud (order 1000) draws under the window's 0.85 backdrop (1053). Now ShrinePanel.Summon checks CoinsInPack first and shows a UnifiedPopup WarningPopup (1900) when short (CompanionManager.ShowShortPopup); HireWithCoins re-checks with the same popup and takes the coins.
- Strings: hc_ui_btn_spawn / hc_ui_btn_hire "Summon Companion", hc_ui_title_hire "Summon a Companion", hc_ui_desc_hire / hc_ui_desc_starter (the user's texts), hc_shrine_opt_buy "Summon a new companion ({0} coins)", hc_msg_hire_need "Odin asks for an offering of {0} coins to summon a companion - you have {1}, {2} short.", hc_msg_hired "Odin has summoned a new companion for you!". Trader-mode strings kept: TraderUIPatches still opens CompanionPanel as a Companions tab when the optional trader mod is installed.
- Hair/beard names: CompanionPanel.StyleName - the item's m_name localised (as the game's creator shows it), else the prefab name.
- Game look: new UI/GameLook.cs - PanelSprite() = the largest image on the inventory window's player/container/crafting panels (their own Image or a child named *bkg*), with its material, logged once ("[UI] Game look: panel background 'x' from ..."; plain colour + log if none). Applied to StarterCompanionPanel's container (was PanelBackground.png), the slider tracks (was SliderBackground.png), ShrinePanel's background. Gender/type toggles and category arrows keep the copied Craft button's own sprite (were ButtonBackground/CategoryBackground.png). ShrinePanel buttons are Craft-button copies (CompanionPanel.CreateButton, now internal). The packed PNGs stay in the DLL for ConfigPanel, HUD bars, interact panel and the radial icons (backlog).
- Logs: "[Panel] Summon confirmed", "[StarterPanel] Summon cancelled", "[Shrine] Summon - offering N, you have M coins".

### 1.5.77 - shrine menu, combo on a latched tick, no fleeing with one on (user, log 0a0d75c2: "That was much better! Does the NPC use a combo on the tick? They should if they have stamina until the tick is dead. Then keep clear until stamina is restored" / shrine: "Using the shrine should give a popup that has 2 options. 1. Recover lost companions 2. Purchase a new companion ... The purchase should pop-up the same starting window ... Then have a purchase button at the bottom ... report they are short and by how much"; dead: status only; Shift+E removed; tick fixes in the same build)
- Log 0a0d75c2: latched handling worked ("latched on to Gunter" -> swings, no block/back-off), but single swings only - combos are tactics (_burstLeft / WantsChain) and TacticsFor is off against a latched tick. WantsChain now also true while LatchedTarget() and stamina >= AttackCost (no reserve) - CompanionChainPatch chains the combo; once it's dead the normal rules (catch breath) apply.
- Both deaths: "Badly hurt - running to its owner" with the tick still on. The flee branch is skipped while anything is latched ("[Flee] hp=..% but X is latched on - running won't shake it, fighting it").
- "let go" was never logged when the tick died: `_latched != was` uses Unity's ==, and a destroyed tick equals null. Now ReferenceEquals and the kept name ("X let go of Gunter (dead)").
- Spear (40 deg) missed a tick latched 95 deg off; iron sword (90 deg) hit ticks 21-33 deg off - backlog (sweep, dodge roll when it can't be reached, ticks on others first).
- Shrine: new UI/ShrinePanel.cs (ModUiLayers.ShrinePanel = 1056, Hud.InRadial while open): menu -> Recover list (MHR_Roster.GetRoster; Recover button for Lost/Unknown -> the old YesNoPopup text incl. the Unknown warning -> MHR_Roster.RecoverAt by the shrine; Dead "its tombstone will bring it back", no countdown) or Purchase (StarterCompanionPanel.ShowHirePanel - opens even when short; HireWithCoins checks on Purchase: "You need {0} coins to purchase a companion - you have {1}, {2} short"). MHR_Shrine.Use/Ask/Hire/StatusText removed; Interact has no alt branch. Strings: hc_shrine_*, title "Purchase a Companion", button "Purchase ({0} coins)".

### 1.5.76 - latched ticks fought, 1.5.75 combat rolled back (user, log fd7b1683, after the whole log was re-read: "The issue is what we were working on from the last build ... The ticks latch on and the NPC tries to move back from it ... This time the ticks latched on while the NPC was blocking. The NPC did not swing back. Even when not blocking the NPC only sometimes tried to attack the tick while it was feeding. You can roll back the combat changes." / "We just have to be careful going forward as this is becoming complex enough that I do not want to break other features.")
- Rolled back to 1.5.74: CompanionCombatTactics.cs and CompanionBlockLog.cs (counter after a block, Leaper guard-up, TryEvade skip); [Combat] CounterWindow removed. Loot never swimming (1.5.75) kept.
- The log: "[You]" lines are the PLAYER (PlayerCombatLog) - the companion's own hits are "[Durability] Armor reduction" lines. Fight 1 (log 2372-2621): ~10 "Blocked $enemy_tick ... 6-8 damage" in a row, no swing - consistent with a tick latched on its front (bites from the front are blocked; Humanoid.BlockAttack), guard held, stamina drained to "Low on stamina (26 %) - backing out". Death 2 (4392-4436): 8 hits of ~45 (6-7 after armour) with no block, only "Keeping just out of $enemy_tick's reach" / "Stepping aside" - a feeding tick, and the 1.5.74 latched handling never engaged (TacticsFor stayed on) - either not detected or another tick (2-3 around) was the target; no log line said which.
- 1.5.74 regression: "[Balanced] bow for $enemy_tick (2.0 m): it's above, out of reach" twice (3742, 4148) - a latched or mid-jump tick is above its feet. MeleeCanReach's above/below now needs the target on the ground or flying, and not latched.
- Balanced took the spear (40 deg stab, waits to face within ~12 deg) every tick fight; a latched tick turns with the companion (Attack.UpdateAttach keeps it at a fixed offset), so it might never line up.
- Latched (CompanionCombatTargets.Latched, 0.25 s cache) now: logs "[Combat] X latched on to Gunter" / "X let go of Gunter (dead)"; UpdateTarget switches to it every frame ("is latched on - turning on it (was fighting Y)"); UpdateBlocking clears the guard while anything is latched; melee reach ignores the height rule; no stab step-back; the swing doesn't wait for facing; canSeeTarget true. TacticsFor off against it (1.5.74).
- Backlog (user): a bow-swap cooldown, especially for fast-moving targets, so it doesn't flip between bow and melee (it swapped to the bow and back during tick fights).

### 1.5.75 - hit back after a block, jumpers, loot never swims (user, log fd7b1683: "NPC jumped into the water and was swimming around. Maybe to get the dropped items?" / "The tick fights were only a little bit better. Most of the time the NPC just blocked rather than trying to attack or swing at the tick.")
- Swimming was loot: after a Neck kill "[Move|Loot] taking the 13m way round ... deep water", "diving in", swimming, "stamina low while swimming - heading for the nearest shore", waiting on the bank, swimming again - "Neck Tail is in deep water" came only after. MHR_Loot skipped only a route over JobSwim; now any RouteSwim > 0.5 m skips the drop ("is across water - leaving it" / "stopped picking up (it's across water)"), and TryWaterJump never dives in for the "Loot" tag. Wading stays.
- Ticks (10 fights): 37 guards raised, 29 blocks/parries (0 through), 26 swings, 9 missed with the tick already 4-9 m off. Spacing held "just out of its reach (3.4 m)" - the tick's leap range, against a 1.8 m spear - and after each block (tick landed 0.3-1 m away) TryEvade/Spacing stepped out again; the only opening ("can't swing again for X s") almost never fired (0.6 s cooldown). Now:
  - CompanionBlockLog postfix calls NoteBlocked(attacker); Spacing: blocked/parried this target within [Combat] CounterWindow (0.8 s) and it's within our reach, with the stamina reserve -> "Blocked X - hitting back", return 2 (no hold). TryEvade doesn't step away inside that window.
  - Leaper(t): radius < 0.8 and Danger > our reach + 1 m - Spacing stands guard up facing it (return 2, no hold) for up to 3 s, then goes in (return 0) for 3 s, and repeats; TryEvade doesn't step out of its jump. Big hitters (trolls) keep the old spacing.
- Arrows: ammo=1 for most of the session (37 status lines), 4 twice; the ticks were fought at 4-5 m - melee by distance.

### 1.5.74 - bow when melee can't reach, leash from you, giving up, draw at the edge, no tick roll (user, on 1.5.73's plan: "I never have an issue hitting things that are standing close and was able to swing on a tick that was attached to me. This is something that should also get updated by the enemy strategies overhaul." / "did not use arrows on a neck or on a grey dwarf while back in the meadows ... As soon as I added more arrow the NPC started to use them" / "Some of the draws at the edge of range caused the NPC to draw, mob gets out of range, run after it, then drawn again" / "some bugs were below us and it would have been a good place to use a bow" / "chasing a neck in deep water and just stuck on it rather than dropping it and following me again or going after a close target" / "include all of it and build 1.5.74")
- Tick roll removed (settings ShakeOffTicks / DodgeStamina gone, no InDodge / IsDodgeInvincible patches). MHR_TickShake -> `AI/MHR_Latched.cs`, detection only: a latched tick is the first target and TacticsFor is off against it (the log's tick died to the first swing once he swung). Ticks: enemy strategies overhaul.
- Arrows: log evidence still says distance, not count - the 16.6 m Neck was shot before arrows were added (wantRanged by distance > BowSwitchDistance 15); the 13.2 / 11.1 m Neck and 13.1 m Seeker got the sword; the inventory visit that added arrows was followed by a wheel order and the next fight was stance 2 (Ranged). The Balanced status line had a lifetime cap of 30 (none after the dungeon) - now every 3 s, with ammo=N (the stack of the best bow's ammo) so the next log settles it.
- WeaponLogic Balanced: after the distance rule, the bow when CompanionCombatAI.BowOnly(target) and ClearShot: MeleeCanReach false (swim-deep water; within 3 m flat and > 1.4 m above / 1.8 m below; no full walking route, or a route > 2x straight + 15 m - MHR_Mover.WalkRoute, cached 2 s per enemy) or Defensive and > DefendRange from you. "[Balanced] bow for X (d m): why" every 5 s.
- Dungeon (Defensive): PickTarget allows an enemy beyond DefendRange when a bow is ready, it is alerted, within 30 m and a clear shot; CheckTargetStillValid keeps it while the shot is clear; ChaseMove stands (never walks to it) with the bow out. Melee rules unchanged.
- Leash from you: [Combat] LeashFromYou 25 - PickTarget skips enemies further than that from you; CheckTargetStillValid lets the target go (10 s ignore). Not for an order, hunting, or something within 3 m hitting it (the existing early returns). The 40 m companion-to-target leash stays.
- Give up: [Combat] GiveUpTime 10 - a target MeleeCanReach says no to, whose health hasn't dropped for that long, is let go for 20 s ("[Combat] Can't get at X (why) and not a hit on it for 10 s - letting it go"). PickTarget adds 15 to the score of an enemy it can't reach (not one up close on it).
- Bow edge: [Combat] BowEdgeMargin 3 - the bow's reach for starting is 27 m, 36 m with a draw on; past 30 m with a draw on it walks (ChaseMove, run false) to 27 m still drawing, instead of the chase running and the draw lapsing (Maintain cancels after 1 s without Tick).

### 1.5.73 - ticks, Necks in deep water, shallow pools, spikes (user, log 93298113: "Tick sucked NPC blood and NPC did nothing? Did a bit of standing ground in water at the start. Water was shallow. Would not use bow even with arrows. Only had 4 left... Got stuck fighting a neck in the water. Kept going after neck in deep water. Got hit by spikes a few times.")
- Tick: it latched on (0.0-0.3 m, 5 dmg a bite) and the tactics "stepped back to get its breath back" for 7.7 s. Game: Attack.TryAttach/UpdateAttach - a latched tick lets go only when its host dies or IsDodgeInvincible (checked every 0.3 s), and attaches behind/beside the host, out of a forward swing. New `AI/MHR_TickShake.cs`: finds a Humanoid whose m_currentAttack is attached to the companion (FieldRefAccess m_currentAttack / m_isAttached / m_attachTarget), rolls like Player.UpdateDodge (turn, "dodge" trigger, stamina [Combat] DodgeStamina 10) in the first SafeStep-checked direction (away from the tick first), postfixes Character.InDodge (0.75 s, so the roll's root motion applies - Character.AddRootMotion only while InDodge) and Character.IsDodgeInvincible (0.45 s) for companions. PickTarget returns a latched tick first (even holding an order); TacticsFor is off against it (no stepping away). [Combat] ShakeOffTicks to turn off.
- Bow "not used with 4 arrows": not the arrows - FindReadyBow has no minimum. Every dungeon fight in Balanced started within 5 m (wantRanged=False at 0.1-5.1 m); the Balanced switch distance decides (backlog: the Balanced bow-distance setting with the stats overhaul). Ranged stance shot every Neck.
- Neck: AvoidChase keeps a chase into swim-deep water when the target is hostile and a bow is ready, but Balanced still had the sword out and the mover swam/waded after it. Now Balanced picks the bow for a target in swim-deep water (WouldSwim, >1.5 m away), and ChaseMove refuses a point in swim-deep water unless swimming already or ordered ("not swimming out after ... - waiting on the bank").
- Shallow pool: SafeStep refused any step through water over 0.4 m, so standing in a 0.5 m pool every step failed. Now the limit is max(0.4, depth where it stands + 0.15), capped at 1.0.
- Spikes: hit 15 dmg x6 (0.7 after armour) at 1.6-3.7 m from the stake centres while following you past them. Per-step check now also looks at where it stands (radius + 0.35) and 1.2 m ahead (radius 0.8, was 0.8 m / 0.6); at the edge it turns away 1.5 (was 0.6).

### 1.5.72 - movement safety audit, spike walls (user, 2026-10-25: "run a check on all the movement in the mod to see if there are any other sections that do not have the safety check for things like a drop? Also what about defenses like a spike wall?" / "The build plan sounds good" / tar escape: "a tar pit could have a large drop next to it" / following your path: leave it - "I am taking a specific path for a reason")
- Audit (read-only sweep): MHR_Mover.Go has edge stops (LedgeAhead, MaxDrop 3), swim/stamina, fire routing; tactics steps use SafeStep. Vanilla BaseAI MoveTo/Flee/RandomMovement/RandomMovementArroundPoint have no drop checks, and companions run with m_avoidWater/m_avoidFire/m_afraidOfFire off. Nothing knew about stake walls (MHR_PieceDamageLog only logs piece damage).
- New AI/CompanionCombatSafety.cs: SafeGameMove (ChaseMove's fallback after the mover gave up - which it does right after "stopping at the edge" - only while SafeStep passes 2 m ahead, else stand), SafeFlee (low-HP flee fallback and the bow back-off: 30-deg steps up to 100 deg off "away", SafeStep 3 m, else stand), SafeWander (replaces the 3 RandomMovementAroundPoint + 3 RandomMovement in combat: a SafeStep-checked point within the radius, walked with MHR_Mover.Go "Combat"). The stab step-back SetMoveDir only if SafeStep 1 m. Log "[Combat] no safe way to back off ..." / "not walking on towards X ...".
- MHR_Mover: SpikeNear(p, r) - OverlapSphere (all layers, triggers too) for a collider whose parent has an Aoe with damage, on a Piece, not under a Fireplace (stakes, Dvergr stakes, modded defences; fires are handled separately). Used in SafeStep and Clear (step/sidestep checks), the per-step steering (spikes 0.8 m ahead -> slide along them, a little away - always, also when the goal is an enemy caught on them; "[Move|X] keeping off the spikes at"), the walking-step re-check and jump landings. In-progress walking steps (not jumps) are re-checked each frame for drop/fire/spikes ("stopped a step"). LandingHazard (fire or spikes 2-4 m on) blocks the gap leap and both water leaps. HazardAhead(pos, dir, dist) (drop/fire/spikes, no wall ray) guards the three HarvestController nudges at trees/rocks.
- Placement: CompanionAI.MHR_SafeSpotNear now also rejects water > 0.4, fire, spikes; CompanionManager.FindSpawnPosition tries it first (recall teleport via ReturnLogic, respawn at bed/player, roster recovery, hiring); ship detach without a ladder uses it. Tar/lava/drowning escape skips target spots on a rim (ground 1.5 m beyond more than 3 m lower), in fire or at spikes.
- Left as is: MHR_Follow walking your exact path (user); deliberate drops (MaxDrop 3 m, StuckDrop when stuck); climb/ladder jumps.

### 1.5.71 - hire companions at the shrine (user, 2026-10-24: "I want to add a way to purchase another NPC. Can we add that as an option to the shrine? At least for right now. Make the purchase price 1000 gold. I think we have a setting to adjust that already.")
- Existing: [General] CompanionPrice (2000, "coming soon") and CompanionManager.Purchase, which paid from another mod's bank (m_customData "TraderSharedBank_Balance") through the original mod's trader tab (TraderUIPatches, reflection on that mod's TraderUI) - nothing a vanilla player can use.
- Now: CompanionShrine hover "[Shift + E] Hire a companion (1,000 coins)"; Interact(alt) -> MHR_Shrine.Hire: price vs coins in your pack ($item_coins); short -> "You need 1,000 coins to hire a companion (you have N)"; else StarterCompanionPanel.ShowHirePanel - the starter's one-companion panel with CompanionPanel.HireMode (title "Hire a Companion", button "Hire (1,000 coins)", hire description). Confirm -> CompanionManager.HireWithCoins: re-checks coins, SpawnCompanion (human, next to you, owned and following), then removes the coins; panel closes. Close button: "[StarterPanel] Hire cancelled". Dverger tiers not offered here.
- CompanionPrice default 1000; an existing 2000 (the old default, never used) is moved to 1000 on load.

### 1.5.70 - jumped off a ledge in the mine (user, 2026-10-24, log 6f49ec1d: "Looks better. The NPC did jump off to the side at the end and did seem to go down a level after a mob that I was not currently chasing. I went in and out of this dungeon a few times to test if the NPC got stuck at all.")
- Dungeon in/out: 6 teleports, all "[Portal] Warped companion to ... (GameObject present)" next to you - no stuck companion.
- Going down a level: following you along the top (y 5051) of the Mistlands mine, "stuck - stepping back to the side", then "jumping the shallow water at (-3441.9, 5052.0, -5651.9)", "stopping at the edge (7.2m down)" - and it was on the level below (y 5041.9), where a Seeker came for it ("its target=Gunter") and it fought it there. So it didn't chase the mob down; MHR_Mover.TryWaterJump took the gap beside the ledge for water (DepthAt: water level vs ground far below) and leapt. The jump at the end (3588, same spot) is the same thing; that time it stopped at the edge.
- TryWaterJump: never when InInterior; and stops if the ground (or the water's surface) anywhere on the way is more than 1.5 m below its feet.
- Also: two deaths to Seekers in the mine (cornered / low stamina; the second "Badly hurt - running to its owner"); "Lost sight of" once.

### 1.5.69 - stuck in burial chambers (bug report, 2026-10-24: "there are instances where the companion cannot get out of a burial chambers, locked behind the invisible barrier. sometimes they won't even teleport to the player. my only fix was, commanding them not to follow me while they are inside the cave then i will go out and press Z")
- Game: dungeon entrances/exits are Teleport components; OnTriggerEnter/Interact only take the local Player (Player.TeleportTo, distantTeleport false). Our PortalPatches queues following companions on TeleportTo and warps them when UpdateTeleport finishes. The warp spot (FindNearbyPosition) and the follow teleport (TeleportToFollowTarget: 2.5 m behind + random 1.5) were random points with ZoneSystem.FindFloor - no wall check, so in a crypt's small entrance room the companion could land on the other side of a wall. And the follow teleport sits after the combat block in UpdateAI - a fight inside kept it from running.
- CompanionAI.MHR_SafeSpotNear(at, preferDir): 8 directions at 2 m then 1.2 m, starting behind you; needs a clear Linecast from your chest height to the spot's and a floor within 1.2 m of your height below it (Default/static_solid/terrain/piece/vehicle); none -> your own spot. Used by PortalPatches.WarpCompanion and TeleportToFollowTarget.
- UpdateAI, before combat: following, the follow target's InInterior() differs from the companion's, > 30 m apart and you're not mid-teleport -> "[Follow] You went out of/into the dungeon - following you through", Disengage, TeleportToFollowTarget.

### 1.5.68 - bow skill and aiming (user, 2026-10-24: "Does the bow skill impact the NPC like it does a player. I noticed that sometime the NPC seems really slow to draw and aim the bow." / "We should do 1 and 2")
- Checked: draw time (Humanoid.GetAttackDrawPercentage: m_drawDurationMin down to 20% by the companion's own Bows skill) and damage (GetRandomSkillFactor x draw) use the companion's skill - Gunter is Bows 5, so ~2.4 s full draws. Spread with a bow depends only on the draw (player too). The exception: ItemData.GetDrawStaminaDrain uses Player.m_localPlayer's skill factor. MHR_BowDraw.DrawDrain now computes m_drawStaminaDrain x (1 - 0.33 x the companion's skill factor).
- Aim: the draw starts within 45 deg, looses within aiAttackMaxAngle (10); turning was [Movement] TurnSpeed 300 deg/s. While drawing, Maintain sets m_turnSpeed/m_runTurnSpeed to max(TurnSpeed, [Combat] BowAimTurnSpeed 600); Cancel puts TurnSpeed back.
- Log 26d28214: one shot only - Balanced takes the bow over ~10 m (Greydwarfs at 16-18 m: one 74% draw at 12 m, then melee under 9 m); everything in the mine was close. Backlog: a setting for that distance with the stats overhaul (user).

### 1.5.67 - flanking off a ledge (user, 2026-10-24, log 26d28214: "It does seem a bit better. The NPC did fall off an edge and then was killed. They are standing up to enemies fairly well and did not go running off completely on their own.")
