- Log (Mistlands, then the infested mine at y ~5050): in the mine, "$enemy_babyseeker is busy with Human - working round behind it" -> "fell 7.1 m and took 20 fall damage" - Flank sent MHR_Mover.Go to a spot beside/behind the target with goal.y = our height and no ground check. Later, cornered by Seekers ("Can't get clear (nowhere to step back to) - fighting on what stamina it has", 26% then 5% stamina), a 111 hit (43 after armour) killed it.
- Flank now checks the straight way to its spot with MHR_Mover.SafeStep (ground every 0.5 m within -1/+0.8, no deep water, no fire); not safe -> "No safe way round X (a drop or water) - attacking from here", no flanking for 6 s. The other tactic moves already used SafeStep; spacing/approach goes to the target itself.
- Also in the log: falls of 5-8 m outdoors while following you down the Mistlands cliffs (following your path / dropping down); 1 "Lost sight of" (outdoors), no closed-door or dungeon-defensive "letting it come to us" lines - targets in the mine were within DefendRange; no errors.

### 1.5.66 - food top-up, combat freeze (user, 2026-10-24, log b78220a8: "The NPC should not wait until the food has expired to eat more. Maybe when there is just a few minutes left they can eat something new?" / "I teleported and started to just slide around on the ground.")
- Freeze: 968 NullReferenceExceptions from 17:15 to the end - CompanionCombatAI.BreathingRoomAfter (FinishOff <- ReadyToSwing <- UpdateCombat) read .transform of a destroyed creature in _attackers (a skeleton killed earlier; the list is refreshed in Tactics, ReadyToSwing can run on the old one). UpdateAI threw every FixedUpdate, so the companion kept its last movement - the sliding. Every foreach over _attackers/_close in CompanionCombatTactics now skips destroyed entries.
- Food: CompanionFood.TryAutoConsume only filled empty slots. Now, with no empty slot to fill this tick and not mid-swing, a slot with <= [Food] EatAgainMinutes 3 left that the game allows to be eaten again (CanEatAgain: under half its burn time, Player.CanEat's rule) is topped up with the same food from the pack ("[Food] Topped up X in slot n with s left"). A different food can't replace it before it expires (game rule: 3 slots); that happens when the slot empties.
- Same log: the 1.5.65 rules seen outdoors - "Lost sight of skeleton (behind a wall or door) - letting it go", re-engaging when back in view. Mostly a Smelt session (kiln, smelter, chests). Teleports (portal and console) moved the companion fine.

### 1.5.65 - which enemy to fight (user, 2026-10-23, a player's report: fights through walls, ignores recall/commands, goes for a Seeker behind a blocked door; user: "only attack mobs they have a path too, or have line of sight for ranged weapons, with a priority on close mobs ... Only open a door with nearby mobs when commanded. Indoor spaces they should swap to only going after mobs that have actual aggro"; "Lets include under a roof as well"; "the 'defensive fight' should be the default in a dungeon, with a toggle to turn it off")
- Found: targets came from BaseAI.FindEnemy (nearest enemy it can see or HEAR - hear range 20 m through walls; straight-line distance) + MHR_Threat.IsAfterUs; re-picked every 2 s with the same. Combat chases (MHR_Mover "Combat") opened any door on the way. Recall disengaged only targets > 4 m (ReturnLogic) and the next tick FindEnemy re-engaged; a move order ran ahead of combat while walking, then it went straight back. CompanionCombatAI.Recall()/_recallTimer existed but nothing called Recall().
- New AI/CompanionCombatTargets.cs (partial): PickTarget - Character.GetAllCharacters within max(viewRange, 20) (30 with a bow / Ranged stance): enemy, alive, not passive / ignored in water / sleeping / ignored-for-a-while, IsAfterUs, indoors (InInterior or roof raycast, 1 s cache) + [Combat] IndoorAggroOnly -> alerted only, InInterior + [Combat] DungeonDefensive -> within [Combat] DefendRange 8 of the owner (or on it and within 3 m), CanGetAt = edge < 2.5 m or MHR_BowDraw.ClearShot (eye + bow-hand line, no terrain/piece/static between). Score: distance -6 if it targets this companion, -2 if it targets a player, -3 the current target. Used by CompanionAI (not engaged, replaces FindEnemy) and UpdateTarget (every 1 s, not hunting).
- CheckTargetStillValid (every UpdateTarget, not hunting/ordered): out of sight 3 s -> "Lost sight of X (behind a wall or door) - letting it go" (ignored 8 s); dungeon defensive and > DefendRange + 3 from you -> "letting it come to us" (ignored 3 s); not when it targets us within 3 m.
- MHR_Mover: tag "Combat" (CombatTag) never opens a door - OpenDoor refuses, sets DoorBlocked + GaveUp ("closed X in the way - not opening it in a fight"); no route-door search for it. ChaseMove: DoorBlocked -> DoorInTheWay (ignored 15 s, Release). An attack order (DirectEngage -> Order(target)) chases with tag "Order" (doors allowed) and isn't dropped for sight.
- Orders: FireComeToMe (recall, super recall) and move-to-point call HoldForOrder -> Disengage + no new fights for [Combat] OrderHoldTime 10 s ("[Combat] Recalled - leaving X, no new fights for 10 s"); OnDamaged while holding only engages an attacker within 3 m (MayFightBack); the vanilla m_targetCreature fallback is skipped while holding and needs CanGetAt.

### 1.5.64 - Ranged stance weapons (user, 2026-10-23, log aec6b3cf: "When in ranged stance the NPC is still equipping a sword and shield. I thought they only fall back to that when being attacked and in melee range? At the end they would not fight the last neck and did not swap to the fallback weapons. They had arrows and stamina.")
- Log: on load AutoEquipBest put on sword + shield (stance 2, no rule for it - only combat's SelectBestAttack forced the bow). Last Neck: "Too tired to draw (24/90)" -> resting until 50%, but the ranged flee branch (projectile weapon, target < 10 m) ran FleeFrom - running drain every frame, regen never started (stamina 29 -> 27 over a minute), bow out, blocking with the bow; ranged stance had no melee fallback at all (melee only when no ready bow).
- AutoEquipBest: stance 2 and not engaged -> the ready bow (WeaponLogic.FindReadyBow) in hand, both hands cleared, no shield; armour as usual.
- SelectBestAttack: stance 2 (not hunting) -> RangedWantsMelee: target edge within [Combat] RangedMeleeRange 4 m, or within 8 m while MHR_BowDraw.IsResting or the melee weapon is already out (hysteresis) -> EquipBestWeapon + TryForceMeleeWeapon (+ shield) "[Ranged] X is in close - melee weapon out"; past that the bow comes back (existing branch). Flee branch skipped while resting.

### 1.5.63 - bow: stamina, arrow drop (user, 2026-10-23, log d40905e0: "Mostly better. The low stamina handling on a close enemy is not great at the moment. The boar at the end it struggled to hit and kept starting a draw with really low stamina. The arrows do have some drop as they travel so that needs to be taken into account.")
- Log: full draws in 2.4 s and hits on Necks at 15-19 m. The boar: engaged at 24% stamina (run up the hill following), "Loosed at 53% (low on stamina)" at 14.8 m, 4.3 m up - missed; then ~100 "Drawing" / "Out of stamina - letting go" pairs one frame apart at 0 stamina (each draw's drain kept regen from starting); more 50% shots at 11 m, +2.3 m up - missed low.
- Drop: CompanionProjectileAimPatch aimed (lead + 0.5 g t^2) with the full m_projectileVel; a bow arrow flies at Lerp(velMin, vel, drawVelocityCurve(draw)) (weapon + ammo, the ammo's curve). Now uses that speed from Attack.m_attackDrawPercentage when m_bowDraw.
- Stamina: MHR_BowDraw.TooTiredToDraw before starting a draw - needs drain x full draw time x (BowMinDraw close / 1 far) + 1 stamina, and for a far target (>= BowFullDrawRange) at least StaminaReserve; else "resting" (s_resting) until stamina >= [Combat] StaminaResume ("Too tired to draw" / "Got its breath back"). Out of stamina mid-draw under the minimum also sets resting. The low-stamina partial draw now only applies under BowFullDrawRange.
- Bow string: [BowDiag] for both - the companion's Huntsman bow ("attach(Clone)" under LeftHand_Attach: MeshFilter, MeshRenderer, BoxCollider, ItemStyle, MaterialManNotifier) and your Draugr Fang (MeshFilter/MeshRenderer/BoxCollider + glow particles/light): no Animator, no SkinnedMeshRenderer on either - both are static meshes, so the string can't bend on either model; the body animator had drawpercent 0.50 for you. Asked the user to compare with the same Huntsman bow.

### 1.5.62 - bow draw on the clock, clear shots (user, 2026-10-23, log b939a7ec: "Bow usage looks a bit off. The NPC shot into a hill at one point and its slow to shoot. It also looks like they draw the bow but the bow string does not change at all. Can you check the process a player uses and match that?")
- Log: "full draw in 2.4 s" but loosed after 8.4 s and 6.5 s - MHR_BowDraw.Tick added dt only on frames DoAttack ran (flag5 + IsLookingAtPoint within aiAttackMaxAngle ~10 deg), and in range the bow branch ran RandomMovementAroundPoint(20 m, run) - circling, rarely lined up. Re-draw waited m_aiAttackInterval + 0.5 s (flag2).
- Player process (Player.UpdateAttackBowDraw, FixedUpdate): while attack is held, m_attackDrawTime += fixedDeltaTime every step (turning, walking - not running: CheckRun is false while IsDrawingBow), bool m_drawAnimationState + float "drawpercent", stamina drain (half at full draw); released -> StartAttack with GetAttackDrawPercentage. The player can draw again as soon as the shot is done.
- Now: MHR_BowDraw.Maintain(h, dt) every frame from CompanionCombatAI.Update advances the draw, animation and stamina; Tick(h, target, aimed, clear) only starts the draw / marks "still wanted" and looses at full draw when aimed (aiAttackMaxAngle) and ClearShot. Let go when not wanted for 1 s, bow put away, blocking, no stamina. Combat: in range + visible + bow -> stand still (StopMoving), turn, draw - start angle 45 deg, re-draw gated only by AttackCooldown (flag3), not the AI interval; blocked line -> ChaseMove to (dist - 4 m, min 5) at a walk, still drawing ("[Bow] Something is in the way of a shot at X - moving closer"). Bird shots: draw starts within 45 deg, looses within 3.
- ClearShot: RaycastAll Default/static_solid/terrain/piece/vehicle from the eyes and from bow-hand height (pos + 1.3 up + 0.3 forward) to target.GetCenterPoint(), ignoring characters. Straight line, not the arrow's arc.
- Partial draws (user: "I dont always do a full draw of the bow. If its closer or I have less stamina I sometimes use a not full draw." / the shrinking circle): Hud.UpdateCrosshair scales m_crosshairBow Lerp(1, 0.15, draw). Attack.ProjectileAttackTriggered with m_bowDraw: spread = Lerp(accuracyMin, accuracy, sqrt(draw)) (weapon + ammo), skill/damage factor *= draw (ModifyDamage + push force), speed = Lerp(velMin, vel, drawVelocityCurve(draw)). MHR_BowDraw.ReleaseAt: low stamina (< [Combat] StaminaReserve share) -> BowMinDraw; target within 8 m closing > 1 m/s and reaching it (dist - 2 m) before the rest of the draw -> BowMinDraw; at/over [Combat] BowFullDrawRange 15 m -> full; closer -> the draw whose spread angle fits atan(target radius (min 0.3) / dist), x jitter 0.9-1.1, clamped to [BowMinDraw 0.5, 1]. Out of stamina mid-draw at >= BowMinDraw -> loose (LooseNow) instead of dropping the arrow. Log: "[Bow] Loosed at N% draw (close, 7 m | low on stamina | it's rushing in, 6 m | full draw | out of stamina) after n s".
- Bow string: nothing in assembly_valheim (or the other game assemblies) touches the string - "drawpercent" is only set in Player.UpdateAttackBowDraw on the body animator; the bow is attached under the left hand by VisEquipment.AttachItem ("attach" child, no animator rebind). So the string is in the bow model / animation assets. Added [BowDiag]: once for a companion's draw and once for the player's (drawpercent > 0.5) - bow model name, parent, components, animators (controller, params), skinned meshes (bones, root), body animator cull mode and drawpercent. Compare the two lines in the next log.

### 1.5.61 - bow draw, player-like chopping (user, 2026-10-23: "Lets update the chopping and bow draw. The only time I use a power chop is when taking out a stump as it swings down rather than across. Occasionally I will use it if a log is below me and I cannot hit the log with a regular swing.")
- New AI/MHR_BowDraw (static, per humanoid): mirrors Player.UpdateAttackBowDraw - Attack.StartDraw (ammo check + equip ammo), m_holdStartEffect, m_attackDrawTime += dt (ReflectionHelper Get/TrySetAttackDrawTime), m_drawAnimationState bool + "drawpercent" on ZSyncAnimation, GetDrawStaminaDrain * dt (half at full draw; no stamina -> let go). At GetAttackDrawPercentage() >= 1 -> StartAttack (the game reads the draw % itself: full-draw spread, speed, skill factor), then reset. Cancel on no stamina; Maintain (CompanionCombatAI.Update) lets go of a draw not ticked for 0.5 s (aim lost, target gone). Log: "[Bow] Drawing X - full draw in n s", "[Bow] Loosed at full draw after n s". CompanionCombatAI.DoAttack and HuntController's bird shot call Tick every frame they're aimed and ready (the old m_bowDraw=false trick is gone). Not changed: CanUseAttack's m_aiAttackInterval still gates the start of a draw.
- HarvestController: swings chain like combat - after StartAttack a 0.4 s guard (InAttack is false for a frame), then each frame: mid-swing it waits until Attack.CanStartChainAttack and starts the next (CompanionChainPatch now also asks HarvestController.WantsChain), so the weapon's combo runs with its last swing x2. [Advanced] GatheringSwingInterval 2.5 removed. Stamina: stops when a swing would leave less than [Gathering] RestStamina 0.15 of max ("Catching its breath"), resumes at ResumeStamina 0.6 ("Got its breath back"). Power chop (secondary) only on Stump targets and TreeLog whose collider top is under feet + [Gathering] PowerChopLogBelow 0.3; mining never uses it. Power chops don't chain.

### 1.5.60 - panels above the HUD, more strings translatable (user, 2026-10-23: reported by someone working on a fork of the mod - the F8 click strip and hard-coded strings)
- Game canvas orders (from a player's [UiDiag] log in that fork; prefab values, not in the decompiled code): EnemyHud 200, HUD 400 (override), inventory 600, HudMessage 1000, text input 1100, Menu (pause) 1700, UnifiedPopup 1900, LongPressRadial 2000, console 5000. Ours were 28-31 - under the HUD. HUD/hudroot/crosshair/HoverName (350x150 TMP in the screen centre, faded with SetAlpha but still a raycast target) took the clicks of a strip of F8 controls and drew over the wheel's centre labels.
- New UI/ModUiLayers.cs: every mod canvas order from one place, Base 1050 (+0 FarmZonePlacer, +1 HomeZonePanel, +2 RadialMenu, +3 StarterCompanionPanel, +4 FarmZoneCropPicker, +5 ConfigPanel - same relative order as before). User: our panels never above the pause menu - 1050 is above the HUD and centre messages, below text input (1100) and the pause menu (1700); the despawn confirm (UnifiedPopup 1900) stays on top.
- ModUiLayers.LogSceneLayers: once per session, on the first F8 or wheel open, "[UiLayers] ... canvases in the scene: name=order(override), ..." - check the game numbers in the next log.
- Naked with the Wood job on (user report, 2026-10-23): gear only goes on through AutoEquipBest (OnInventoryChanged -> deferred, SyncEquipmentToInventory); a loaded companion's saved m_equipped flags don't equip anything by themselves. HarvestController.EquipTool sets SuppressAutoEquip as soon as the job takes out its axe, and every AutoEquipBest call was skipped while it was set ("Deferred auto-equip skipped") - so if the job grabbed the tool before the first auto-equip, armour stayed off until RestoreLoadout (job ends). Same risk for Farm/Fish/Hunt/Upkeep/PileTaker, which all set it. Fix: SuppressAutoEquip now only holds back the hands - AutoEquipBest(armorOnly: SuppressAutoEquip) still puts on chest/legs/helmet/cape/utility/trinket (new EquipBestArmor). TorchOpener's reflection call passes the new argument. Log: "Deferred auto-equip firing now (armour only - a job holds the hands)". Not confirmed from a log - the reporter's log would show it.
- User test of 1.5.60 (log c6074698): [UiLayers] confirmed the game's layers (HUD 400, HudMessage 1000, TextInput 1100, TextViewer 1200, ConnectionPanel 1300, Tutorial 1400, Menu 1700, UnifiedPopup 1900, LongPressRadial 2000...). Armour-only auto-equip worked with the Wood job ("Deferred auto-equip firing now (armour only ...)", dressed while chopping). Tier buttons: the user says there are none in game (hire panel not built) - the keys are ready for when it is.
- Same log, fixed: (1) HarvestController.LateUpdate steered to a low target (stump/log) with MHR_Go("Harvest") whenever state was Attacking - also mid-fight, since Update returns early in combat but LateUpdate doesn't ("[Move|Harvest]" during the greyling fight; user: "stuck on a tree when in combat"). Now skipped while IsInCombat or loot pickup is active. (2) RestoreLoadout after a fight queued the sword (equip queue); the job then took the axe and the queue put the sword back over it -> "Wrong/missing tool" -> axe again. EquipTool now calls CompanionSetup.MHR_DropQueuedHandItems (weapons/shields/tools out of the queue). (3) EquipTool unequipped the sword then EquipItem(axe) returned FALSE three times - Humanoid.EquipItem refuses while InAttack/InDodge; EquipTool now waits for the swing to end. (4) Logs ignored: with Follow on, InWorkArea only took targets within FollowRange (10 m) of the player and skipped the rest silently; logs roll well past that, so it felled new trees next to the player. Logs/stumps now count within [Gathering] FollowLogRange 20; the Breakdown line counts "out of range".
- Same log, explained (no change yet): bow - DoAttack turns m_bowDraw off for the shot, so there's no draw: Humanoid.GetAttackDrawPercentage is 0 for companions (m_attackDrawTime only runs in Player's input code), and with m_bowDraw off Attack.ProjectileAttackTriggered uses the full-draw spread and speed - every shot is instant and fully drawn. Chopping: fixed [Advanced] GatheringSwingInterval 2.5 s between swings, single swings (no combo), power chop (secondary) every swing from the 4th on a target when stamina allows. A log rolled down a slope after the player chopped it and hit Gunter for 80 (47 after armour).

- Translation keys: hc_tier_* (CompanionTierDef.NameKey / LocalName - tier buttons, buy button, "arrived" message; DisplayName stays English for logs and m_name), hc_speech_found_1-3 (ReturnLogic greetings), ON/OFF in ConfigPanel (reuses hc_radial_on/off), hc_hover_hold, hc_tomb_hold_resurrect, hc_tomb_resurrecting (and the tombstone's "Companion" fallback -> hc_tier_companion). hc_msg_resurrected was already a key.

### 1.5.59 - flanking (user, 2026-10-17: "If the enemies are not focused on the NPC the NPC should try to get behind them if possible and take advantage of the backstab damage.")
- Backstab checked in the game (Character.RPC_Damage ~2336): the bonus (weapon m_backstabBonus, 4 by default) applies only when the victim's AI is NOT alerted, at most once per 300 s per creature, and with PassiveMobs only if it can't see the attacker. Facing plays no part. A creature fighting the player is alerted -> no backstab from behind. Told the user; flanking is still worth it - its swings go where it faces.
- Flank(dt, weapon), first thing in Spacing: target's AI target is someone else (player, another companion), no combo under way, no other attacker within reach, target not staggered, [Combat] Flank on. Angle between its facing and the companion (0 front, 180 behind): >= 110 -> behind, attack normally (return 0, "[Tactics] Behind X - attacking while it's busy with Y"); else move (MHR_Mover.Go, jog, run above StaminaResume) to the side it's on at our reach (angle < 70) then to its back - return 1. 4 s without getting there -> attack from where it is, no flanking for 6 s ("Couldn't get behind X - attacking from here").
- Not done: sneaking up on unaware enemies for the real backstab (only Hunt sneaks now) - BACKLOG if wanted.

### 1.5.58 - spacing (user, 2026-10-17: "Yes, let's build the spacing next")
- Modelled on the user's own fighting (1.5.57 notes). Tactics ends in Spacing(dt, weapon) when nothing else took over. Applies when [Combat] Spacing is on, no combo is under way, the only attacker within reach (_close) is the target, and the target's AI target is this companion (a creature fighting the player is attacked normally).
- Hover distance = the target's Danger (its MHR_MeleeReach + both radii) + [Combat] SpacingMargin 0.4. Further off -> MHR_Mover.Go to that distance at a jog, facing it (return 1). Within: hold, facing it (return 2) and _spacingHold - ReadyToSwing starts no combo while holding (a staggered target / finishing blow still go ahead). Our reach > its reach and in between -> hit it from there (return 2 without the hold).
- Opening: EnemyReadyIn(t) = MonsterAI's own gate - weapon m_lastAttackTime + m_aiAttackInterval - now, and m_minAttackInterval - GetTimeSinceLastAttack (MonsterAI.UpdateAttack ~line 461). Open when staggered, or not mid-swing and ready > time to get in (gap beyond our reach / 4 m/s + 0.35 s for the swing). Then _dipping (until closeIn + 0.6 s, or it starts a swing) -> return 0: the chase closes in and ReadyToSwing starts the combo. Needs stamina above the reserve + one swing. "[Tactics] X can't swing again for n s - going in".
- After the combo MaybeStepOut always steps back out (spacing fight). An enemy walking in and swinging: UpdateBlocking -> TryEvade, whose chance is at least [Combat] SpacingStepChance 0.6 when it's the spacing target and the step needed is <= 1.2 m (a short step makes it miss and costs no stamina); otherwise a block (parry roll) and then the usual punish on a stagger.
- "[Tactics] Keeping just out of X's reach (its reach r m) - waiting for an opening" (TLog, one line per 5 s).

### 1.5.57 - chairs further off, log fixes, the user's fighting in numbers (user, 2026-10-17, log 9232441a: "I thought we had fixed it so the NPC did not idle so close indoors? That they would look for a nearby chair or whatever to rest on rather than the floor. I fought the skeletons a few times. Then Gunter did it. The combat for the NPC does look better with those changes.")
- Chairs: it was still the BACKLOG item from 1.5.44 (never built) - "sat down on the floor" 3x indoors in this log. Follow's indoor sit now looks within max(follow spot distance + 1.5, [Follow] ChairRange 8 m) of the player (same level, in the player's sight, not in use, as before). The floor-sit line now says why: "(no chair within 8 m of you)" or "(N chair(s) within 8 m: a on another level, b in use, c out of your sight)". Idle sitting already used [Idle] Radius 8.
- Log spam: CompanionAI reported "Passive stance" to AIGateDiag every tick it was Passive, and the gate is cleared at the top of each tick -> "blocked by Passive stance" / "resumed" every tick (~200 lines until the 200-line cap). Removed - Passive doesn't pause the AI.
- [You] swing lines came 3-8 times per swing, 0.02 s apart: holding attack, Player.Update calls StartAttack every frame (m_queuedAttackTimer / m_attackHold) until the swing shows as an attack - the game itself restarts the swing like 1.5.55's companion bug. Logged once now (same combo swing within 0.4 s skipped).
- The user's fighting (bronze sword vs single skeletons, Meadows; stamina 50 early, 126 after eating): guard goes up as the skeleton starts its swing at 0.3-1.5 m; parries land 0.19-0.24 s after raising the guard (the game's window is 0.25 s); then the guard drops at once and a full combo goes into the staggered skeleton - 2/3 and 3/3 at 0.00 s, hits of 25-30 and the finisher 53-59 (stagger x2 + finisher x2). Not guarding when it swings: mostly moving away 2-4 m/s, sometimes out of reach by the time it lands. Guard held 0.5-1.5 s, longer (2 s) when standing toe to toe. Skeleton reach ~1.5-1.6 m edge to edge.
- Gunter after (max stamina 58, no food): combos 2/3 and 3/3 chained (0.00-0.12 s), a punish with the 3/3 finisher for 48.9, "Low on stamina (11 %)" -> one step back (0.6 m, then 1.5 m). The user: combat "does look better".

### 1.5.56 (cont.) - the user's own fighting style, and a log of it (user, 2026-10-17: "The idea is to either kill the enemy quickly or to step back when it goes to attack to be just out of range and not spend the stamina blocking if there is not a ton of stamina available. This is not something I always do but I do try to stay just out of range and then dip in to attack then back out. And block when needed to either parry or create an opening for an attack. I can swap to the bronze sword this time and do a few runs myself to get some of what I am doing into the logs.")
- Target style (next combat step, after the user's logs): spacing - hover just outside the enemy's reach so its swing whiffs, dip in for a combo while it recovers, back out; block mainly to parry or make an opening; with little stamina, step out of a swing rather than pay for a block; or kill quickly when it can (FinishOff). The catching-its-breath step is separate and fine as is.
- Patches/PlayerCombatLog (temporary, [Advanced] LogPlayerCombat, on): Debug "[You] ..." - your swings (Humanoid.StartAttack postfix, local player only: weapon, combo swing n/3, time since the last swing ended, nearest enemy gap edge-to-edge + its reach (SwingReach), mid-swing/staggered, stamina), "Guard up/down" (IsBlocking edges, how long it was up), "Dodge roll", "Parried/Blocked X n s after raising the guard: damage/got through" (CompanionBlockLog now also for the local player), "X swings at you from g m (its reach r) - in/out of reach, moving away/closing in/standing, guard up, mid-swing", "Took N damage", "Hit X for N (staggered - double damage)" (CompanionHitLog), and "Spacing: ..." once a second with an enemy within 5 m. Tick from CompanionsPlugin.Update.

### 1.5.56 - one step back to catch its breath, clear of walls (user, 2026-10-17: "It should just be a step back to get beyond the hit window for the melee weapon."; "Hitting nearby walls is fine. Thats what a player might do. They would try to maneuver away from the walls to a clear spot if there is one.")
- Test setup (user): Meadows, skeletons spawned with devcommands - "a good example of combat without being to hard to fight". Keep in mind reading logs: several at once at the crypt entrance by the base wall.
- Catching its breath: an attacker inside its hit window (its Danger + 0.3) -> one step (straight back if safe and not towards another attacker, else EvadeDir) to Danger + 0.4, then guard facing it (StopMoving, return 2 - blocks via UpdateBlocking); the next step no sooner than 1.5 s later (_recoverStepReady). Was: keep moving away (BestDir 1.5 m every 0.3 s) while anything was within Danger + 1. Nothing inside the window -> walk up / hold as before. BackOffTime and "nowhere to step back to" still go to cornered.
- Walls: at the end of a combo, MaybeClearOfWalls - WallInSwing: a player-built Piece (layers piece, piece_nonsolid; Piece.IsPlacedByPlayer) within its reach (MHR_MeleeReach + radius + 0.3) and inside the sweep (attack angle / 2 + 10 deg) towards the target. If so, spots around the target at the same distance (+-30, 60, 90 deg, nearest first) with SafeStep, not towards another attacker, and no piece in the swing from there -> step there (a step that doesn't stop at "out of reach"). "[Tactics] Moving to a clear spot - its swings were catching the $piece_...". None -> fights on (hitting the wall like a player would). MaybeStepOut only if it didn't move for a wall.

### 1.5.55 - less backing off, combos not restarted (user test of 1.5.54, log d973bc65: "The reset fix looks good. I think the backoff mechanic is still to much.")
- Log (read in full): crypt entrance again, 3-5 skeletons, max stamina only ~62 (no stamina food), tower shield out with 5 about; reset: "cleared the starter-companion flag" -> "First spawn in world — showing starter companion panel" -> skipped with the close button (works). Combos: 2/3 now follow at 0.00 s (chaining works), but "N on it from different sides - getting clear" fired again and again (4, 3, 5...), plus "Low on stamina (26 %)" -> "(60 %)" (the cfg still has StaminaResume 0.6).
- Bug: lines 1311-1317 - three "Swing at ... combo swing 1/3" 0.04-0.06 s apart, one stamina drain. DoAttack succeeded, but InAttack() only turns true once the attack animation is playing (a frame or two later); ReadyToSwing saw "not attacking, combo under way" and started it again (no AttackCooldown gate inside a combo), restarting swing 1 and using up the combo's count. Now ReadyToSwing waits after each swing until InAttack shows (or 0.4 s).
- Groups: Flanked / the GroupSize count use only attackers within their reach + 1.5 m (GatherClose), not everything targeting it within GroupRange; repositioning at most 2.5 s (was 4), then 6 s fighting where it stands (was 5); once they're on one side, 4 s before it repositions again.
- MaybeStepOut after a combo: below Reserve + 0.15 or 15% of the time - no longer after every combo when 2+ are on it.
- Noticed: two "[Piece] Stakewall ... took 16-21 damage (hit EnemyHit by Gunter)" - its sword sweeps hit the base's stakewall while fighting next to it (a player's swings do the same). Not changed; BACKLOG if the user wants companions to mind their own walls.

### 1.5.54 - ResetStarterFlag fix (user, 2026-10-16: "I also noticed that the reset starter flag does not seem to be working.", log 8e1d1e5d)
- Log (1.5.53, read in full; two loads of the same world, Gunter with his gear both times - the first AutoEquipBest sees only the legs before the container finishes loading, as always): "ResetStarterFlag: cleared the starter-companion flag" then at once "Found existing companion in scene — marking world key and skipping panel". CompanionManager.SpawnStarterCompanion's existing-companion check (meant for worlds from before the flag) set the flag straight back. StarterFix.ResetIfRequested now returns true when it cleared the flag, and then that check is skipped, so the starter panel shows.

### 1.5.54 - smoother melee (user test of 1.5.53, log 4b8d4066: "Looks mostly like its working. Its not terribly smooth yet. The NPC only looks to step backward rather than to a side. There seem to be a fair number of times when the NPC seems to pause when attacking. Maybe that the stamina controls.")
- Log (read in full): crypt entrance, 4-5 skeletons at once, bronze sword, tower shield out (alertedNearby 5) and back to the parry shield later; 33 [Block] lines, all 0-1 through; finishes and a punish ("is staggered - going in"); "2-hit combo, not 3 - saving stamina" lines; "Low on stamina (24 %)" -> "Got its breath back (60 %)" twice (the user's cfg still has StaminaResume 0.6); won at 46% hp. Steps: "back" 5x, "back and aside" 2x, never "aside".
- Combos: most "N-hit combo" lines were followed by only "combo swing 1/3"; a few 2/3 (0.00-0.12 s after the last - the chain works when it happens) and one 3/3. Causes: (1) every swing waited for facing within m_aiAttackMaxAngle (5 deg for the sword) - the target shifting a little missed the chain moment; (2) ReadyToSwing ended the combo whenever anything swung at it (SwingAtUs), and with 4-5 skeletons around that was almost always. Fixes: a wide sweep (m_attackAngle >= 40) may swing within min(30, angle x 0.3) deg (sword 90 -> 27) while tactics are on (stabs keep the tight limit); a combo needs an Opening only to start, then carries through; UpdateBlocking doesn't raise the guard mid-combo (_burstLeft > 0).
- Pauses: "Too close to swing (0.2 m) - stepping back" 4x, yet the sword hit at 0.2-0.3 m - the step back now only for narrow attacks (m_attackAngle < 40). [Combat] ComboPause default 0.5 s (was 1; existing configs keep theirs).
- Steps: EvadeDir (TryEvade, MaybeStepOut) - straight back 25%, back-and-aside (45 deg) 35%, aside (90 deg) 40%, falling back through the others in that order; the side away from the other attackers first (else towards the owner, else random); never towards another attacker within 4 m; SafeStep as before. A sideways step rarely gets out of reach by distance, so it runs to its time cap (dist/3 + 0.3 s).

### 1.5.53 - bug report: companions bigger with the Combat world modifier (user, 2026-10-16: "Turning the world modifier: combat to max makes the companions larger than the player. Their heads clip to the helmet when bigger." + screenshot)
- Game code: Character.Awake scales every non-player by Game.m_enemySpeedSize (GlobalKeys.EnemySpeedSize, set by the Combat modifier; outdoors only) and by 1 + m_worldLevel x m_worldLevelEnemyMoveSpeedMultiplier; Character.UpdateWalking multiplies a non-player's speed by the same two. Companions (built from the Player prefab, Player component removed) aren't players, so they grew and sped up like enemies.
- Fix: Patches/CompanionWorldModifierPatch - a transpiler on Character.Awake and Character.UpdateWalking replacing the ldsfld of Game.m_enemySpeedSize / Game.m_worldLevel with SpeedSize(this) / WorldLevel(this): 1 / 0 for a companion (GetBaseAI() is CompanionAI - m_baseAI is set before the scaling), the game's value for everyone else. Warns in the log if a game update removes those reads.
- Not changed, for the combat review (BACKLOG): the same modifier's damage rates treat companions as enemies - Character.RPC_Damage scales a hit from any non-player by EnemyDamage (and the player-count scale), and Character.ApplyDamage scales damage to any non-player by PlayerDamage. So a companion takes EnemyDamage x PlayerDamage from monsters (a player takes EnemyDamage x its own damage-taken rate), and a companion's hits on monsters get EnemyDamage x PlayerDamage too. World level also gives non-players base armour (m_worldLevelEnemyBaseAC) and more max health (our GetMaxHealth postfix replaces that for companions).

### 1.5.53 - combat part 2 fixes (user test of 1.5.52, log f4f75240: "It looks like the NPC is doing mostly power attacks? Also they brought out the tower shield for a moment then put it away. The step back looks like they are moving back a lot and not re-engaging afte.")
- Log (read in full; game 1.0.20, a crypt/skeleton fight in the Black Forest, bronze sword + wood shield, tower shield in the pack, max stamina ~85): combos 2-3 hits (bronze sword 3 chain levels), blocks 3-4 dmg 0 through, 4 parries out of 9 blocks (bound to vary), "Too close ... stepping back" now moving; "4 on it from different sides - getting clear"; steps "back and aside (1.7 m)", "back (2.2 m)", "back (0.8 m)" then "Low on stamina (30 %)" (a 14.6 block at 45) and 15 lines later "Got its breath back (60 %)"; afterwards "Stepping back out of ... reach after its combo" after most combos. Fight won, ~70% hp.
- Power attacks: DoAttack only ever calls StartAttack(target, false) - the primary attack, never the secondary (power) one. What looks like power attacks are the later swings of the weapon's own combo (the 3rd is the finisher, m_lastChainDamageMultiplier x2) - before 1.5.52 it only ever did the first. The [Attack] swing line now says "combo swing N/levels (the finisher ...)" and the seconds since the last swing ended (Humanoid.m_currentAttack -> Attack.m_currentAttackCainLevel by reflection), to confirm.
- Tower shield: ShieldLogic swapped to the tower shield (alertedNearby=4); 1.5 s later CompanionSetup.AutoEquipBest (OnInventoryChanged - durability/stack changes fire it) queued the parry shield back. The shield swap in AutoEquipBest now skips while the combat AI is engaged, like the weapon swap already did.
- Steps too far: the step ran for a set time (dist/6 + 0.25 s at a run) and Danger used the attacker's m_aiAttackRange (where the AI starts a swing). Now: Danger/SwingAtUs use MHR_MeleeReach (min(aiAttackRange, attackRange + 0.5)), SwingAtUs margin +1 m (was +1.5); a step ends as soon as it's Danger + 0.3 m away (time cap dist/3 + 0.3 s); steps jog, running only when > 1.5 m to go. MaybeStepOut after a combo only below Reserve + 0.15, in a group, or 20% (was below StaminaResume - nearly every combo - or 30%).
- [Combat] StaminaResume default 0.5 (was 0.6) - existing configs keep 0.6.
- Punish on a stagger (user: "Will the NPC go back to attacking when stamina hits 30% or will it wait ..." - answered: it waits for StaminaResume, blocking/stepping, unless cornered; then "If the NPC parries is should perform a combo if possible against the enemy." and "It should at least do a single hit."): Punish(w) - target IsStaggering (a parry stagger or any other) -> ReadyToSwing ignores catching its breath, the combo pause and a pending combo; Tactics returns 0 (no backing off / repositioning, the chase closes in). The first swing needs only the stamina for that swing; BurstSize uses the half-reserve floor (min 1), and later swings need cost + half the reserve. One combo per stagger (_punishUsed until the stagger ends). "[Tactics] X is staggered - going in with a combo (catching its breath can wait)".
- Finishing off (user: "the NPC should push their stamina low if it means killing an enemy that then gives them some breathing room ... if the enemy is low health and it takes 1-2 more hits ... use up their stamina so they can do a larger recharge. Then they can re-join the fight by walking upto another enemy."): TrackDamage (each Tactics tick) measures the target's health drop within 1 s of one of its own hits (NoteHit -> NoteOwnHit), halved if the target was staggered (Character.RPC_Damage doubles damage to a staggered non-player). HitsToKill = ceil(hp / (perHit x 0.9, x2 while staggered)). FinishOff: <= [Combat] FinishHits 2, stamina for all of them, no other attacker within its Danger + 2 m (BreathingRoomAfter), started on an Opening -> ReadyToSwing ignores catching its breath / the pause / the reserve, BurstSize >= the hits needed with no floor; carried through while stamina >= one swing. "[Tactics] X is nearly dead (hp, about N hit(s)) - spending its stamina to finish it". No measured hit yet on that target -> no finishing.
- Re-joining: while catching its breath with nothing within reach + 1 m, it jogs (MHR_Mover, SpeedJog - jogging doesn't use stamina) to just outside its target's reach (Danger + 2) instead of standing still, and goes in at StaminaResume. ChaseMove doesn't run below StaminaResume + 0.15 while tactics are on.
- Combo length (user: "So basically all melee weapons have a 3 hit combo. Where the last hit does more damage. You will have to double check how the game does this. So we probably only need the one setting for combo."): checked - Attack.m_attackChainLevels, the next level wraps to 0 after the last (Attack.Start ~line 540), the last level's hits get m_lastChainDamageMultiplier (2) and push x1.2; the chain only continues if the next swing starts <= 0.2 s after the last ended (or is queued). BurstSize = the weapon's chain levels capped at ComboHits (the one setting); the two-handed cap is gone (heavier swings cost more stamina, which shortens combos anyway); one short only 1 in 5 (was every other), never when finishing or punishing.
- Chaining like a player: Player.HaveQueuedChain lets the next swing start mid-swing once the animation's Chain event fired (Attack.CanStartChainAttack); Humanoid.HaveQueuedChain is false, so companions waited for each swing to end. Patches/CompanionChainPatch (Humanoid.HaveQueuedChain postfix, non-players only since Player overrides without calling base): true for a companion mid-combo (WantsChain = tactics on and _burstLeft > 0) when its current attack CanStartChainAttack. ReadyToSwing allows a swing while InAttack only in that case; the usual checks (openings, stamina) still apply.
- Single swings at the end of the fight (user: "It had just looked like the NPC was taking 1 swing near the end of the log rather than multiple swings."): stamina ~35/85, reserve 25.5, swing 7.9 -> BurstSize 1 every time. Now a combo starts above the reserve (ReadyToSwing with no burst under way) and may dip to half the reserve (BurstSize and mid-combo ReadyToSwing), then _recovering takes over. "[Tactics] N-hit combo, not M - saving stamina to block (s/max)" when cut short.

### Game patch 1.0.20 (2026-10-16, user: "vlaheim had a small patch today. The mod loaded without issue")
- New Managed files from the user. Decompiled the old and new assembly_valheim.dll the same way (ilspycmd 9.1) and diffed: the patch is cheat-flag handling (ZDO.IsCheated/SetCheated replace ZDOVars.s_cheated reads/writes, ItemDrop.ResetVariantIfInvalid -> EnsureValid, item data version 110, network version 41), pseudo-random drops keyed by hash and saved with the player (CharacterDrop.s_pseudoCounter), and a per-player seed for crafting upgrade chances (Player.m_itemUpgradeSeeds, InventoryGui). Combat, AI, movement, Attack, Humanoid, BaseAI, the station RPCs (Fermenter/CookingStation RPC_AddItem, Smelter) are unchanged. The mod uses none of the changed members; it compiles against the new files with 0 warnings / 0 errors. The session's reference copy of the game source (scratchpad full_new) is now 1.0.20.

### 1.5.52 - combat review, part 2: fighting like a player (user, 2026-10-15: "Lets build part 2.")
- New AI/CompanionCombatTactics.cs (CompanionCombatAI is now partial). On for melee weapons ([Combat] FightLikePlayer, default on) unless stalking unaware prey. _tacticsOn set before UpdateBlocking (current weapon) and again after SelectBestAttack; turning off -> ResetTactics (also in Disengage).
- Combos: melee swing gate is ReadyToSwing instead of m_aiAttackInterval (2 s default on player weapons) + AttackCooldown (0.225 s - longer than the game's 0.2 s chain window in Attack.Start, so even a parry counter never comboed). NoteTacticSwing after each DoAttack: a burst of BurstSize = the attack's m_attackChainLevels (2 if none), max 2 for two-handed, capped at [Combat] ComboHits 3, randomly one less, and shrunk until it doesn't spend into the reserve; the next swing starts the moment InAttack ends. Burst over -> _burstPending; when the last swing ends, pause [Combat] ComboPause 1 s x 0.6-1.4, and MaybeStepOut (stamina < Resume, a group, or 30%): step back to target reach + 0.7 if that's <= 2.5 m.
- Openings (Opening): target staggering, no SwingAtUs, or the watched swing has landed (_swingLandedAt >= _swingStartedAt; NoteIncoming from CompanionAI.OnDamaged and CompanionBlockLog). UpdateBlocking lowers the guard once the watched swing has landed so it can hit back. A burst ends when there's no opening or the stamina floor is reached. _parryAttackCount (the old 2 free counters) only without tactics.
- Stamina: below [Combat] StaminaReserve 0.3 -> _recovering: no new swings; an attacker within its Danger (weapon m_aiAttackRange + both radii) + 1 m -> move away (BestDir 1.5 m, recomputed every 0.3 s, jog), else hold still facing the nearest. Until [Combat] StaminaResume 0.6. After [Combat] BackOffTime 8 s still pressed, or nowhere safe to go -> _cornered 4 s: fights on openings down to 0 stamina, then tries backing off again.
- Group: attackers = enemies within [Combat] GroupRange 6 m that are its target or targeting it. [Combat] GroupSize 2+ and Flanked (two more than [Combat] FlankAngle 100 deg apart) -> move by BestDir 2 m (run if stamina > Resume) until they're on one side; 4 s without managing it -> fight where it stands for 5 s.
- BestDir: 12 directions, SafeStep(pos, dir, dist) must pass; score = away from each attacker (weighted by closeness, x2 for the one it's stepping from) + 0.5 towards the owner within 2-30 m + a little random.
- MHR_Mover.SafeStep (new, public): no wall at 0.5 m height along the way, ground every 0.5 m within 1 m below / 0.8 m above, water depth <= 0.4, no FireArea.
- Steps (TryEvade, once per swing at the reaction moment, before raising the guard): [Combat] StepAwayChance 0.3 (x2 below the reserve); needed = its Danger + 0.6 - distance, only if <= 2.5 m; run if stamina allows. Reaction time per swing = [Advanced] BlockReactionTime x 0.75-1.5.
- Facing while moving: BaseAI.MoveTowards with m_smoothMovement (on for companions) sets moveDir = forward x alignment, so it only walks the way it faces; Character.AlwaysRotateCamera is true for non-players, so they turn to the look direction. Tactics' Move sets Character.SetMoveDir/SetRun directly and LookAtPoint at the enemy -> backs off facing it. The old "too close - stepping back" used MoveTowards(away) + LookAtPoint(target) and never moved (alignment 0); fixed the same way.
- Flee: FleeToOwner first - owner 4-60 m away and more than 60 deg from the enemy's direction -> MHR_Mover.Go to the owner (SpeedRun), else the old FleeFrom.
- The Ranged-stance circling and "not ready - random movement" are skipped while tactics are on.
- Debug "[Tactics] ..." lines (5 s per repeated line): combos, low stamina / breath back, groups, steps, cornered, running to the owner.
- Not done (backlog): dodge roll, secondary attacks, per-creature tactics from the user's reading list, the per-weapon height window, item 8 leftovers.

### 1.5.51 - combat review, part 1: blocking (user, 2026-10-15: "I agree with doing 1-2.")
- Review of the combat code (CompanionCombatAI, WeaponLogic, ShieldLogic, CombatPatches) shown to the user; findings: (1) every block a parry, (2) blocks any swing within 8 m, (3) basic flee (from one target, not towards the player, no edge/water checks, no eating), (4) no dodge or secondary attacks, (5) no edge checks in combat movement, (6) nearest target rather than most important, (7) one height window for every weapon, (8) leftovers (a 9999 m leash against the player that never triggers, a 3 s log line built and never printed, unused circle constants).
- (1) Checked in Humanoid.BlockAttack: parry = blocker m_timedBlockBonus > 1 and m_blockTimer in [0, 0.25). UpdateBlocking set m_blockTimer = 0 every frame of any enemy swing, so every block was a parry - no cooldown anywhere (user: "I thought the blocking was on a cooldown. I do not see the NPC get a perfect block every time." - many hits aren't blocked at all: the 0.2 s reaction delay, hits landing early in a swing, the companion mid-attack or staggered, facing). Now one roll per swing (SwingAtUs target changes or the swing ends): [Combat] ParryChance 0.3 at Blocking 0 -> ParryChanceMax 0.7 at Blocking 100 (lerp on GetSkillFactor). Parry roll: timer 0 at the raise and held at 0 through the swing (as before); plain block: timer 0.3 at the raise, then the game counts it up. Reaction delay is now [Advanced] BlockReactionTime 0.2.
- (2) SwingAtUs: enemy in attack within 8 m whose edge-to-edge gap is within its weapon's m_aiAttackRange (2 for non-Humanoids) + 1.5, and either targeting this companion or facing it within 60 deg; nearest wins. Swings at others further off no longer turn it away from its own fight.
- Patches/CompanionBlockLog (temporary, like [Attack]): Humanoid.BlockAttack postfix for companions - Debug "[Block] Parried|Blocked X with <shield>: N damage, M got through"; UpdateBlocking logs "[Block] Raising its guard against X - timed for a parry|a plain block".
- User notes for the tempo work (next): "the counter attacks are slow. The attacks in general are slow. Most of the time I will do 2-3 quick swings with a fast weapon, then block again. Less swings with a slower weapon. All the time while moving a repositioning to take advantage of the area." Cause found: melee waits for the weapon's m_aiAttackInterval between attacks - player weapons leave it at the default 2 s (ItemDrop.SharedData) - so it never chains a combo (Attack.Start only continues the chain when the last attack ended <= 0.2 s ago, m_attackChainLevels) and never gets the last-hit bonus (m_lastChainDamageMultiplier). The Ranged stance's circling also pauses the fight every 3-4 s. Plan: swing in bursts - chain the weapon's combo (2-3 hits, fewer for slow weapons, stamina allowing), then guard / step out and reposition.
- User notes for group fights (2026-10-15): "when blocking multiple enemies that the NPC should focus on blocking and getting clear of the group and attack when there is an opening. Not just block attack one opponent and take hits from the others at the same time. Often times its better to just take a step back or to the side to avoid an attack. We also dont want to make the NPC perfect like some super robot." Proposed plan (part 2, with the tempo work): attack in combo bursts that stop when another enemy starts a swing; with 2+ attackers close, get clear first (move so they're all on one side, towards the player / open ground, through MHR_Mover so edges are checked) and only attack on an opening (target recovering from a swing or staggered, nobody else mid-swing in reach); sometimes step back or aside out of a swing's reach instead of blocking (not when cornered or out of stamina); imperfect on purpose - varied reaction time, chance-based choices, slower and sloppier when tired, all configurable. Dodge (corrected): Player.Dodge/UpdateDodge live in Player (removed from our prefab), but the companion is built from the Player prefab, so the animator has the "dodge" trigger and Character applies root motion - a roll is possible: rotate, m_zanim.SetTrigger("dodge"), stamina, and patch Character.InDodge/IsDodgeInvincible for companions while the dodge anim tag is on (Character.RPC_Damage skips m_dodgeable hits when IsDodgeInvincible). Not planned yet (user: "Not sure we need to add it").
- Stamina (user: "It has stamina just like a player so it should prioritize staying alive and reducing damage, then attacking. Trying to stay out of hit range to recharge stamina when possible."): replaces "slower and sloppier when tired". Keep a reserve for blocking; at or below it no new attacks - back out of reach so regen starts ([Stamina] RegenDelay after the last use), block only if it can't get clear. With 0 stamina a block stops nothing (Humanoid.BlockAttack needs HaveStamina) and attacks don't start (Attack.Start).
- Reading for the later combat work (user): https://valheim.fandom.com/wiki/Aggressive_creature_strategies , https://valheim.fandom.com/wiki/Boss_strategies#Minibosses , https://valheim.fandom.com/wiki/Passive_creature_strategies , https://valheim.wiki.fextralife.com/Valheim_Creatures , https://physgun.com/tools/valheim/weapons/ , https://steamcommunity.com/sharedfiles/filedetails/?id=2424865980 , https://requnix.com/complete-valheim-guide/ (general).

### 1.5.43 - speech overhaul phase 3 (user, 2026-10-14: "Lets do phase 3 first."; "Smelting should also mention if there is no wood to add to the kiln. Repair and Restock, will this also mention what the NPC is doing? Like fueling the fires, organizing the chests, etc?")
- CompanionStatus: Item(label, key, args) + WhyRotate(c, items) - one reason per call in turn (per-companion counter), the jobs call it once per scan. Doing(c, key, args) / DoingAs(label...) - what it's doing now (fresh 90 s); Doing clears the why and Why clears the doing. Describe: idle -> "<Job> - <why>"; busy -> Farm's own DoingText, else "<label or Job> - <doing>". Cook moved onto WhyRotate.
- Gather (MHR_GatherRules.Scan/AllowTarget): Per.AtLimit item -> (stock, limit) -> hc_why_gather_limit "Wood 512/500 - at the limit."; HarvestController.LastTierSkips/LastTierName -> hc_why_gather_tier "Copper deposit needs a better pickaxe." (user: "3 need a better tool" read like a typo); Per.NearBaseRejects -> hc_why_gather_near_base (BaseProtectRadius). Doing on every target pick: chopping/mining/picking up/picking + MHR_Items.NiceName (Hoverable name, else the object name tidied: "Beech1(Clone)" -> "Beech").
- Hunt: FindPrey fills _whyItems - each meat at target (hc_why_hunt_at_target with HuntCatalog.Target), else out of reach / no game within Range. Doing "hunting X" (prey and birds).
- Fish: PickTarget fills _whyItems - each fish at target, no bait (BaitNames), no bank; else no fish within Radius. WhyRotate runs after Say() (Say records its own key). Doing "fishing for X".
- Smelt: TryPlanRefill/TryPlanFuelRefill -> AddWhy: no ore for <smelter>, no wood for the kiln (m_maxFuel == 0), no <fuel> for <smelter>, a pile that needs a hammer; else monitoring / nothing to smelt. Doing at each plan: loading the X with Y, fuelling the X, emptying the X, taking down the pile.
- Upkeep: Enter(step) -> ShowDoing (only while the companion's job is Repair and Restock - its idle chores for other jobs mustn't replace their reasons): repairing/fuelling/sorting/stacking up/putting back/piling up/taking down/collecting honey/heading home. Idle reasons: _whyNoFuel (fuel names), _whyNoStation (count), else everything's in order.
- Cook: ShowDoing per task (mead base craft, fill, tap labelled "Mead"); FoodTask: wanted food short of an ingredient -> FoodShort (hc_why_mead_short with the food's name), replaces the generic no-food line.
- Farm: _farmWhy per scan - no <seed> in chests for a zone, zone full, PickSeed null (SeedMinimum), no seeds (still spoken), no zones; else waiting.
- Action lines: ToolKeeper StartTrip fetch/return -> "Fetching an axe." / "Putting a cultivator back." (Event, topic per kind); CompanionRest auto-bed -> "Off to bed.".
- Content: speech.json v3 (old file kept as speech.v<version>.json): travelling Follow lines tagged [away], [home] follow lines, a few more Hunt/Fish/Smelt/Upkeep lines.
- Lost announcements: built (roster check a minute after load and every 10 min) and taken out again (user: "I am not sure we need the 'Lost' companion notification. The player can just check the shrine. I think having the death notification is nice though.").
- Test build 1.5.43.

### 1.5.47 - from the 1.5.46 test (log 929a8763; user: "Right now the settings show a toggle to respawn at the stones. We should as one to force spawn at the player."; "Went through the portal and the npc says getting its gear back and stood there for a while. They were still set to passive on the wheel from before they died."; "The NPC is jumping a lot in and out of the water in the swap rather than wading through the water. They should be jumping out of the water when it gets them to dry land."; "The NPC mostly avoided the hearth till the very end. Then they jumped into it again.")
- [Death] RespawnAtPlayer (false): MHR_ResurrectCore auto-respawn -> next to the local player (FindSpawnPosition 4 m) even with a bed; message "is back - next to you".
- Water (MHR_Mover.TryWaterJump): 17 water jumps in the log in the swamp. The clear-the-water jump now needs dry land where it lands and 1-2 m beyond (depth < 0.05 at landAt, +1, +2 - no tufts with water behind); the "as far across as it can" jump only when the water ahead would mean swimming. Shallow water: wade.
- Hearth: the last entry - route had the bypass corners ("going round the fire ... via (-267.2, 40.5, -311.0)"), Gunter at (-268.7, 40.0, -311.0) then "standing in a fire at (-269.8, 40.9, -310.2)". The per-step check (1.4.88 jump-over-fire) still used InFire at floor height: now FireArea at body height, and only fires with a collider under 0.9 m half-size are jumped (a hearth isn't); FireExit uses NearFire. AvoidFire logs when there's no clear way round.
- Recovery: the second run worked as meant (waited while a skeleton was after it, then went at 26 m and collected). The first: back at the bed, the player went through the portal and it came along, then spent a while below the portal ledge ("7.0m below where it needs to be and no ladder found", ladder, water jumps) before "Found its own tombstone 22m away" - movement, not the recovery. It was Passive on the wheel because it died Passive (it had been set Passive with the new key) - RecoverFirst returns to that.
- Test build 1.5.47.

### 1.5.46 - from the 1.5.45 test (log 31ec683a; user: "I can bind a key now. The NPC still tried to jump in the hearth and started damage being caused to it. Took the NPC a while to notice the tombstone and go to it the first time they died. Ran off to get his gear during a later attempt after using the tombstone to resurrect the NPC. Got most of the way there then a mob was around. Would have been better to finish getting the gear then working back to me. It looked like they were closer to the gear than to me." + "Something is show a 0.0 fps in the top left of the screen.")
- Hearth damage found: [Piece] "Hearth took 10.0 damage (hit Self (no attacker))" with Gunter 1.2-1.5 m away at y 40.8 (on the hearth stone). HitType.Self = Aoe.m_damageSelf (decompiled Aoe.cs): the hearth's fire Aoe damages its own piece every time it burns a character standing in it - vanilla, a player does the same. So: keep it out of the flames. AvoidFire sampled 0.3 m above the floor (40.3) and passed under the fire's area; it stepped up onto the stone into it. MHR_Mover.FireArea(p, r) checks at 0.3/0.9/1.5 m; AvoidFire uses FireArea(p, 0.5); bypass corners and HopLine use NearFire (same heights).
- Recovery: first death - came back at its bed, the player portalled to the death site, it followed, and only "Found its own tombstone 6m away" after a ladder climb and a fall: the scan runs every TombstoneScanInterval; while recovering now every 1 s. Later run: "Found its own tombstone 59m away" -> "Waiting before going for its tombstone: skeleton is after it" with the gear nearer than the player. SafeToGo(committed): on its way (pending tombstone) and nearer the tombstone than the owner, a mob after it only turns it back if the mob is nearer the tombstone than it is (in the way) or within 2 m; mobs busy with others are ignored unless within 3 m of the tombstone.
- "0.0 fps" top left: not ours (no fps text in the mod). The game's own connection panel (ConnectPanel, toggled with F2 - KeyCode 283 - shows world, server and fps "0.0" format) matches; F2 hides it. Also in the log: one "GUI Error: You are pushing more GUIClips than you are popping" (IMGUI, another mod's OnGUI; ours only reads key events).
- Test build 1.5.46.

### 1.5.45 - key binding in the F8 panel (user test of 1.5.44, log 2b4b2635: "Cant bind a key" / "F8 panel, clicking it does nothing. It does not prompt me to hit a key to bind it or anything else. In the F1 panel the screen is greyed out like its waiting for me to click a key. None of the keys do anything. Esc got me out of the menu.")
- No errors in the log; nothing of ours blocks Unity Input (our TakeInput patches only stop the player moving). The F8 key button only started listening from Button.onClick, which evidently never fired; F1 (Configuration Manager) didn't see keys either - so don't rely on the EventSystem click or on Input alone. KeyBindListener now: starts on a left click inside its own rect (RectTransformUtility, checked directly in Update) as well as onClick; reads the key from Input.GetKeyDown and from OnGUI KeyDown events (needs the UnityEngine.IMGUIModule reference - added to the csproj); never binds mouse buttons; logs "[Config] Waiting for a key for X" / "[Config] X = P" / "cancelled". KeyBindListener.Capturing stops ConfigPanel.Update (Esc cancels the binding instead of closing the panel); Hide() resets it. If F8 still shows nothing, the log will say whether the click reached it.
- Workaround meanwhile: set PassiveKey/MeleeKey... in BepInEx/config/Maddhattr.OfflineCompanionsReborn.cfg.
- Test build 1.5.45.

### 1.5.44 - issue #17 (revive stance, stance keys) + issue #16 (where it came back) + hearth fire on routes (user, 2026-10-15: "Lets start the 1.5.44 build")
- BUILT: MHR_Recover (Companion/MHR_Recover.cs). CompanionTombstoneResurrect -> OnRevived(zdo, diedStance, autoPickup): Keep = nothing; Passive = stance 1; RecoverFirst = stance 1 + HC_Recovering, HC_RecoverStance, HC_RecoverPickup, AutoPickup off. CompanionAI.UpdateTombstoneRecovery: no range limit while recovering; before each step SafeToGo (rules below) else RecoverWait = go to the owner and wait (nav timeout held). CompanionAI.OnDamaged / CompanionCombatAI.Engage / UpdateCombat: Passive + recovering still fights when attacked with a weapon in hand (MayDefend: its attacker targets it; MayDefendHit on the hit itself). MHR_Recover.Tick (from MHR_ToolKeeper.TickOne, 1 s): own loaded tombstone with items -> status "Getting its gear back" / "... waiting for it to be safe" (+ one "Too many of them" line); none: TombstoneIdHash set and unarmed -> "Its gear is out of reach", after 60 s end staying Passive ("My gear's out of reach..."); else wait for the equip queue (CompanionSetup.IsEquipping) + 1.5 s, CompanionFood.EatNow, restore stance (if armed) + Pickup, "Got my gear back.". CompanionSetup.SetCombatStance -> MHR_Recover.Cancel (the wheel and the stance keys: your choice ends recovery, Pickup restored). Status: CompanionStatus.Describe shows MHR_Recover.StatusText first after combat. Settings [Death] RespawnStance (RecoverFirst/Passive/Keep), RecoverThreatRange 15, RecoverMaxMobs 3. AutoEquipBest already queues weapon and shield before armor.
- BUILT: MHR_StanceKeys (UI), called from CompanionInteract.HandleDirectCommand after the UI checks: [Controls] PassiveKey/BalancedKey/RangedKey/MeleeKey (None). Hovered own companion -> that one; else own, Command on, within RecallRange. TopLeft "Gunter, Saber: Passive" ("No companion in range for X").
- BUILT (#16): MHR_ResurrectCore message: auto-respawn -> hc_msg_back_bed / back_spawn ("Saber is back - at its bed (Meadows, 850 m from you)") / back_here; a tombstone revive keeps "has been resurrected!". MHR_Roster.GetStatuses fills s_where (loaded companion position, or the ZDO position for unloaded alive ones); Describe Alive -> "(Meadows, 850 m from you)" (WhereText).
- BUILT (hearth): MHR_Mover.Plan -> AvoidFire: each leg sampled every 0.4 m with EffectArea Burning; at the first sample in a fire insert two corners round it (collider bounds + 0.8 m, the side with ground, out of the flames, in sight), unless the goal is in the fire; HopLine refuses a straight line through a fire. Idle spots (IdleController) and follow spots (MHR_Follow) use NearFire(p, 1.2 m) instead of InFire. Patches/MHR_PieceDamageLog: Debug "[Piece] X took N damage (hit type / attacker / fire, chop, blunt, or wear) - Gunter is d m away" for pieces within 5 m of a companion (per piece every 2 s).
- BUILT: FishController logs the stamina food it carries once per fishing session ("Stamina food for reeling: Honey x5 (35 stamina)" / "No stamina food in the pack").
- Test build 1.5.44. Plan notes below.
- From the 1.5.43 test (log 5fc30b76, no errors; user: "It looks really good. The status message are really helpful."; "I had to repair the hearth again. Somehow when the NPC jumps onto it and is stuck for a moment it does damage to the hearth but not the NPC."): an idle walk went to (-269.4, 40.0, -310.3), right next to the hearth (fire ~(-269.8, 40.5, -308.6)); Gunter took 10 fire damage a tick, cut to 0.7 by armor (so it does hurt him, barely). What damages the hearth isn't in our code (no companion attacks on pieces outside commands; the game's Fire/cinder damage only comes from Ashlands cinders) - add a Debug patch on WearNTear.RPC_Damage that logs hit type, attacker and damage for pieces within 5 m of a companion, to find it. The route/idle fire fix (sample legs with InFire, no idle goals within a fire's reach) should keep it off the hearth anyway.
- Stamina food for fishing (user: "Did the NPC grab high stamina food automatically for fishing?"): ToolKeeper already fetches K_FOOD for the Fish job (1.4.111, foods with stamina >= health, 5 at a time) - but only when the pack has none, and CompanionFood only eats when a food slot is empty. The log had no food trip, so it probably carried some already; it doesn't say. Log which stamina food it has when fishing starts; consider topping up (eating) when it keeps stopping for breath and a slot is close to running out.
- Issue #17 (Femis64): revived companion goes straight back into the fight that killed it. [Death] RespawnStance = RecoverFirst (default) / Passive / Keep (Keep = today: the stance saved on the tombstone, RespawnCombatStanceHash).
- RecoverFirst (user design, 2026-10-14): after a revive with items in its tombstone it acts Passive (status "Recovering its gear"), goes for the tombstone when safe, re-equips, eats, then goes back to the stance it died with ("Got my gear back."). Ends early when you set a stance yourself. No time limit; tombstone out of reach (bed respawn far away) -> stays Passive until the gear is collected or you set a stance.
  - Auto-pickup OFF while its tombstone still has items (user: so it never ends up leaving something in the tombstone), back on after.
  - Safe = checked against the game's targeting (MonsterAI.UpdateTarget, decompiled): every 2 s (player within 50 m; else 6 s), when not mid-attack, a mob re-runs FindEnemy and takes the CLOSEST enemy it can sense (sleeping mobs skip; CanSenseTarget = sight/hearing). Damage only sets a target when it has none. So "engaged with the player" holds only while the player stays closer to that mob than the companion. Rule: unsafe if any alert mob is targeting the companion within [Death] RecoverThreatRange (15 m), or the companion's path/tombstone would bring it closer to a mob than that mob's current target (+ margin), or more than [Death] RecoverMaxMobs (3) alert mobs are within RecoverThreatRange of the tombstone (user: "If there 2-3 mobs its probably ok to risk it... More than that wait somewhere safe"). Waiting = near the player, out of the mobs' reach; status "Recovering gear - waiting for it to be safe".
  - Turned on mid-run: keep running if it reaches the tombstone before the mob reaches it (remaining distance vs mob's distance, margin), else back to the player.
  - Order after pickup (user): self-defence/escape > re-equip asap (weapon + shield first, ~1 s each = m_equipDuration, then armor) > fight back once armed and caught > make its way to the player; equip on the move. Once equipped: eat the best food at once (CompanionFood.TryAutoConsume now, not at the next food check) - health and stamina back up.
  - Sneaking toward the tombstone would cut hearing detection - tie-in with the sneaking backlog item, not in this build.
- Stance keys: [Controls] PassiveKey / BalancedKey / RangedKey / MeleeKey, all None by default. Looking at a companion -> only that one; else every companion within RecallRange with Command on. Top-left message "Gunter, Saber: Passive". No cycle key (user asked; aim-to-pick instead; proper selection belongs with formations).
- From the 1.5.42 test (log b97a9478, no errors; user: "looks much better for the notifications"): routes still go through the hearth's flames. Idle walk to (-270.4, 40.0, -306.9) and the cultivator fetch in log 4b94cbba both crossed the fire at about (-269.8, 40.5, -308.6): the navmesh route skirts the hearth's collider but the Burning EffectArea reaches further, so it steps in, takes 10 fire damage a tick (armor -> 0.7), then EscapeFire/jumps out. The jump-over check only looks 0.8 m ahead along the current heading. Fix: after a route is planned, sample each leg every 0.5 m with InFire and put a corner round the fire (left/right of the burning area at its radius + 0.6 m, on ground, in sight), unless the goal itself is in the fire. Same for idle wander goals: never pick one whose straight leg crosses a fire.
- Same log: Follow-pool lines ("Which way now?", "What lies ahead, I wonder?") while following you around at home / sitting on the throne - tag those [away] (phase 3 content pass).
- Issue #16 (745692208, closed without a comment): auto-respawn with a claimed bed spawns at the bed (CompanionTombstoneResurrect.MHR_ResurrectCore, RespawnBedPosHash) - far away = unloaded zone, so no map pin (pins come from the loaded CompanionSetup), recall/super recall only see CompanionSetup.AllCompanions (loaded), roster says Alive. Do now: (1) message on auto-respawn "Saber is back - at its bed (850 m away)"; (2) roster shows where an alive companion is (distance/biome from the ZDO). BACKLOG: (3) super recall for unloaded companions, (4) map pins for unloaded companions from the ZDO.

### 1.5.42 - speech overhaul phase 2 (why lines, status, death announcement)
- CompanionStatus (Companion/CompanionStatus.cs): per companion the last "why it isn't working" (localization key + text, 180 s fresh). Why(c, key, speak, args) records it and with speak=true also CompanionTalk.SayProblem(key, text) (topic = key, 300 s). Clear(c) when the job gets going again; ToolKeeper clears it on a job change. Describe(cs) = the hover status: sleeping > fighting/hunting X > fetching its spear > resting > carrying out your order > ToolKeeper trip (TripText: fetching/putting back/emptying bags/repairing gear/needs X till home) > "<Job> - <reason or nothing to do>" when idling, "<Job> - following you, looking out for work" when following with nothing on > "<Job>" > Following you / Idling / Staying put / Waiting. CompanionInteract.GetHoverText adds it under the name in grey ([General] ShowStatus).
- Reasons recorded: Hunt (pack full/far - spoken, spear out - spoken, nothing: enough meat / out of reach / no game, Passive stance - spoken; cleared on a new prey; "It got away." Event); Fish (its Say() keys + nothing to fish); Gather (MHR_GatherRules.Scan: plenty wood/stone/ore at limits, else nothing left); Farm (per scan flags: no output chest, no seeds in pack/chests, no zones - spoken; else waiting for crops); Smelt (no chests - spoken, monitoring, nothing to smelt); Cook (nothing to cook); Upkeep (everything's in order). IdleController.SayWhy says CompanionStatus.Reason first, else hc_idle_why_<mode>.
- Give-ups spoken: MHR_Mover give-up records hc_why_cant_reach_up (goal > 2 m up) / hc_why_no_way and speaks it for Follow/Command/Combat/Hunt tags (jobs: status only). MHR_WhyNotCommanded also shows top left (Command off / too far, with the hold-key hint). ToolKeeper: taken tool (Event), no chest has room (Problem), Speak() for every kind via hc_why_need_tool (was axe/pickaxe/arrows only). RepairController: no station can fix X - spoken only at home. MHR_SpearThrow: lost/gave up on the spear.
- Cook why (user: "will the NPC say something like 'I need more than 20 ingredients' reading whatever the set number is from the config?"): ShortIngredient (mead brewing) now remembers the item/need/have; with PickTask null the status/idle reason is hc_why_cook_reserve "I need {need} {item}, but there are only {have} and I keep {Reserve} back for you" (raw ingredient, [Cooking] IngredientReserve), hc_why_cook_short (made ingredient), hc_why_cook_no_station (no usable station: level, fire, roof), else nothing to cook. Other cooking tasks (cauldron food, grill) don't report a shortfall yet.
- User test (log 920b68b9): "Farming kept saying no zones. And forage was listing as nothing to gather when the NPC looked to be gathering. It looks like it is working though." No errors. Farm: no zones -> planting skipped every cycle, but harvesting works without zones; the no-zones reason was spoken as a problem and again as the idle why, and "All done farming." came after every harvest round. Now status-only ("Nothing ripe yet - and I only plant in farm zones, there are none."), farm-done Event once per 600 s (Speak overload with a repeat). Forage: MHR_GatherRules.Scan found nothing but HarvestController then took a loose pickup - the "nothing to gather" reason stayed; now cleared whenever HarvestController picks any target.
- Farm zones lost (user: "Did the farm zones disappear? I had some set at one point."): zones live on the companion's ZDO (HC_FarmZones, FarmZoneSerializer); the respawn from the tombstone (CompanionTombstoneResurrect) and the roster recovery (MHR_Roster) copied name/gear/home/bed/skills/settings but not the zones. Now: tombstone gets HC_Respawn_FarmZones (CompanionSetup.CreateCompanionTombstone) -> copied back on resurrect; roster Entry.FarmZones appended at the end of the saved entry (older entries: ReadOptionalString -> "") and restored on recovery. Zones lost before this build can't be brought back. (CompanionManager.QueueRespawn has no callers.)
- Tilled ground as an Any zone (user: "update farming to use tilled ground as an 'any' zone? Maybe with an option to turn it off so the NPC will only use set zones."): [Farming] PlantTilledGround (true). FarmController.RefreshFarmZones appends FindTilledPatches (cached 30 s): a 2 m grid over [Farming] Range around home (if within range) or the companion, cells that are cultivated (MHR_SafeCultivated) and not inside a player zone, flood-filled into patches -> square FarmZone{Tilled=true, CropSeed="" (Any)}, half = max extent/2 + 1.5 m. FindPlantPositionInZone: in a Tilled zone every cell must be cultivated (whatever the seed) and not inside a player zone. Any = MHR_SeedRules.PickSeed -> the [Farming] PlantAny list (crops, no tree seeds). Zones placed with the editor aren't affected (FarmZonePlacer saves its own list; tilled patches are never saved).
- Death: CompanionSetup.OnCompanionDeath -> MessageHud Center "Gunter has died (biome, N m from you)" when MHR_Owner.IsMine ([Speech] AnnounceDeath). Not done: "lost" announcements - the roster only works out Lost when asked (ShowCompanionRoster/shrine); would need a periodic check. Death on another player's game isn't sent to the owner (issue #10 item).
- User test (log 612481ad): "Gunter keeps trying to shoot a birds and shoots directly into the hillside." Bird shots (HuntController) aimed with no BaseAI target creature, so GetProjectileSpawnPoint/CompanionProjectileAimPatch fell back to the look direction; and the line-of-sight check used MHR_Mover.CanSee, which adds 1.6 m to both ends - from ~3.2 m up, not the bow. Now CompanionProjectileAimPatch.AimAt(humanoid, point) gives the patch a point target (2 s fresh, no lead) and the LOS is from hand height to the bird.
- Same log: "now planting under the wall" - the plant-spot search only avoided Plant/Pickable/Piece colliders within half the grow radius (GetComponent on the collider itself; wall colliders sit on children). The game (Plant.UpdateHealth) gives NoSun for any Default/static_solid/piece hit straight up and NoSpace for any non-plant collider in the full grow radius. FarmController.NoSunOrRoom does the same check (roof raycast + overlap with GetComponentInParent Plant/Pickable skipped), in FindPlantPositionInZone (counted as roofOrWall in the log) and FindPlantPosition. "Ran off outside the base": the tilled scan joined 88 cells into one patch; the planting spots were his tilled ground ~25 m south - working as built.
- User test (log 7209b50a): "There is a little tiny spot of tilled soil just outside the wall... Gunter keeps trying to get to it." The tilled scan (2 m grid flood fill) joins the garden inside and the spot outside the wall into one patch (hs 13). Route to (-268.5, 36.5, -337.2) was partial (ended at the wall), unstuck ladder ran out -> give-up -> _ai.BlacklistPosition (2 m cell, 30 s) -> next search picked (-267.8, ...) in the next cell, same again. No errors in the log. Fix: FindPlantPositionInZone collects all valid cells, sorts by planting order and takes the first one MHR_Mover.Reachable says it can walk to (full route whose end is within UseDistance of the spot - Pathfinding snaps the end to the nearest navmesh, which can be inside the wall - or CanGetTo: hops/doors), at most MaxReachChecks 8 route queries per search. Unreachable spots and plant-spot give-ups go into _unreachable (1 m cells) for [Farming] UnreachableSpotMemory (600 s), skipped by both plant searches. Log: cantReach=N.
- "Status just says farm": CompanionStatus.Doing -> FarmController.DoingText (harvesting / picking up the harvest / fetching seeds / planting <seed> / storing the harvest) while the farm is busy. Other jobs: phase 3.
- Status effects (user: "Do we show status effects on the HUD bar? And do status effects impact the NPC?"): CompanionHudPanel shows the companion's SE icons (food, Rested...). SEs apply through the game's own handling (regen via StatusEffectsOnRegen, damage mods, speed). The environment effects (Wet, Cold, Freezing, Sheltered, campfire warmth) are only applied to players by the game (Player.UpdateEnvStatusEffects), so companions never get them -> BACKLOG status-effects review.
- User test (log 4b94cbba): "When making mead it should say mead rather than cook... It just reports the need for honey and nothing else. I took all the ingredients so there really is nothing to cook. Thats not clear... It should rotate between missing details every so often. The mead message is to long." + "The messages should be shorter where possible. The command off notice was nice just long." Log: all three focus bases short of Honey (0 in reach), food silently nothing. Now CookController keeps every reason of a scan (_whys, max 8, de-duplicated): ShortIngredient adds one hc_why_mead_short "{item} {have}/{need} for {mead}" per short ingredient (need = recipe amount + IngredientReserve for raw items), no station -> hc_why_cook_no_station; then one food reason: hc_why_cook_no_food when FoodTask saw some food under target (_foodWanted) else hc_why_cook_stocked. ShowWhy shows one per scan (10 s) in turn via CompanionStatus.WhyAs(label "Mead"/"Cook"); Describe uses the label in place of the job name, IdleController.SayWhy speaks SpokenReason ("Mead: Honey 0/30 for ..."). Wording still to be agreed with the user. Shortened: hc_cmd_refused_off "{0}: Command is off on the wheel.", hc_cmd_refused_far "{0} is {1} m away. Hold {2} s to call all.", hc_why_farm_no_zones "Nothing ripe, and nowhere to plant.", hc_why_cook_no_station "Can't use the {0} (level, fire, roof)." Other things in the log: no errors; 3x vanilla "Trying to add item to occupied slot -1, -1" while the player had a chest open (not companion code); [Food] CanConsume REJECTED / AutoConsume lines 768 and [Repair] Scan 436 lines - BACKLOG log cleanup; a cultivator fetch from (-270.4, 41.7) got stuck 30 s, then the detour went through a fire (stepped out) - chest at (-273.0, 40.0, -305.8) "no spot to stand", watch.
- Test build 1.5.42.

### 1.5.41 - speech overhaul phase 1 (user, 2026-10-13: "make sure anything the NPC says is location and action appropriate. Also it will help players understand why / why not a NPC is doing something"; "The NPC is way to chatty"; far-away messages and a hover status line agreed)
- Version rule: test builds 1.5.41, 1.5.42...; the next published build is 1.5.5 (rename the CHANGELOG section then).
- CompanionTalk is now a speech manager. Priority Ambient < Event < Problem < Order. Order (SayOrder: radial Action pool, CompanionInteract command replies, radial repair results) ignores the cooldowns. Problem (SayProblem(topic, text)) - not repeated for 300 s per topic, queued until there's a gap (60 s), and when the player is beyond [Speech] CullDistance it goes to MessageHud TopLeft as "Name: text" ([Speech] FarAwayMessages). Event (Say - all older callers) queues for 6 s. Ambient is dropped when blocked. Same line never twice in a row per pool. Every shown line is logged "[Speech] <prio> <name>: <text>" (Debug).
- Removed: the Action line on any ActionMode change in Update (fired on code-made changes - roster restore, 16->15, CancelAll); the radial menu already says it when the player picks.
- Ambient: [Speech] Chatter Off/Rare(240-480 s)/Normal(90-180)/Often(30-60) replaces MinInterval/MaxInterval (15/30; old keys cleaned out by MigrateAndClean). [Speech] Enabled. Nothing while sleeping/sitting/going to rest, swimming, on/boarding a ship, or carrying out an order (MHR_JobRules.OtherOrder). Combat pool only when IsInCombat and not hunting. Needs (overweight, hungry, worn gear, no bed - bed only at night) at most every 600 s per need.
