Both turn themselves off; checked once a second from CompanionsPlugin.Update (MHR_Roster.Tick), only with a live player in a world. Bools so they work in the in-game config panel too (it doesn't show strings).
- Despawn button: PrepareForDespawn -> MHR_Roster.Forget (no "recovering" a despawned companion). A companion only joins the roster once it's been loaded near its owner on 1.5.1+, so companions lost before this update can't be recovered.
- User test (2026-10-11): recovery worked (Gunter Lost -> recovered, bed re-linked), but came back with 0 items, and the user asked for a save at logout/exit. Cause: the first snapshot ran the moment the world loaded, before the companion's Container had loaded its items from the ZDO (Container.CheckForChanges, after Awake), and nothing re-saved before quitting. Fix: snapshots skip a companion for 10 s after its Awake (CompanionSetup._mhrAwakeTime), the first snapshot waits 12 s, and a Game.SavePlayerProfile prefix (Patches/RosterSavePatch.cs) snapshots before every character save (autosave, logout, quit); s_saving stops our own save re-entering. Log lines now give item counts (added, recovered).
- User asked for recovery to remember the old tombstone: already does - CompanionAI's tombstone scan matches tombstones by the permanent companion ID (MHR_IsOwnGrave: tomb MHR_TombCompanionIdHash == companion MHR_CompanionIdHash), which recovery keeps; the companion's own TombstoneIdHash isn't used for finding it (only CancelTombstoneRecovery). Within [Death] TombstoneRecoveryRange it goes and collects. Guide updated; no code change.
- Odin's Shrine (user, 2026-10-11: "What about a ingame object the player can use." - built into 1.5.1 since the PR isn't merged). First version: ward-stone clone + a custom UGUI window. User test: worked, but "I could not click on the close button. I was thinking more of a pop-up using the default valheim message window. This looks very different." and "Can we make it a tombstone and call it 'Odins Shrine'?" -> custom window dropped (UI/ShrinePanel.cs and its input-block entries removed).
- Now Companion/MHR_Shrine.cs: prefab HC_CompanionShrine (name kept so shrines already placed stay) = clone of [Death] ShrineModel - Tombstone (default, Player_tombstone) or Ward (guard_stone) - in an inactive DontDestroyOnLoad holder. Tombstone: m_worldText set to "Odin's Shrine", floater off, TombStone/Floating/Container/ZSyncTransform/Rigidbody removed, Piece added (place effect from the ward, icon TrophySkeleton), non-trigger colliders moved to the "piece" layer (hammer remove/aim masks). No WearNTear -> the hammer removes it the non-WNT way (DropResources refunds). Ward: PrivateArea removed (area marker destroyed, in-range effect off, enabled glow on), CircleProjectors removed. Registered on every ZNetScene.Awake (always - shrines would otherwise be deleted as unknown prefabs); added to the Hammer's PieceTable only with ShrineBuildable; recipe from ShrineRecipe; workbench station.
- Using it: UnifiedPopup with the roster (coloured statuses). Nothing to recover -> WarningPopup (OK). Otherwise a YesNoPopup per Lost companion, then per Unknown one (with the warning): Yes -> MHR_Roster.RecoverAt (re-checks the status) 2 m in front of the shrine; No -> next one.
- Not verified offline: how the tombstone model looks as a fixed piece (world text, any glow) - check in game.
- Known: recovering an Unknown companion that had actually died gives the snapshot gear while its tombstone keeps its items (the warning covers it).

### 1.5.1 - companion lost when it dies while the player is dead (GitHub issue #10, 2026-10-10)
- Report (knollixer): "My Companion disappeared after die on sea". Log: WaterSafety "stamina low while swimming, but no nearby shore found yet" (open sea), companion died, "[Error] [Setup] Could not find tombstone prefab!", then "[Return] OnSpawned: respawn hook fired / AllCompanions was empty" - the player respawned right after, so the player had died too.
- Cause: CompanionSetup.CreateCompanionTombstone took the prefab from Player.m_localPlayer.m_tombstone; while the player is dead m_localPlayer is null -> no tombstone, no respawn data (WriteRespawnData lives on the tombstone) -> the companion is gone for good. Version can't be told from the log (every line also exists in 1.5.0) - the bug is in 1.5.0 too.
- Fix: fall back to Game.instance.m_playerPrefab's Player.m_tombstone, then ZNetScene "Player_tombstone".
- Second part (user: "Are there any other features of the mod that copy from the player like that?"): MHR_RespawnTracker.OnCompanionDied treated a death with no local player as "another player's companion" (IsLocalOwner needs Player.m_localPlayer), so it wasn't put on the pending-respawn list (kept in the player's m_customData) - it would only come back when the player went near the tombstone, at sea. Now: owner checked against Game.GetPlayerProfile().GetPlayerID(), entry (uid prefix + RespawnAt) kept in s_deferred and added to the list on the first Tick with a live player.
- Audit of the other Player.m_localPlayer uses: the rest only pause while there's no player (ToolKeeper tick, follow restore watchdog, grave-pin sweep, messages, RestoreActionMode at load - jobs read the mode from the ZDO anyway) and pick up again; resurrect position with no bed and no player falls back to the world spawn. Nothing else loses data.
- Found while answering "does the respawn have an animation" (effects: fx_eikthyr_forwardshockwave, vfx_offering, sfx_lore_read, sfx_GP_activate at the spawn spot + "X has been resurrected!"): MHR_ResurrectCore called comp.PlayResurrectEffects(vector) with comp == null on the far-away auto-respawn path (MHR_RespawnTracker.Tick -> MHR_ResurrectCore(zdo, null, true)). NullReferenceException after the companion was spawned, before the tombstone respawn data was cleared and before RemovePending -> caught by CompanionsPlugin.Update -> next Tick 1 s later spawned it again (duplicates every second). Since the IL->C# conversion (1.4.74), from the original. Fix: PlayResurrectEffects static. Also comp == null with an empty tombstone: respawn data cleared + CleanupHash set (else loading the tombstone later re-attached the resurrect component and respawned a second copy).
- GitHub report (no log): companion missing after logging back in at base (logged off with it working there). No code path found that destroys or moves a companion at logout/login (ZNetView persistent; only Despawn, death and tombstone code destroy it; ProtectCompanions guards other mods). Asked for LogOutput.log, versions, single player vs server, mods that clean up creatures; ResetStarterFlag for a new one.
- Recovery for the reporter: the companion can't come back (nothing was saved); [General] ResetStarterFlag = true gives a new free starter companion on the next world load.
- Related, for the ship/cart review: companions in open sea with no shore in reach (WaterSafety only seeks a shore nearby) - e.g. a sunk ship or falling off mid-voyage.

### 1.5.0 - major version (user, 2026-10-10: "bump the build to 1.5.00 so its clean and shows its a major version")
- Version 1.5.0 (not 1.5.00: mod managers/BepInEx want Major.Minor.Patch without a leading zero). Same code as 1.4.156. CHANGELOG has a 1.5.0 entry pointing at the 1.4.x entries below it; README highlights header v1.5.0.

### 1.4.156 - [Cooking] MakeMead (user, 2026-10-10: "A setting to turn off making mead")
- [Cooking] MakeMead (default on). Off: PickTask skips FillTask and both BrewTask passes, and HiveTask doesn't go for honey for priority mead bases (idle honey collection unchanged); finished fermenters are still tapped.
- Docs (user review of GUIDE.md): removed the "Farm Zones" wheel row (there's no such slice - right-click Farm opens the editor, or Left Alt + Z); added the unloaded-area note (jobs intro + troubleshooting; README Repair and Restock); several attackers "(a tower shield, say)"; MakeMead mentioned.

### 1.4.155 - EngageOnNotice (user, 2026-10-10: "I think it was better the other way where the NPC would engage mob's that have noticed us. Maybe we can have it as a toggle")
- [Combat] EngageOnNotice (bool, default true = the pre-1.4.154 behaviour: MHR_Threat.IsAfterUs only needs the creature's target to be a player/companion). Off = also requires BaseAI.IsAlerted() (1.4.154). Hunt unaffected. README Combat section updated.

### 1.4.154 - unprovoked fights, early "nothing dropped" (user test of 1.4.152/153, 2026-10-10)
- User: short test, nothing "crazy off". "NPC did not go and gather from the neck it shot while wandering around with forage enabled. Seems to be going after creatures that have not engaged us yet."
- Log: "[Combat] Engage -> target=$enemy_neck ... its target=\"Human\" alerted=False" from CompanionAI's FindEnemy path; MHR_Threat.IsAfterUs only checked the creature's target. Now also requires BaseAI.IsAlerted() (a monster that has only spotted the player sets its target before it's alerted). Attackers are still engaged (OnDamaged, near projectile hit), Hunt engages prey directly. User: "This same check should not apply to hunting ... great to catch creatures off guard" - confirmed it doesn't: HuntController picks prey itself and calls CompanionCombatAI.EngagePrey (no IsAfterUs); the mid-fight target-switch check (CombatAI ~617) skips the current target, so the prey is never dropped for being unalerted.
- Log: "[Loot] Going to pick up what Neck dropped (18 m away)" -> walked to (-268, -364) next to the kill -> "nothing dropped": no ragdoll found (k.Body false) and no drop within 2 s of arriving. The no-body/no-drop finish now also needs 8 s since the kill; the log line says how long it waited. (Possible: the modded Neck_RtD's ragdoll/drops differ - check the next log.)

### 1.4.153 - carts/ships for unloading (user, 2026-10-09: "I thought we made it so the NPC could unload into a ship or cart if commanded or away from home, like at an outpost or out at a mining vein")
- Confirmed unchanged by 1.4.152: MHR_GatherRules.DropOff (overweight unload) still allows carts (Vagon) and ships away from home, chests only near home (user decision from the HarvestController audit); a commanded deposit (DirectDeposit -> CompanionAI deposit, MHR_Chests.Open) works on any container incl. carts/ships. MHR_Chests.Usable's vehicle exclusion only applies to the jobs' own chest scans (Smelt, Cook, Farm, ToolKeeper, Upkeep sorting). DropOff now uses MHR_Chests.InUse (synced) and a ward check.

### 1.4.152 - release blockers A-C (user, 2026-10-09: "Lets start on A-C"; also "I think the others should also be at 10 m")
- [Gathering] FollowRange default 20 -> 10 (Wood/Stone/Ore; Forage has ForageFollowRange 10). Existing files keep their value (user's says 20 - tell them).
- A. New AI/MHR_Owner.cs: Id (OwnerHash), Of (owner's Player if loaded here: local if it matches, else Player.GetAllPlayers by GetPlayerID; unclaimed -> local), Go, IsMine, CreatorId (owner id as long), TakeControl (ClaimOwnership). Replaced "the player" = Player.m_localPlayer in: CompanionAI RestoreFollowOrPatrol + ship detach spot; Harvest work area/leash/follow restore; Farm/Smelt/Cook/Hunt follow restore or follow centre; ToolKeeper WaitHome/trip follow; MHR_Loot "you've moved on"; CompanionRest StopAll/CancelNavigation/StopSitting/StopSleeping, directed-sit distance, sleep when its player sleeps (OwnerInBed; Player.InBed and GetPlayerID read the ZDO so work for remote players), sit emote only for IsMine; CompanionSetup ApplyFollowMode + follow watchdog (now follows the owner when loaded instead of refusing on owner mismatch); TombstoneResurrect follow; Idle social buddy. SetCreator (Farm plants, Upkeep piles) and private-chest checks use CreatorId. Messages (tools weak, rested, level-up) only when IsMine. Commands: MHR_InScope (every Direct command's filter) calls TakeControl when the companion answers; CancelExistingActions too; radial/panel Show take control for IsMine. Valheim keeps the ZDO with that player while they're near.
- B. MHR_Chests (in MHR_ToolKeeper.cs): Usable(c, who) (not Character/TombStone/Vagon/Ship, has a Piece, valid, not InUse, PrivateArea.CheckAccess, private only if creator == owner), InUse (local flag or, when not the owner, ZDO s_inUse), Claim, Open (InUse -> false; Claim then SetInUse - SetInUse does nothing on a non-owner), Close. ToolKeeper.UsableChest -> MHR_Chests.Usable with s_current (the companion being ticked). Smelt/Farm/Cook/deposit/Upkeep open via MHR_Chests.Open; ToolKeeper trips skip InUse chests and Claim before editing; Smelt/Farm/Cook chest scans use Usable. Cook.Usable: ward check for Fermenter/Beehive; Farm plant spots: ward check. Farm/Smelt/Cook ground pickups: CanPickup(false) else RequestOwn (no add-before-own copies). PortalPatches.WarpCompanion: SetOwner(GetSessionID) before SetPosition.
- C. MHR_Chests.AddHomeChests (near home: Usable chests within HomeTripRange of home) in Smelt ScanNearbyChests and Cook scan - they no longer fall back to taking down piles while the wood is in the home chests. MHR_Chests.DropsNear snapshot before extract/removal: Upkeep honey (_dropsBefore), Upkeep piles (_pileDropsBefore), MHR_PileTaker (_before), Cook hive (_honeySkip) only pick up drops that weren't there before.
- Not done: Cook "Known" recipes / unique keys still read the local player (a remote player's known recipes aren't available on this game); static maps pruning; bed hover text.

It should be more of a you stumbled upon some raspberries". ### 1.4.150 - full-mod conflict audit (user, 2026-10-09: "run through the full logic of the mod and see if there are any conflicts")
- Five read-only audit agents (steering, equipment/inventory, state transitions, shared resources, Harmony/multiplayer); findings re-checked in code before fixing. User: fix 1-14 now, plan A-C; multiplayer matters for the release.
- Steering: MHR_Mover.GoInner resets the route whenever the tag changes, so two steerers on one frame freeze each other. Fixed: Hunt UpdateGoingHome returns while IsEngaged; DoorHandler.Update waits (and extends its 60 s) during combat/loot/ToolKeeper/UI, new DoorHandler.Cancel() called from CancelExistingActions and CancelAll; Harvest Update: Loot/OrderPending before TickDodge (pile walk, dodge), combat before HandleOverweight (SetPendingDeposit beat combat in UpdateAI); MHR_ShouldPauseWork also true for WaterSafety.Active (new; _shoreTarget by Character id), CompanionAI.IsOnShip and CompanionRest._isSitting; Idle.Free blocks IsOnShip; ToolKeeper.TickOne starts no trip while MHR_JobRules.OtherOrder.
- Items: Smelt UpdateInsertingItem consumes from the pack first (ConsumeOneFromInventory returns bool) and rescans when it's gone - ToolKeeper's unload can put fetched ore back in a chest mid-job. Fish _rodOut: RestoreGear on mode change/combat even in Idle (rod stays out between casts). Hunt birds: SuppressAutoEquip while the bow is out (_birdBowOut, ReleaseBirdBow in EndBird/Stop/mode change/Notify). Farm seed take: n limited by CanAddItem before RemoveItem. Upkeep StopIdleChore: leftovers -> MHR_ToolKeeper.RequestUnload.
- State: CompanionRest StopAll/CancelNavigation/StopSitting/StopSleeping set the follow target only when GetFollow() (they always followed the local player). CancelAll and ExitGatherMode call MHR_JobRules.OnJobSet(setup, 0); new MHR_JobRules.FollowSetByPlayer clears HC_FollowPausedByJob (radial ToggleFollow, FireComeToMe). Respawn carries HC_FollowPausedByJob (HC_Respawn_FollowPaused on the tombstone). MHR_Follow.Tick refreshes LastUpdate for an idle chair seat (ChairTaken ignored seats older than 5 s). Idle social stops when the buddy is > Radius*2 from the idle centre.
- Chests: Smelt/Farm/Cook/CompanionAI deposit check IsInUse before opening (abort / back to scanning / cancel deposit); ToolKeeper.EndTrip no longer SetInUse(false) (never set it); Smelt/Farm CloseAnyOpenChest on the pause paths and OnDestroy. Farm PlantSeed re-checks the spot's spacing before planting.
- Patches: Attack.SpawnOnHitTerrain prefix only skips for companions (was every non-player - also Aoe.OnHit uses the return). TombstonePreventDespawnPatch still runs UpdateFloater and PositionCheck (AccessTools) and only skips the empty->destroy. Sleep "waiting for" message only when ZNet IsServer. ModConfig: MigrateAndClean returns whether an old file was upgraded; the ForageItems "*" and ViewRange/ViewAngle 40/90 fix-ups only run then.
- Not changed: bed hover text ("X's Bed" replacing the claim prompt) - it marks a companion's bed; left as a design choice. Static per-instance maps never pruned (MHR_Follow.s_s, MHR_Mover.s_s/s_opened, MHR_ToolKeeper.s_state, MHR_Loot.s_state, MHR_GatherRules.s_per, MHR_SeedRules.s_rot; CompanionRest frees _claimedBeds only when sleeping) - slow growth, BACKLOG.

### 1.4.148 - ship seat kinematic warnings (user, 2026-10-09, before the config review and public release)
- CompanionAI.AttachToShip no longer sets isKinematic (Character.UpdateWalking sets linearVelocity every physics tick -> Unity warning each tick on a kinematic body; same fix as Follow chairs 1.4.77 and beds 1.4.97). Gravity off, ship colliders ignored (as before), PinToShip (ship-local seat pos/rot -> world, body velocity = ship point velocity) from UpdateShipAttach + new CompanionAI.FixedUpdate/LateUpdate, owner only. DetachFromShip unchanged (isKinematic=false, gravity back on).

### 1.4.147 - every order beats every job (user, 2026-10-09: "check the other jobs for this issue")
- Audit of the Direct commands: move, cart, deposit, tombstone (1.4.145), repair (1.4.146) were covered. Not covered: board ship (CompanionAI._pendingShip), open door (DoorHandler - only Smelt/Farm/Harvest waited), sit by fire / sleep in bed when ordered (CompanionRest._isDirected; MHR_ShouldPauseWork only covers sleeping and walking to a bed). Attack = combat (every job yields to IsInCombat/IsEngaged). Come to me / cancel set mode 0 (jobs off). Gather/smelt commands only set the mode.
- MHR_JobRules.OtherOrder(c) = move/cart/deposit/tombstone/ship (CompanionAI.IsBoardingShip), DoorHandler.IsActive, CompanionRest.IsDirectedOrder (new: _isDirected && (navigating || sitting || sleeping)). OrderPending = OtherOrder || RepairController.IsDirect. RepairController aborts on OtherOrder. Upkeep's order hold and IdleController.Free now use OrderPending instead of PendingMoveTarget / the old list.
- CompanionAI drives rest navigation, ship boarding, move, deposit and tombstone before its job IsActive check, and DoorHandler / RepairController steer themselves, so a paused job that still reads IsActive doesn't block them.

### 1.4.146 - ordered repair goes first (user test of 1.4.145, 2026-10-09)
- Log: NoStationWait worked (arbalest skipped 10 min), idle started after Cook ran out ("Going to sit on the $piece_throne01"). But the Direct repair order (forge, then workbench) was aborted the same frame: 1.4.144's ShouldAbort aborts on Cook IsActive.
- RepairController._direct (set by DirectRepairAt, cleared when the phase goes Idle); IsDirect => _direct && phase != Idle. ShouldAbort returns a reason string; with IsDirect only combat, loot, other orders and the panel/radial abort it. MHR_JobRules.OrderPending also returns true for IsDirect, so every job pauses for it (Upkeep already yields to any gear repair; Idle's Free blocks on it).

### 1.4.145 - one mover at a time, repair give-up (user, 2026-10-09: "check the other jobs for the same issue? There certainly should be some sort of timeout if there is not a station that the NPC can repair at.")
- Audit of who calls MHR_Mover.Go/MHR_Nav.Move and what each yields to. Orders (PendingMoveTarget, PendingCartAttach, PendingDepositContainer, IsRecoveringTombstone) are walked by CompanionAI before its job check, but only Upkeep waited for them - Cook, Smelt, Farm, Harvest, Hunt, Fish and gear Repair kept steering. New MHR_JobRules.OrderPending(c); each job returns next to its MHR_Loot.Active check (a pause - the phase is kept; their _stuckTimer only counts while they run). Repair aborts on loot pickup or an order (ShouldAbort). Already fine: ToolKeeper and rest (MHR_ShouldPauseWork), Idle (Free), Upkeep (own order hold + yields to gear repair).
- RepairController: _noStationUntil per item - a worn item below the threshold that no station in ScanRadius can repair (blacklisted stations don't count; claimed ones do) is skipped for [Repair] NoStationWait (600 s), logged once. MovingToStation also aborts on MHR_Mover.GaveUp or LegTimeout (120 s) and blacklists the station (30 s), so the next scan marks the item as above. Before: no station -> silent 60 s back-off forever.

### 1.4.144 - gear repair vs jobs (user test of 1.4.143, 2026-10-09)
- Log: Cook on, went for honey, then took Ooze for Muckshake and stood still. RepairController had started MovingToStation (forge) just before; it only yielded to Harvest/Smelt, so Cook's MHR_Mover.Go and Repair's Go ("Repair") ran every frame and cancelled each other. ShouldAbort now also covers Cook/Farm/Hunt/Fish IsActive (Upkeep already yields to gear repair).
- The trip was triggered by the arbalest at 56% (< HomeThreshold 80% at home), which only the black forge repairs; the station was picked by "can repair any worn item", so it went to the forge for 96-100% bronze gear - and would again every scan. Now only items below RepairThreshold pick the station (log "canRepairWorn"); none repairable in range -> 60 s back-off. The station's queue still tops up everything else it can fix there.
- Idle never started in that log (Cook stayed IsActive on the stalled task).

### 1.4.143 - idle behaviours (user, 2026-10-09)
- User's answers: all four activities (small chores, sit and rest, wander and look busy, socialize); near their job; say why now and then; global [Idle] settings.
- New Jobs/IdleController.cs (added in CompanionPrefabs). Free() = not Stay, not following, no combat/swim, no pending order (move, cart, deposit, tombstone), no ToolKeeper trip or loot pickup, not resting, panel/radial not open, no job controller IsActive (Harvest, Smelt, Farm, Cook, Hunt, Fish, gear Repair, DoorHandler, Upkeep other than its own idle chore). Free for [Idle] StartAfter (10 s) -> Begin: center = where it is (mode 0 near home: home). Not free -> Stop at once (chore stopped, stands up, mover released).
- Next(): chore first (UpkeepController.StartIdleChore(center, ChoreRadius 15) - repair, fuel, honey, then stow; retried every 20 s), else random Wander x2 / Sit / Social (if someone is near). Sit: MHR_Follow.IdleFindChair within Radius, walk there (20 s limit), 20-60 s seated; no chair -> sit on the floor. Wander: 40% a station/chest approach spot (PickApproachPiece), else a dry reachable ground point within Radius; walk speed, then stand 4-9 s looking around. Social: local player or another companion within Radius x2, stop at 2.5 m, face them, hc_idle_chat_1..6 (60%) at most every SpeechInterval/2 (min 60 s).
- SayWhy: hc_idle_why_<mode> when idling starts, at most every [Idle] SpeechInterval (300 s, 0 = never).
- UpkeepController: idle-chore hook (_idleChore, _idleRange; Update runs outside mode 15 while it's on; ends after the chore + stow).
- MHR_Follow: S.IdleSeat + IdleFindChair/ClaimChair/Sit/SitFloor/Seated/Stand; an idle seat only stands up for combat or bed navigation.
- CompanionAI: IdleController.IsActive in the job list (no StopMoving / vanilla idle wander while it idles).
- [Idle]: Enabled, StartAfter, Radius 8, Chores, ChoreRadius 15, Sit, Wander, Socialize, SpeechInterval 300.

### 1.4.141 - movement phase 4, part 2 (user, 2026-10-09)
- After PR #5 (1.4.86-1.4.140) was merged; branch restarted from main.
- CompanionAI: removed MoveToPoint (context-steering fallback, path-stuck timer, nav "claimed done" retries), ContextSteer, UpdateFallbackStuck, FindBestStrafeAngle, UpdateGroundStuckRecovery (strafe, jump, TeleportToFollowTarget after 5 tries, only active while the old MoveToPoint steered), UpdateProactiveJump (jumped after 2 s below 0.5 m/s with any job active - including while working in place), IsNavMeshFailing/Retrying and ~40 fields/consts. Formation follow no longer falls back to vanilla Follow. _continuousSwimTimer (hazard recovery) is now updated in UpdateAI. ### 1.4.140 - lining up with doorways (user, 2026-10-09)
- User (1.4.139 test): closing and reopening is quick and fine; can the pathing avoid walking into the open door in the first place?
- Cause: the route map ignores doors ("doors don't block"), so routes cut across a doorway at an angle and clip an open leaf.
- MHR_Mover.DoorwayLine (every Go, not swimming): DoorwayOnRoute every 0.4 s - doors within 5 m (s_doorCols) whose doorway plane (door.transform = middle of the doorway, forward = through it) the route crosses within the next ~6 m, within 1.3 m of the middle and > 0.5 m before it. Then: on our side and > 0.25 m off the centre line -> steer to the centre line 0.8-1.4 m in front; lined up / in the doorway -> steer to 1.4 m past it. Done once 0.9 m past (or > 5 m away / 2.5 m up or down): PassDoor cleared, route re-planned. Closed-door peek still opens it on the way (rays towards the centre-line target).
- Reset clears PassDoor.

### 1.4.139 - from the 1.4.138 test (log 5976ced3)
- User: handles the door much better, still gets stuck on it (didn't used to).
- Log: no stuck-ladder lines at all. Each pass: "closed the $piece_wooddoor ... open towards us", "couldn't open $piece_wooddoor" (still swinging shut - Door.CanInteract false), "opened" on the next 0.4 s peek, walk through, "closed ... behind us". About 1-1.5 s stood at the door.
- Door peek: after CloseDoor, or an OpenDoor that left it closed, the next peek is 0.15 s away (was 0.4 s).
- If it still looks stuck: ask what exactly it does there (walks into it / waits / turns) - the old DoorHandler didn't close and reopen doors, it just pushed through.

### 1.4.138 - from the 1.4.137 test (log 0eb3e1f9)

Later (Tools) the peek's OpenLeafAhead closed it, reopened from this side and walked through without stopping.

### 1.4.134 - movement phase 4, part 1 (user, 2026-10-09)

### 1.4.129 - piles as a resource, smarter pile spots (user, 2026-10-09)
- User picks: piles refill the chests (Repair and Restock keeps chests at PileKeep both ways). Placement: the row grew into the main path - prefer clear areas; beside a wall / the back of a furnace is fine, by its ports isn't.
- PlanPiles: chest total + carried < keep, nothing of it carried, a chest with room -> PlanTakeDown: pile with the highest base above the ground first (top of a stack), then nearest; removable and ward access. PileDown: approach/reach like repair, hammer, Player.RemovePiece's steps (station in range of where it stands unless NoWorkbench, CanBeRemoved, IRemoved.OnRemoved, WearNTear.Remove or DropResources + Destroy, hammer attack anim; no stamina or wear, as in vanilla). PileCollect: picks up matching ItemDrops within 4 m (CanPickup/RequestOwn/Pickup) and empties a loot box the pile left (m_destroyedLootPrefab), 30 s max; the put-away step returns it to the chest with the most. Hysteresis: refill below keep, stack at keep + one pile.
- PileSpot scores every free spot beside/on top of up to 24 nearest piles of the kind: rejected if a side has something 0.1-1.6 m away at 0.4 or 1.2 m height (narrows a walkway) or the spot is within reach of a used thing (UsePoint: smelter/kiln switches 1.6 m - not the body, so its back is fine; door/portal 2 m; bed, chair, crafting station, cooking station, fermenter, beehive, fireplace 1.4 m; chest 1 m). Score = 2 per backed side (within 0.1 m: wall, pile, anything solid), top of a pile 1.5 (max ~3.2 m above the ground), minus 0.05/m from the nearest pile. Logs how many spots were rejected and why when none is left.

### 1.4.117 - Repair and Restock (user, 2026-10-09)
- User: combine the wheel's Restock and Repair, add chest sorting, use the new movement and do the real actions instead of faking them; rename Repair to "Repair and Restock"; Restock and Homestead slots become "Pending" (no action, no speech) for later; nothing in Homestead worth keeping (the jobs do it better, Wander already moves them around). Sorting: like with like (user's pick). Scope: near home (user's pick) - chests within HomeTripRange of home; no home = no sorting.
- New Jobs/UpkeepController.cs (mode 15). Priority each scan: repair -> fuel -> put back leftovers -> sort -> walk home and idle. All movement through MHR_Nav.Move / MHR_Mover (tag "Upkeep"); chests via MHR_Chests.PickApproach / InReach; pieces and fires via PickApproachPiece.
- Repair = vanilla Player.Repair: piece placed by a player, below RepairBelow, crafting station in range (CraftingStation.HaveBuildStationInRange, checked again from where it stands; NoWorkbench key respected), PrivateArea.CheckAccess; hammer equipped (SuppressAutoEquip), WearNTear.Repair(), attack animation, m_placeEffect, stamina = attackStamina less up to half by skill, durability -= m_useDurabilityDrain x Game.m_durabilityRate. Reach = closest collider point within Reach (4.5 m) of the eyes with a clear line. Hammer = a build tool whose piece table is a Hammer (not hoe/cultivator), durability > 0. ToolKeeper still fetches one (K_HAMMER); away from home a missing hammer no longer makes it stop and follow.
- Restock = vanilla Fireplace.Interact: one fuel item removed from the pack + RPC_AddFuel per WorkTime x 0.6, until full. Fires below FuelBelow (0.8), m_canRefill, not infinite. Fuel from the pack first, else fetched from the nearest chest (exactly what that fire needs). Fetched items are tracked (_carried) and put back when nothing needs them.
- Sort: stackable items (SortGear for the rest) on SortItems; key = name + quality + world level + variant. A kind in 2+ chests goes to the chest holding the most (ties: nearer home), as much as the pack carries; part-stacks merged by taking the smallest out and putting it back (AddItem fills part-stacks first). Chests: MHR_ToolKeeper.UsableChest (now internal) + ward access. Chest opened with SetInUse + interact animation for WorkTime, then closed; only chests it opened are closed.
- Retired: HomesteadController (its item helpers moved to Jobs/MHR_Items.cs for Cook), CompanionAI's old RepairBuildings/Restock modes, the radial's unused DoRestock, [Homestead] settings. GetStayHome() is always false; load clears the StayHome flag and turns mode 16 into 15. Wheel: slots 40/41 "Pending" (three dots), return before the speech line.

### 1.4.106 - Fish redone, one list format (user's answers to the Fish review, 2026-10-09)
- User: (1) Fish stays put and looks for nearby water. (2) Fish for real. (3) Fish list (*, -Name) and a stock target, yes. (4) Special bait needs trophies - no bait crafting for now, backlog. (5) Take the catch home like Hunt. (6) Use the Fishing skill and let it apply. Also: make every list use the same standard - * everything, -Name to leave out, Name:Number for that item's own amount.
- Core/MHR_List.cs: shared parser (cached per raw string): All, Named, Excluded, Amounts, Order; Allows(names...), AmountFor, HasAmount; MHR_List.Local(token). Used by: GatherWood/Stone/Ore (MHR_GatherRules.Parse returns it; amount = item limit, default category limit), ForageItems (Name:N -> stop at N via MHR_GatherRules.Stock, now internal), PlantAny (* = every seed in s_seedToPlant with NeedsCultivated, i.e. not saplings; -Name; Name:N = crop stock target: the vegetable for seed families, the item itself for barley/flax), Cooking.Foods (Name:N = that food's FoodTarget; CookCatalog.Target), Hunting.Prey (creature or meat Name:N = target; HuntCatalog.Target; NeedsDrop for meat and bird drops), Fishing.Fish. WoodSkipTrees and NeverDiscard stay "part of a name" lists (they aren't item lists). MeadFocus keeps its boss table format.
- Vanilla fishing for a companion: FishingFloat.GetOwner looks the caster up in the player list by user id -> for a companion that's the player, so the float looked for _RodTop on the player and destroyed itself (and Setup's FindFloat could kill the player's own float). Patches/MHR_FishingPatches.cs: Setup prefix stores the companion's ZDOID on the float ZDO ("HC_FloatOwner"); GetOwner prefix returns that companion; ReturnBait prefix gives unused bait back to the companion (vanilla only refunds players). Field refs: m_nibbler, m_nibbleTime, m_lineLength, m_nview.
- FishController rewritten: Idle -> Moving -> Casting -> Waiting -> Reeling (+ GoingHome). Anchor = where the job started; targets = Fish.Instances within ScanRadius, not hooked, allowed, under target, with a bait in the pack (best m_chance), and a bank spot (walk out from the fish towards us, then all round, 3-14 m, first dry spot with line of sight to the water over the fish, not >4 m above the water). Move with MHR_Mover (skip the fish 2 min if the route swims / gives up). Cast: rod + chosen bait equipped (EquipItem on the bait so the float gets that bait), LookAtPoint at the fish, draw time set via Humanoid.m_attackDrawTime (percentage from a learned cast scale, updated from where the float lands), StartAttack, draw time back to 0 (IsDrawingBow would kill the float). Waiting: nibble seen (m_nibbler within 0.4 s) -> strike = blocking on (the float's reel); float on dry ground -> reel in; CastWait (60 s) with no bite -> reel in. Reeling: hooked -> reel while the fish isn't escaping and line tension (rod top to float minus line length) <= 2.5 m, else ease off; float gone -> caught if the fish count went up, else lost. Skill: the float raises Fishing while reeling and uses the skill for speed/stamina. Pack full near home -> home + RequestUnload. Counts: fish in pack + chests near home (HomeTripRange) or 25 m round the anchor. Reference: OfflineCompanionsReborn.Fishing.txt.
- Config: [Fishing] ScanRadius (kept), Fish, FishTarget, CastWait, BagFull. Removed WaitTimeMin/Max, ReelTimeMin/Max, HookChance, MissChance, ReelStaminaDrain.
- Untested risks: whether the companion model has the rod's "_RodTop" (if not the float dies at once: "The cast didn't come off"), cast distance scaling, strike timing.

Repair buildings / Restock / DoorHandler: MHR_Nav.MoveWalk -> MHR_Nav.Move (tags RepairBuild, Restock, Door).

- CompanionCombatAI.HuntMove: while HuntMode, the chase moves (target in sight but out of range, and moving to the last known spot) go through MHR_Mover.Go (walk while sneaking). Route would swim / Mover gave up -> "[Hunt] Leaving X - ..." + Disengage (HuntController then skips that prey 60 s). Ranged weapon and the target more than 2.5 m below: EdgeSpot - walk the flat line towards it while the ground stays within 1.5 m of our height and dry, take the first spot that sees it within 27 m; none -> leave it. Non-hunt combat still uses vanilla MoveToPoint (combat review).

### 1.4.99 - "close enough" jumps (user, 2026-10-09)
- User: jumping doesn't need to be perfect; if a jump gets most of the way across and lands in shallow water, it's worth it - they can jump again, like a player. Same for objects.
- TryWaterJump: after the dry-landing case, if water starts within 1.5 m and HasSpare(25% stamina after the jump cost), jump when the spot at Reach (max 6 m) isn't swim depth and isn't higher than +0.5 m / lower than MaxDrop. Jump cooldown 1.5 -> 0.8 s so it can chain jumps. Reach margin 80% -> 90%. HopLine gap margin 0.6 -> 0.25 m; with the companion known, only swim-depth water rules a straight line out (was 0.8 m). Step/log hops (StepAhead) already hop anything 0.35-1.3 m high.

### 1.4.98 - jumps like a player (user, 2026-10-09)
- User: does sprinting into a jump help the NPC like a player? -> Character.Jump keeps the velocity going in (adds up force m_jumpForce x (1 + 0.4 x Jump skill), forward m_jumpForceForward), air control is low (0.1), with no stamina the whole jump is x m_jumpForceTiredFactor (0.7). Same code for companions. Our Leap set a fixed 6.5 m/s after jumping. User: no visible run-up needed (players do instant/short sprint jumps); use its actual speed and Jump skill, and its stamina; anything else that makes it behave like a player is welcome.
- New AI/MHR_Jump.cs: Cost (m_jumpStaminaUsage, SEMan.ModifyJumpStaminaUsage, Game.m_moveStaminaRate - as Player.OnJump), CanAfford (CompanionStamina), RunSpeed (m_runSpeed + SEMan speed mods, or current speed if faster), Reach (projectile with Physics.gravity, landing height, tired factor, 80% margin), Leap (sets run velocity BEFORE Character.Jump so the game's jump carries it). Character.OnJump postfix charges companions the jump stamina (vanilla only charges in Player.OnJump).
- Stream jump (TryWaterJump), GapAhead and HopLine gaps (Direct mode, with the companion) now also need CanAfford and landing within Reach. JumpWaterWidth / JumpGap stay as upper limits.
- Noted for the skills/stats review: Character.GetRunSpeedFactor is 1 for non-players (no Run skill bonus, no armor movement penalty for companions), and jump/run stamina use for companions is our own system.

### 1.4.97 - from the 1.4.96 test (log 55281769)
- User: took a shortcut to dropped items in the water and jumped in, then was sneaking in the water. Stairs: going for honey, jumping across to the hives would be quicker than down the stairs and round (but not if it'd jump there from everywhere). Asked to check the bed fix after sleeping.
- Bed: slept and woke ("Bed collisions ignored/restored"), no kinematic warning anywhere in the log - fixed.
- Water: the Boar died at the water; drops were skipped as deep, but 1.4.96's ragdoll follow moved the kill spot out with the floating body and he went after it. Now Loot goes to DrySpot (last non-swim point between him and the body), stops if the route would swim (MHR_Mover.RouteSwim), leaves single drops reachable only by swimming; Mover's "dive in" leap is skipped for Loot. Skipped drops are a separate set (the "already lying there" count was including them).
- Crouch while swimming: IsCrouching postfix and MHR_SetCrouch refuse while swimming; UpdateStalk off while swimming; CompanionAI drops a crouch when he starts swimming.
- Honey: the cook's honey trip was 21 m / 23 corners for 11.6 m straight (deck at 40.1 down to 38.0) - HopLine refused it, reason unknown. HopLine now allows drops up to General.MaxDrop (3 m) and gaps up to General.JumpGap (1.5 m, landing no higher than +0.3 m, counted as hops); Direct mode leaps a gap right ahead (GapAhead). Direct still only kicks in when the route is much longer than the line (longWay, or 2 m+ longer with hops), so it doesn't jump everywhere. When a long way round is taken instead, the log says why the line was rejected ("taking the Xm way round, not straight: ...").
- Honey eaten: AutoPickup grabbed the honey as he walked up, so it never got into the ledger, and AutoConsume ate it. Ledger now = bag increase since the extract (whatever picked it up); CompanionFood skips food the cook's ledger covers (CookController.IsWorkFood).

### 1.4.94 - Hunt redone (user's answers to the Hunt review, 2026-10-08)
- User: (1) Follow on = hunt targets of opportunity near the player, catch up when nothing's near; Follow off = hunt where they are (likely home). (2) Anything that drops meat, never tamed; a list setting (*, -Name), mods too. (3) ~100 meat, configurable; leather/hide a bonus. (4) Go to the kill and pick up its auto-pickup items - for every kill, not just Hunt. (5) Following: unload when home; near home/outpost: go unload. (6) Bow first, melee second; defend itself and fight what attacks, like a player.
- MHR_JobRules.OnJobSet keeps Follow for mode 7; radial Follow toggle doesn't turn Hunt off; SetActionMode notifies HuntController.
- HuntController rewritten: Idle/Hunting/Returning/GoingHome. Center = player (Follow on, FollowRange 25) or anchor (home if NearHome, else start position; Range 40). Leash = range + ChaseExtra 15. Prey: HuntCatalog (ZNetScene prefabs with CharacterDrop dropping a cooking-station input or *Meat*; not boss/player/companion; IsTamed checked live), [Hunting] Prey list, needs a meat under MeatTarget (pack + chests within HomeTripRange of home, recount every 15 s / after a kill), not in deep water, nearest first. Lost prey skipped 60 s. Pack full (BagFull 0.9 weight or no slots): Follow off near home -> walk home + RequestUnload (60 s retry); Follow on -> keep it (ToolKeeper arrival unload now also for mode 7).
- CompanionCombatAI: HuntMode is now computed (target == _huntPrey), EngagePrey(); retargeting mid-fight only to things after us (hunt too); OnDamaged while hunting switches to the attacker - so blocking/parry/leash apply when defending. Bow first / melee second was already in SelectBestAttack for hunt mode.
- ToolKeeper: mode 7 with no bow/arrows away from home no longer WaitHome (melee works).
- MHR_Loot (new): Character.OnDeath prefix credits m_lastHit's companion; snapshot of items already lying within Radius+2; CompanionAI drives it after combat/ToolKeeper (walk to new auto-pickup drops via MHR_Mover, Humanoid.Pickup within 1.6 m; waits while a Ragdoll is still near the kill). Stops: auto pickup off, pack full, kill > MaxDistance+5 away, player moved > MaxDistance from it while following, Wait+15 s. Hunt also queues its prey's death (no snapshot) in case the player landed the last hit.
- Not done yet: outposts (needs the outpost idea below), combat strategy per enemy (combat review).

### 1.4.92 (cont.) - mead plan reviewed with the user
- Boss order: Eikthyr -> The Elder -> Bonemass -> Moder -> Yagluth -> The Queen -> Fader -> Kall Fimbulbringer. Agreed table (last beaten -> priority bases): start: HealthMinor, StaminaMinor; eikthyr: HealthMinor, StaminaMinor, PoisonResist; gdking: PoisonResist, HealthMedium, StaminaMinor; bonemass: FrostResist, HealthMedium, StaminaMedium; dragon: HealthMedium, StaminaMedium, Tasty, BarleyWineBase; goblinking: HealthMajor, StaminaMedium, EitrMinor, Lightfoot; queen: BarleyWineBase, HealthMajor, StaminaLingering, HealthLingering, EitrMinor; fader and kall: FrostResist, HealthMajor, HealthLingering, StaminaLingering, EitrMinor. Some later base names unverified - unknown names are logged.
- Priority, not exclusive: PickTask = rescue/tap, store >= 10, Fill (priority bases, then any base in bag/chests), Brew(priority), Food, Brew(any other known base), store.
- Stages: MeadFocus entries in the player's order; the last entry whose boss is beaten wins ("start" when none). A label matches a boss by key ("defeated_x"), key suffix, display name or prefab (normalised); bosses come from Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<Character>() with m_boss + m_defeatSetGlobalKey (mods included), listed in OfflineCompanionsReborn.Cooking.txt. Beaten = ZoneSystem global key or the local player's unique key (Character.OnDeath queues m_defeatSetGlobalKey as a player unique key too). Kall's key isn't in the code (boss keys are prefab data) - "kall" matches by name.
- ModConfig: a cfg still holding the 1.4.87 draft MeadFocus string is replaced with the reviewed table.

### 1.4.91 - fix from the 1.4.90 test (log a0174312)
- User: looking better; trouble reaching the fermenter. Log: cooking works (Black Soup, Muckshake, 3x Mead Base: Medium Healing at the Mead Ketill, 3 fermenters filled). The fermenter stand spot was (-273.4, 42.6, -312.9) with him on the floor at 40.4 - the top of the neighbouring fermenter -> stuck ladder until close enough.
- Fermenter never filled (user: "It never succeeded. The fermenter is still empty."): the game's Fermenter registers RPC_AddItem as (int nameHash, bool cheated) and stores s_content as an int hash (GetContent = GetInt). Cook sent the prefab name (RPC ignored, base already consumed from the bag -> 3 Medium Healing bases lost) and read s_content as a string (always "empty"). Now InvokeRPC("RPC_AddItem", name.GetStableHashCode(), false) and GetInt(s_content) != 0. Same in the old cook code; not from the 1.0.17 patch (Fermenter unchanged). All other InvokeRPC calls checked against the game's Register signatures (Smelter RPC_AddOre(string,bool)/RPC_AddFuel/RPC_EmptyProcessed, CookingStation RPC_AddItem(string,bool)/RPC_RemoveDoneItem(Vector3,int)/RPC_AddFuel, Fireplace RPC_AddFuel, Fermenter RPC_Tap) - OK.

PickNear (stations, slots, fermenters) now RaycastAll's down and takes the first walkable non-furniture surface that passes the checks; PickApproach (chests) skips furniture too.

### 1.4.87 - Cook rewrite (agreed design in the BACKLOG entry "Cook redesign")
- `Jobs/CookController.cs` rewritten. Task picker every 4 s (scan of chests, crafting stations, cooking stations, fermenters within Cooking.Range round the companion): 1) Rescue (a cooking station slot done/burnt) 2) Tap a ready fermenter 3) Store if carrying >= 10 things it made 4) Fill an empty fermenter (focus bases + any base in the bag; finished mead < MeadTarget) 5) Brew a focus base (< MeadBaseTarget, its mead < MeadTarget) 6) Best food (value = food + stamina + eitr): cauldron/prep-table recipes the player knows (`Player.IsRecipeKnown`), food (or what the oven makes of it) below FoodTarget, ingredients ok; or a cooking-station conversion (n = min(free slots, target room, usable raw)); finishing a made item gets +0.5 7) Store what it made.
- Reserve: `Usable(item)` = count in range chests + bag, minus IngredientReserve for raw items (not the output of any cooking recipe/conversion).
- Crafting: walk to a stand spot near the station (PickApproachPoint), require roof (Cover.GetCoverForPoint >= 0.7, like CheckUsable) and fire (private m_haveFire) else the station is skipped for 60 s; PokeInUse, "interact" anim, craft effects, 2.5 s, consume, add item (dropped if bag full).
- Cooking stations: fetch raw (+ up to 3 fuel for an oven), RPC_AddFuel / RPC_AddItem per item, then stand by it: every 0.5 s RPC_RemoveDoneItem for done/burnt slots and pick up drops round the spawn point (only made food, meads, coal); leaves when nothing of its item is still cooking (max 240 s). Slot state read from the ZDO ("slot"+i, "slotstatus"+i: 0 cooking, 1 done, 2 burnt).
- Ledger: what it made/picked up (ZDO HC_CookLedger "prefab:n;"), trimmed to what's still in the bag; storing moves exactly that. Store chest: same item > other made food (Cooking.StoreWithCookedFood) > nearest to the last station used.
- `CookCatalog`: built once from ObjectDB recipes, CookingStation and Fermenter prefabs (mods included); writes BepInEx/config/OfflineCompanionsReborn.Cooking.txt (foods with value + station, mead bases -> meads, stages). MeadFocus stage = last `defeated_<boss>` global key; unknown names in MeadFocus are logged.
- Batches (user): Cooking.BatchSize 5 - crafting repeats up to min(batch, target room / recipe amount, usable ingredients / need) times in one trip (2 s each); cooking stations fill min(free slots, batch, room, usable) slots.
- Settings: [Cooking] Range 25, IngredientReserve 20, FoodTarget 20, BatchSize 5, Foods *, StoreWithCookedFood on, MeadBaseTarget 10, MeadTarget 20, MeadFocus (draft table, user to review).
- Moves through MHR_Mover (tag "Cook") with stand spots; gives up on MHR_Mover.GaveUp or 12 s with no progress.

### 1.4.85 - companion route map (user, 1.4.84 test: "3 doors is not enough", why does the count need a number)
- Valheim's Pathfinding builds a navmesh per agent type from colliders on m_layers; closed doors are colliders, so they're walls, and Humanoid has agentClimb 0.3 m so most stairs/steps don't connect. The door BFS (1.4.84) had to test door combinations with route queries, hence the cap.
- `Patches/MHR_CompanionNavMesh.cs`: postfix on Pathfinding.SetupAgents adds AgentType 40 (copy of Humanoid, agentClimb 0.6); prefix on Pathfinding.BuildTile rebuilds tiles of that type exactly like vanilla (walkable sources + water as area 3, Humanoid swims) but drops every build source whose collider has a Door in its parents. Tiles only get built where GetPath pokes them with that type, i.e. around companions. Any failure -> `Ready = false`, back to Humanoid. Config General.CompanionPathing (default on, restart).
- MHR_Mover (Plan, FindRouteStart, ring search, hop bridge) and MHR_Chests.PickApproach / PickApproachPiece use `MHR_CompanionNavMesh.AgentOrHumanoid`. RouteDoor's own queries stay on Humanoid (it reasons about closed doors) - with the new map it should rarely run. Doors on the route are opened by the existing DoorAhead peek and closed behind as before.
- Steps: `StepEdge` (level 0.3 m ahead, 0.35-1.3 m higher at 0.8 m) hops as soon as he reaches a step, on any route.
- Chest stand spots (user: chests are usable from 2 m): PickApproach rings 1.2/1.7/2.2 m, 16 directions (was 1.2/1.7, 8). Farm counts standing on its picked spot (within 0.7 m, line clear, ReachDist <= UseDistance + 1) as in reach (`AtChestSpot`), for the seed and output chests; tool trips already did (atSpot).
- To check in the log: "[Move] Companion route map ready" at world load; no "couldn't set up" / "failed" warnings.

### 1.4.84 - fixes from the 1.4.83 test (log 991999f4): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
User: not emptying bags; should hop logs and the like instead of going round; stairs hop works; farming better; planting starts in the middle instead of from an edge; got stuck, put sword and shield back on, went back to work, then again and ran off. With Ore on and no ore around he just stood there while the player left (see backlog: job + follow).
- Bags: the log starts in Follow (mode 0) at home carrying TinOre, BoneFragments, RawMeat... from the last session; unload only ran on a job change (0 -> 8 later), and UnloadPending isn't saved. MHR_ToolKeeper now also sets UnloadPending on arriving home (or loading in at home) in mode 0 with something to put away (`State.WasAtHome`).
- Farm store: "[Store] Have crop output but no chest with space found" every scan - the 4 nearest of 24 chests are on the ledge at y 42 (unreachable), the search only tried 4, and ScanNearbyChests cleared the unreachable set every scan. Now up to 12 tries and unreachable chests are remembered for 60 s. User: those chests are in the house, up the stairs, and the door was open - so it's the stairs (not in the navmesh, like the front steps). Also wants any number of doors in a row handled. Now:
  - `MHR_Mover.CanGetTo(pos, goal)`: HopLine straight (< 25 m), or `FindHopBridge` (rings 3/6/10/15/20 m x 12 round the goal: ground point B with HopLine(B, goal) and a full route pos -> B, nearest ring wins, max 24 route queries), or a closed-door chain.
  - PickApproach keeps the stand spots that passed every check but the route and accepts the first (of up to 4) that CanGetTo reaches.
  - Mover.Plan: partial route and no door chain -> detour to the hop bridge; from there Direct mode hops the rest.
  - RouteDoor is now a breadth-first search over door sides, up to 3 closed doors in a row, 8 doors nearest the midpoint, 120 route queries max; returns the first door of the shortest chain (the next plan after walking through finds the next one).
  - HopLine samples ground from 1.5 m above the last ground point (not from above everything), so roofs and upper floors don't count as ground.
- Planting: FindPlantPositionInZone picked the free cell nearest the companion, so he stood still at the zone centre planting in a ring until a spot was 2.2 m off through his own saplings -> "Aborted - stuck moving to plant spot" x3 -> RestoreLoadout (sword + shield back on) each time. Now rows: `GetPlantOrder` per zone (kept until the zone is full) - rows run across the side he comes from, row 0 is the far edge, serpentine along each row; blacklisted spots skipped.
- Logs: Mover direct mode also when the route is > straight + 2 m and HopLine has at least one hop (before: only > 1.6 x straight + 6). StepAhead hop now also on a normal route when held up > 0.6 s (anchor not moved), not swimming.

### 1.4.83 - fixes from the 1.4.82 test (log c0ff32c5): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
User: climbed out of the moat (log: "climbing out at ... steepest step 0.4m" then free); ran around the steps in front of the base instead of jumping them and warped while still in view; shot boars while exploring; shield only equipped in combat; tin mining works; SeneaL_UI blocks the rename box.
- Steps: Follow goal ~4 m away and 2.5 m up the steps, route=full but 85-120 m (navmesh doesn't use the steps). Plan now tries `HopLine` when the route is partial or > 1.6 x straight + 6 m and straight < 25 m: half-meter samples, each rise <= 1.1 m, drop <= 2 m, no water > 0.8 m, chest-height (1.4 m) linecast clear, <= 10 hops. Then the path is just the goal (`S.Direct`), and steering jumps (+4 m/s forward) when `StepAhead` sees a 0.35-1.3 m rise or a knee-high block with clear chest height. Stuck while Direct -> no Direct for 20 s and normal route. Log: "going straight to ... (N step(s) to hop) instead of a Xm way round".

- Shield: CompanionSetup.AutoEquipBest had no Shield case, so itemData4 was always null and the shield only came out via the combat weapon logic. Added (best GetBaseBlockPower); the existing code already skips it with a 2H weapon or bow.
- SeneaL_UI rename: not changed. Our name box is a TMP_InputField on a clone of InventoryGui's container; a UI mod that restyles InventoryGui probably covers it or eats the clicks. Fallback is in the BACKLOG (rename fallback + command wheel revisit).

### 1.4.82 - fixes from the 1.4.81 test (log e8ba46da): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
User: chest animation sometimes missing; farm stores after a few crops; planted a few and stopped; Follow ran down to the beach/water again (dry moat = pit with a narrow path); wants the 2.5 m approach hold back; Wood works; fell into the moat and couldn't get out; super recall works when the companion is loaded. Stone and Wood OK.
- Farm storing early: TryNextCropNearby refused while carrying any crop, and UpdateScanning ran TryScanStore first. Now `PackNearlyFull()` (<= 1 empty slot or weight >= Harvest.OverweightThreshold) is the only thing that stops chaining; scan order is store-if-nearly-full, then harvest -> store -> plant (harvest priority) or store -> plant -> harvest (plant priority).

The soft reset now clears GaveUp on a new goal (shift > 1.5 m). Ladder/Passes/MainBest are only reset on a new goal too, not on a pause: the harvest swing pauses reset the ladder, so in the moat he sat on "step 1" and never gave up.
- Follow detour: a 67 m route for ~21 m straight. `MHR_Mover.RouteLength` (remaining path length, 0 when not moving); MHR_Follow switches to the trail when it's > 1.8 x straight + 8 m (and > 3 m from the player).

- Trapped rescue: Plan remembers GoodPos (last spot a full route to the main goal started from, on the ground). On a give-up with the main route partial, >= 2 m below GoodPos, within 40 m and 5 min of it, not swimming or in combat: `CompanionAI.MHR_PlaceAt(GoodPos)` and the move restarts. Log: "trapped at ... moving back to ...".
- Climb out (user: "behave like a player"): Character.GetSlideAngle is 90 for non-players (they never slide) and OnCollisionStay counts normal.y > 0.1 as ground, so jump-climbing steep faces works for NPCs. On the first stuck check with the main route partial and the target (GoodPos, else the goal) > 1.5 m above: `FindClimb` samples ground heights along 16 directions, 1 m steps to 16 m, stops at deep water, a dip > 1.5 m, a non-terrain rise > 1 m (building wall/roof) or a rise > 6 m; picks the smallest steepest-step (ties: shorter), skipping spots already tried. `Climb` faces it, sprints, jumps on the ground when the face ahead rises or forward speed < 1, adds 5 m/s forward on each jump; rests (halts) below 15% stamina until 60% or 5 s; gives up a way after 5 s with no 0.3 m height gain, up to 3 ways per goal; then the normal ladder -> give-up -> trapped rescue. Harvest push is off while climbing (`MHR_Mover.Climbing`). Logs: "climbing out at", "climbed out", "couldn't climb out at".
- Chest animation: Farm and Smelt chest opens and MHR_ToolKeeper chest trips trigger "interact" (Cook/Homestead already did).

Log: "Rock cracked - carrying on with ...". If none: the old destroyed -> drops path.

- Repair: MHR_Nav.RepairThreshold "any wear" only within min(25 m, HomeTripRange) of home (was NearHome = 60 m). RepairController walks to MHR_Chests.PickApproachPiece (spot by the station a route ends at, radii 1.3/1.8/2.2) instead of the station centre (partial route -> swam across instead of the door).
- Follow stutter (Wander off, close): lead = spot + travel x max(1.5 m, speed x 1 s), arrival 0.3 m, ease off a pace when ahead of the spot.

### 1.4.77 - Follow tweaks + phase 3 part 1 (Farm, gathering): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
- User (after 1.4.76): hard to catch a following companion to open its inventory/wheel. Now it holds for 1 s after the player sets off (T.MovingSince) and while the player is within 2.5 m.
- User: tie Follow's moving around to the Wander toggle (radial wheel, per companion, ZDO HC_Wander, default off). User chose: switch it on once for all companions (`HC_WanderFollowMigrated`, in `CompanionSetup.EnsureActionModeMigration`; new companions get it too). Wander on = spots re-picked every 10-25 s + idle shuffling; off = keeps its spot (re-picked only if it becomes invalid) and stands still. Regroup and indoor sitting apply either way. Side effect accepted: Homestead companions with Wander on roam around home (existing Homestead Wander behaviour).
- Phase 3 part 1: `MHR_Nav.Move` (MoveFarm/MovePlant) now calls `MHR_Mover.Go` (Plant keeps its stand-on-the-field rule; the farm checks reach itself before moving). Old jump tracker in MHR_Nav removed. `HarvestController` (Wood/Stone/Ore/Forage) moves to targets and drops through `MHR_Mover.Go(..., "Gather")`; the nav-retry wait and close-range push are gone (they'd fight the Mover). Its 10 s give-up timer counts at 0.3x while `MHR_Mover.Progressing` (routing around something) and gives up at once when `MHR_Mover.GaveUp` (after 0.5 s on a target). Kept: the push when "arrived" on the ground but the target is still out of reach above; the in-attack MoveToPoint re-approach for low targets (short range).
- Still on old movement: MHR_Nav.MoveWalk users (Homestead, Cook, Smelt, Repair-buildings/Restock in CompanionAI) = part 2; Hunt, Fish = part 3. Farm's own 10 s stuck/blacklist and DoorHandler stay until phase 4.

### 1.4.76 - tested by the user (log 0f7e80ce): "everything working as expected"
- Loads clean (58 methods patched). Follow seen in the log: opened wood gates/doors on the way ("no way through - heading for the closed $piece_woodgate" -> opened), one sidestep unstick, sat on $piece_throne01, got up when the player moved, regrouped after 56 s.
- Fixed after the test: 2,779 Unity warnings "Setting linear velocity of a kinematic body is not supported" while seated. Character.UpdateWalking sets m_body.linearVelocity every physics tick even when attached, so a kinematic body warns each tick. The seat is now held without kinematic: gravity off, collisions with the chair piece ignored (like ship seats), position pinned every frame; restored on getting up.
- Probably the same warning flood with ship seats (`CompanionAI.AttachToShip` sets the body kinematic) - pre-existing, not checked; fix the same way when ships come up.

### 1.4.76 - phase 2, Follow on MHR_Mover
Design approved by the user (with: regroup is a toggle + timer; indoors stay closer and sit, emptying bags first near home).
- New `AI/MHR_Follow.cs`. CompanionAI's follow branch calls `MHR_Follow.Update` for Player targets; non-player targets still use `FollowWithFormation`.
- Per followed player (`T`): travel direction from position change (smoothed; not camera facing), speed, still-since, a trail of the last 40 positions (0.75 m apart, cleared on teleport), indoors = local player `InShelter()` (checked every 0.5 s).
- Per companion (`S`): a spot as angle/radius around the travel direction. 15% ahead (20-60 deg), 30% beside (60-100), 55% behind-side (100-170); radius 3 m to 0.7 x MaxDistance outdoors, 1.5 m+ indoors. Re-picked every 10-25 s, re-checked every 1 s. Spot needs ground (normal.y >= 0.6, within 2.5 m of player height, water depth <= 1 m), line of sight from the player's head, and spacing from other companions' spots (3 m, 1.5 m indoors). No valid spot -> trail mode: a point 2 m + 1.5 m x rank back along the player's path; retried every 1-3 s.
- Moving: starts when > 4 m from the spot (2.5 m indoors), > MaxDistance from the player, or swimming. Goal leads the spot by 0.5 s of player travel so it doesn't stop-start. Speed (new `MHR_Mover.Go(..., speed)`): match the player (walk/sneak, jog, run), one step faster when > 3 m off the spot, run when > 8 m off or > MaxDistance + 4. Sneak animation follows the player (`CompanionAI.MHR_SetCrouch`). Mover gave up -> vanilla `Follow` (`MHR_FollowFallback`) until it retries.
- Player still (> 1 s): within range -> stop where it is; idle every 6-14 s (35% turn to look at the player, else walk to a point within 2 m / 1 m indoors of where it stopped). Too far or swimming -> go to a spot within 0.6 x range. Regroup (`Follow/Regroup`, `RegroupTime` 45 s): one move to a spot 2-4 m (1.5-2.5 m indoors) around the player.
- Indoors + still for 5-10 s (random per companion) + `Follow/SitIndoors`: first `MHR_ToolKeeper.RequestUnload` (only with a home set and near it; same rules as after a job change), wait 2.5 s and while a trip or repair runs; then the nearest free `Chair` (not m_inShip, within range+1.5 m of the player, same level, in sight of the player, not in use by a player or claimed by another companion) -> walk there, attach (pinned to m_attachPoint, kinematic, `m_attachAnimation` bool) - weapons stay out like vanilla chairs (HideHandItems would make auto-equip fight it). No chair (or 15 s / gave up -> 20 s cool-down) -> `emote_sit` on the floor facing the player. Companions without player animations don't sit.
- `MHR_Follow.Tick` (from MHR_ToolKeeper.TickOne, owner only) gets them up: player moved > 2.5 m from where they sat down (1 m outdoors), follow target changed, combat, job/Homestead set, ToolKeeper trip, repair, CompanionRest resting/sleep (floor sit keeps the emote for Rest), or the follow branch not running for 1 s. Chair: steps off the front onto the floor (vanilla detach offset if there's no floor).
- `MHR_Mover`: `Go` overload with speed (Auto/Walk/Jog/Run); re-plan within 0.3 s when the goal drifts > 1.5 m from the planned destination (moving goals); tag "Follow" ignores the "no closer for 8 s" stuck rule (keeping pace isn't stuck), still-for-2.5 s still applies.
- `DoorHandler` stays idle while `MHR_Follow.Owns` (follow ran in the last 0.5 s). Old follow water branch no longer used for players.
- Config (new section Follow): MaxDistance 10, IndoorDistance 4, Regroup on, RegroupTime 45, SitIndoors on. AI/FormationOffset and AI/FormationCatchupDist unused for players - remove in the config cleanup.
- Things to watch in testing: stop-start while walking; spots in dense forest/bases (LOS rejects -> trail mode); chairs on raised floors or benches; sitting animation for thrones/benches from mods (unknown animator bool = a Unity warning).

### Release plan (user, 2026-10-04)
- No packaged release until the movement update (phases 2-4) is done. Each step is merged to `main` and tested as a version bump with the user's own build; package and release everything at the end.

### Phase 2 - Follow on MHR_Mover: the user's answers (2026-10-04)
1. Companions move around while following, not locked to one slot. Goal: behave like a real player.
2. Up to ~10 m away (including ahead of the player) is fine.
3. Several companions spread out around the player instead of a line behind.
4. Player stops: companions stay where they are; they may wander a little, and come back to the player if the player stays still for an extended period.
5. Only combat should behave differently. Combat itself (not always running off to engage, player-like strategies) is a later polish pass - not part of phase 2.

### 1.4.75 - code (branch `claude/relaxed-archimedes-ecoh6h`)
What was built (all items below):
- (1) `CompanionAI` (end of the follow/idle branch): job mode on (`MHR_JobRules.IsJobMode`: not Follow 0, not legacy Stay 4) and no job driving -> `StopMoving()` instead of `EnforceHomePatrol`/`IdleMovement`.
- (2) `FarmController.TryNextCropNearby()` after drops are collected: if nothing needs storing (`HasCropOutput()` false), take the nearest ripe crop within `NextCropRange` (Farm, default 3 m, 0 = off) straight to MovingToPickable. Otherwise the old 2 s idle + full scan (so store trips are unchanged).
- (3a) `MHR_Mover.Plan`: GetPath fails outright -> `FindRouteStart` tries 8 directions at 1.5 m and 2.5 m (clear at knee height, footing, not deep water) for a spot a route starts from; steps there as a 3 s detour. Throttled to every 2 s. (3b) `MHR_Mover.Tick`: a door we opened isn't closed while the mover is active and stuck (`Ladder > 0` or a detour) within 8 m of it.
- (4) `FindRipePickable` logs one summary line per scan (counts of picked/claimed, not crops, unreachable) instead of per crop. The per-crop `cultivated=` detail is gone; add it back temporarily if the auto-farm-zone investigation needs it.
- (5) `[BepInIncompatibility]` removed. `CompanionsPlugin.Awake` checks `Chainloader.PluginInfos` for `com.profmags.companions` (loads first: GUIDs load alphabetically) and logs an error and doesn't start. `Start()` re-checks after all plugins loaded and shuts down (unpatch, destroy ConfigPanel/FarmZonePlacer) if needed.
- (6) CHANGELOG 1.4.53-1.4.74 rewritten one line per item; 1.4.75 entry added. (7) README: Note 2, Recent Highlights v1.4.75 (show the user before packaging), install note about removing the original, Source section.
- Also in 1.4.75: the Harmony attribute fix from the C# conversion. `CombatPatches.EquipBestWeapon_PreferHighestDamage.Postfix` only acts outside Balanced/Ranged/Melee (`WeaponLogic.IsManagedStance`), i.e. Passive: swaps to the highest effective-damage weapon. In 1.4.74 it never ran and `Prefix` ran a second time as a postfix.

### 1.4.75 - original proposal (from the 1.4.74 farm/repair log)
1. **Idle wander during jobs:** after each crop the farm job paused 2 s with nothing driving him, so the vanilla idle wander walked him back to his idle point (~4 m behind the farm edge, around (-275,-326)), then he walked back in for the next crop: walk in, pick, walk back, every crop. Follow was off. Fix: while a job is on, a short pause means standing still, not idle wander. No job on = wander as before.
2. **Harvest like a player working a row:** after picking a crop, go straight to the next ripe one within a few meters, no pause and no full rescan. The full scan runs only when nothing ripe is close.
3. **Doorways (repair trip, ~45 s lost):** he opened the door, stood in the doorway and couldn't get a route from there; the door got closed behind him and reopened while he was still trying. Fix: (a) if no route can start from where he stands (e.g. a doorway), step 1-2 m to find one right away instead of running the whole unstuck ladder; (b) don't close a door he opened while he's still stuck near it.
4. **Farm scan log:** one summary line per scan instead of one line per crop (60-80 crops, ~460 Debug lines per scan).
5. **Original-mod check:** remove `[BepInIncompatibility("com.profmags.companions")]` (Hexium lists it as an incompatibility tag). Replace with a startup check: if ProfMags' original is loaded, log a clear error saying to remove it (it doesn't work on Valheim 1.0) and don't start Reborn. Same note in the README and the Nexus description.
6. **Changelog:** rewrite 1.4.53-1.4.74 in the one-line style (see the CHANGELOG style rule below).
7. **README:** add the user's "Note 2" next to the existing note (text below).
- Not in 1.4.75: moving Follow onto MHR_Mover is phase 2. Order after that: Follow (ask the user for their follow ideas first) -> jobs move over -> remove the old per-job stuck code.

### Packaging (agreed with the user)
- Only `Companions.dll` is needed to run. Mod managers need `manifest.json`, `README.md`, `icon.png` (256x256) in the zip root; `CHANGELOG.md` is optional (shows as a Changelog tab). Hexium: packaged the same way as a mod manager. Nexus: DLL only, the description lives on the page.
- `LICENSE` goes in every package, Nexus included (MIT: ProfMags' copyright notice must ship with copies).
- The source now lives in this GitHub repo. The user doesn't mind including source in packages but most players won't care. The repo is **private**, so don't point the README or Nexus at it unless the user makes it public.

## Reference: game mechanics (not plans - things checked in the game code to remember)
- **Game 1.0.21 (patch 2026-10-29, user's Managed folder):** the mod compiles against it with no errors or warnings; every Harmony target and reflected member still exists; ZInput (assembly_utils) and Localization (assembly_guiutils) unchanged. Changes in assembly_valheim: Version 1.0.20 -> 1.0.21; PlayerProfile.GetStat(stat, DifficultyRequirement) (not used by the mod); Player soft-death timer sync; ItemStand/ArmorStand clear m_equipped on items put on them; WearNTear support check rewritten (caches the supporting pieces); Ship sail swaps only with a player at the controls; CookingStation remembers who put each item in (crafter on the cooked item); InventoryGui drag-release outside; Character slippery-mask bit fix; motion controls off by default; small null checks (Chat, Feast, GrapplingPoint, BlockCheckHandler). Reference DLLs in the scratchpad game/Managed are now 1.0.21 (1.0.20 kept in game_1.0.20); decompiled 1.0.21 source in dec_new.
- **Keeping an area loaded with no player near (checked 2026-10-17, game 1.0.20):** three things make the host treat an area as if a player stood there: (1) keep the zones generated - ZoneSystem.PokeLocalZone(zone) for each 64x64 m zone, every ~0.1 s (from a ZoneSystem.Update postfix); (2) create its objects - ZNetScene.CreateDestroyObjects only builds objects near the reference position, so add the kept zones' objects (ZDOMan.FindObjects(zone, list, visited), skipping Player ZDOs and zones whose neighbours aren't loaded yet) to its near list (m_tempCurrentObjects) before CreateObjects/RemoveObjects; (3) own them - objects only run on their owner, and ZDOMan.ReleaseNearbyZDOS drops ownership when no peer is near, so after it (postfix, uid == own session) set the host as owner of the kept zones' persistent ZDOs that have no owner or whose owner isn't in that peer's active area (ZDOMan.IsInPeerActiveArea). Runs on the host / dedicated server, which needs the mod. Cost: roughly another player standing there per area. Sensible limits: a few areas, a modest radius (zones add up fast), optionally only while some player is within a few hundred meters; companions there think less often and stay inside the kept radius. Compare with the catch-up idea on the backlog (apply the work at once on return - cheap, but approximated).

- **Defensive stance (user, 2026-10-23):** "defensive fight" as a stance of its own on the wheel (stay by you, fight what comes, don't advance), not just the dungeon default (Combat > DungeonDefensive, 1.5.65). Do it with the action wheel overhaul.

Ties in with the multi-companion management panel. Opening the F8 settings from a controller (user, 1.5.72: wait): a Settings slice on the wheel is one way; a button in the game's pause menu was offered too (user not sure about touching the start menu). Start + Select together doesn't work well - the game opens the menu/map on the first press; block + Start / Select / D-pad are the game's own (HUD, chat, zoom). Until then: Controller > Settings bind (none by default).
- **Multi-companion management panel (user, 2026-10-17: "will probably also need to happen sooner rather than later"):** one place to see and run all your companions. Outline: a roster list - name, where they are, what they're doing (job/order), health and stamina, area loaded or not (MHR_Roster snapshots already save this every 60 s); per-companion quick orders (follow, stay, gather, come here) and a button to open its normal panel; map pins on the large map for each companion that open its panel when clicked. Overlaps with server-side support and other players controlling companions (user's own plans) - the panel should show who owns a companion and who may command it.
- **Portals and ore (user, 2026-10-17: "This is something we should fix. I would just add it to the backlog for now."):** a player can't use a portal carrying anything Inventory.IsTeleportable rejects (ore, metal; TeleportWorld.Teleport -> Player.IsTeleportable(m_allowAllItems), unless the portal allows all items or GlobalKeys.TeleportAll is set). Our PortalPatches brings following companions along whatever they carry. Fix: check each queued companion's inventory with the same rule (the portal's m_allowAllItems + TeleportAll); one that fails stays at the portal and says why ("I can't take the ore through the portal - I'll wait here"), and waits there (it can't walk home and unload: its area unloads once the player has gone, see the reference note on keeping areas loaded). Recall / super recall from the other side should also respect it.
- **In-game test run (user, 2026-10-17: "I do like the idea of an NPC test that would help catch regression"):** a set of checks that runs on its own in a dedicated test world (or from a console command) and writes PASS/FAIL lines to the log - e.g. spawn a companion, follow up stairs and a ladder chain, open a door, avoid a fire, cross shallow water, equip/unequip, eat, fight a spawned skeleton, put items in a chest, save and reload with gear intact. Lets a build be checked without replaying every case by hand. Pairs with a console command that builds the test course in front of the player.
- **Catch-up for work done while you were away (user, 2026-10-17: "an interesting option ... We will build our own way of doing it"):** jobs only run where the game has the area loaded. Idea: remember when the companion's area was last loaded (a time stamp on its ZDO); when the player comes back, work out what it would have done in that time (capped, e.g. an hour or two) and apply it for real at once - fell trees (leave the stumps), break rocks, pick bushes, put the drops in its bag/home chests until they're full, wear its tools, raise its skills - then report it ("While you were away I felled 12 trees and filled the wood chest"). Counts against the gather limits like normal work. Alternative already on this list: keep a working companion's area loaded (real but costly). Decide between them, or both, when we get to it.
- **Dodge roll (user, 2026-10-17: "I would like to later add it for use in cases like this"):** roll instead of blocking when blocking is the wrong answer - an attack that can't be blocked (item m_blockable false), a wide sweep or area attack (Attack.AttackType.Area, m_attackAngle > 150), a projectile, nothing to block with, too little stamina to block but enough to roll, or a hit stronger than its block power. How (checked in the game, 1.5.52 notes): the companion has the player animator - rotate to the roll direction, ZSyncAnimation trigger "dodge", the player prefab's m_dodgeStaminaUsage and m_dodgeEffects, ZDOVars.s_dodgeinv + a Character.IsDodgeInvincible postfix for the invulnerable window (~0.4 s), Character.InDodge for companions while the dodge anim tag is on, Dodge skill gain; safe ground ~3 m out (SafeStep), sideways first. Not now (user: not needed for the current combat work).
- **Sneak attacks (2026-10-17):** the game's backstab (x4, Character.RPC_Damage) only lands on a creature that hasn't noticed anyone, once per 5 min. A companion could sneak (crouch, like Hunt's stalk) up on an unaware enemy and open with it. Ask the user.
- **Combat world modifier damage (2026-10-16):** companions count as enemies for the Combat modifier's damage rates (see the 1.5.53 world-modifier notes) - take EnemyDamage x PlayerDamage, deal EnemyDamage x PlayerDamage. Decide with the user whether they should take and deal damage like the player.
- **Extended combos (user, 2026-10-16, unconfirmed reports):** "After the normal 3-hit chain, you can hold the normal attack to add extra swings. The last two hits use the 3rd-attack damage multiplier"; "Extended power combo: hold both normal and secondary attack during the extended combo - the last three hits use the secondary attack's multiplier, up to ~300% of base damage at max skill". Checked in code (game 1.0.20): Attack.Start sets the level from the previous attack's m_nextAttackChainLevel, which wraps to 0 after the last level - so held attacks loop 1-2-3-1-2-3, and only the last level gets m_lastChainDamageMultiplier (x2); nothing in Attack/Humanoid/Player gives extra swings a multiplier. If it exists it's in the animator (not in the decompiled code). Test in game with a player first before teaching companions.
- **Low priority - suggestions passed on by the user (2026-10-13):**
  - **Cart transport and supply runs** (https://youtu.be/8yyDwhM9SGcI): companions push a cart from A to B - ores, wood, food between bases or chests, while the player is AFK. Set a destination or a simple repeating supply route. Builds on what's there: the "attach to cart" order (CompanionInteract.DirectCart), chest handling (MHR_Chests/ToolKeeper), MHR_Mover routes. Needs: cart-aware movement (turning radius, slopes, doors too narrow for a cart), loading/unloading, a route set-up UI.
  - **Siege weapons** (https://youtu.be/lWtUEL3gxw8): companions help push a trebuchet/battering ram into place, load ammo and fire at fortress walls (Ashlands). Squad-command feel.
